### [1] [Magnus mountains on spinning neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12378)
*Yashaswi Gangwar,David Ian Jones*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了中子星弹性壳层中被钉扎的超流涡旋产生的非轴对称马格努斯力如何形成"马格努斯山"，该形变可产生连续引力波。通过可压缩双组分恒星模型求解流体与弹性组分耦合运动方程，在无限长圆柱近似下发现质量四极矩可高达~10^-5（受涡旋钉扎强度和壳层破裂应变限制），对当前及未来引力波探测器具有探测前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究中子星表面不均匀性（"山"）对连续引力波源的影响，当前对中子星弹性形变产生的引力波源研究多集中于热应力或磁场机制，而马格努斯力导致的山形变尚未被系统研究，因此需要定量评估其引力波辐射潜力。

Method: 采用可压缩双组分恒星模型（流体+弹性组分），构建无限长圆柱近似（含薄海洋和壳层），自洽求解流体与弹性组分的耦合运动方程，从而确定恒星形变和质量四极矩。

Result: 电流四极矩为零；质量四极矩（无量纲）可达~10^-5量级，其上限受限于涡旋钉扎的有限强度和弹性壳层的破裂应变；该形变对当前及未来连续引力波探测具有前景。

Conclusion: 马格努斯山可作为可观测的连续引力波源，为后续更真实的中子星模型研究提供了动机和基础。

Abstract: We investigate the formation of a "Magnus mountain'' on a neutron star, arising from the non-axisymmetric Magnus force acting on the elastic crust by pinned superfluid vortices. Such a deformed star would act as a source of continuous gravitational waves. We consider a compressible two-component stellar model and solve the coupled equations of motion for the fluid and elastic components self-consistently, allowing the stellar deformation and the resulting mass quadrupole to be determined. For simplicity, we model the star as an infinitely long cylinder, consisting of a fluid region with a thin ocean and crust. We find that the current quadrupole is zero, while the Magnus forces are strong enough to produce (dimensionless) mass quadrupoles as large as $\sim 10^{-5}$, and so would be limited only by the finite strength of the vortex pinning and the breaking strain of the star's elastic crust. Such large deformations are promising from the point of view of the detection of continuous gravitational waves by current and future detectors, and motivate further work on more realistic stellar models.

</details>


### [2] [Synchrotron and Inverse-Compton Signatures of Black to White Hole Explosions: Weibel-Mediated External Shocks in the Interstellar Medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12380)
*Hoang Van Quyet*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了黑洞-白洞转变过程中接近各向同性的相对论性喷出物扫过星际介质时产生的非热电磁对应体（外部激波余辉），基于光球模型参数化动能，在Weibel磁场框架下以单区Thomson近似计算了同步辐射和同步自康普顿辐射，发现其持续时间比经典长暴余辉快数个量级，并提出了区分该场景与短暴、磁星巨耀发等瞬变源的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究黑洞-白洞跃迁这一超出标准天体物理的极端过程是否可产生可观测的电磁信号，并为其提供与已知瞬变源（短伽马暴、磁星巨耀发等）相区分的观测判据。

Method: 沿用以Villani2026光球模型为基础的辐射框架，参数化外部激波可用动能Eiso=R_k*E_gamma,prompt；用Weibel介导磁场模型描述无碰撞正向激波；在单区、Thomson区近似下计算电子同步辐射与SSC辐射，纳入逆Compton冷却、Klein-Nishina抑制、自吸收和γγ不透明度，采用量级或一阶精度处理。

Result: 对示例参数（E_gamma~1e44 erg, R_k=0.1, Γ0=100），电子处于慢冷却状态，p=2.5<3时同步辐射谱在冷却频率νc处达到峰值（MeV-GeV范围且随时间软化）；余辉持续时间为零点几秒到几十秒，比同等条件下经典长暴余辉快数个量级；识别出1 keV光子数估计超过名义探测阈值的参数区间；给出了快速、微弱、近各向同性、无消色差喷流拐折等区分特征。

Conclusion: 黑洞-白洞转变产生的余辉是一种快速、微弱、近乎各向同性的瞬变信号，紧随硬谱瞬时闪光之后，无消色差喷流拐折，可在参数空间上与短GRB、磁星巨耀发及其他快速瞬变源区分；但定量结论受限于仅考虑正向激波、单区Thomson辐射近似及示意性探测判据等假设。

Abstract: We study the non-thermal electromagnetic counterpart expected when the relativistic, near-isotropic ejecta of a black-to-white hole transition sweep up the interstellar medium (ISM). Building on a photospheric model for the prompt $γ$-ray emission~\cite{Villani2026}, we parametrize the kinetic energy available for an external shock, $\eiso=R_k\egprompt$, and model the resulting collisionless forward shock with a Weibel-mediated field~\cite{Weibel1959,MedvedevLoeb1999,SironiSpitkovsky2011}. We estimate the synchrotron and synchrotron self-Compton (SSC) emission from shock-accelerated electrons in a one-zone, Thomson-regime approximation, including inverse-Compton cooling, Klein-Nishina suppression, self-absorption, and $γ$-$γ$ opacity, all at order-of-magnitude or first-order accuracy. For illustrative parameters ($\egprompt\sim10^{44}$ erg, $R_k=0.1$, $Γ_0=100$), the electrons are slow-cooling, so for $p=2.5<3$ the synchrotron spectrum peaks at the cooling frequency $ν_c$, in the MeV-to-GeV range and softening with time; the afterglow evolves in a fraction of a second to tens of seconds -- orders of magnitude faster than a classical long-duration GRB afterglow at comparable energy, density, and Lorentz factor -- with duration, peak energy, and flux depending sensitively on $R_k$, $\nism$, and $\epse,\epsB,\xie$. We identify parameter regions where a 1 keV photon-count estimate, below the intrinsic peak, exceeds a nominal detection threshold, and discuss features -- a fast, faint, roughly isotropic transient after a hard-spectrum prompt flash, without an achromatic jet break -- that may distinguish this scenario from short GRBs, magnetar giant flares, and other fast transients, while flagging the assumptions (forward-shock-only dynamics, one-zone Thomson-regime radiation, an illustrative detectability criterion) limiting its quantitative reach.

</details>


### [3] [Microscopic Interaction versus Purely Gravitational Coupling in Strange Quark Stars Admixed with Dark Matter: A One-Fluid and Two-Fluid Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12383)
*J. Sedaghat,G. H. Bordbar,M. Haghighat,S. M. Zebarjad*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 研究了混合标量暗物质的奇异夸克星，对比微观相互作用与纯引力耦合两种模型，发现非相互作用双流体模型能更稳健地满足当前及未来引力波探测器的潮汐形变约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索暗物质对奇异夸克星结构和可观测量（如潮汐形变）的影响，并比较微观相互作用与纯引力耦合两种建模方式在解释现有观测数据（如脉动星质量和GW170817潮汐形变限制）方面的优劣。

Method: 构建两种理论模型：(1) 相互作用单流体模型，包含微观相互作用；(2) 非相互作用双流体模型，仅通过引力耦合。通过求解TOV方程得到质量-半径关系并计算潮汐形变参数Lambda，与脉冲星观测及GW170817数据进行比较。

Result: 相互作用单流体模型能够产生M_TOV > 2太阳质量的可行构型，满足现有脉冲星质量和半径测量以及GW170817的潮汐形变约束；而非相互作用双流体模型不仅能满足GW170817的现有限制，还能满足下一代引力波探测器预期的更严格潮汐形变上限。

Conclusion: 非相互作用双流体模型（纯引力耦合）在满足未来更严格观测约束方面具有更强的鲁棒性，为暗物质-夸克物质混合系统的建模提供了更可靠的理论框架。

Abstract: In this study, we investigate strange quark stars (SQSs) admixed with scalar dark matter (DM), focusing on the role of microscopic interactions versus purely gravitational coupling. Our results demonstrate that while the interacting one-fluid model yields viable configurations with M_TOV > 2M_sun that satisfy current pulsar M-R measurements alongside the tidal deformability (Lambda) constraints from GW170817, the noninteracting two-fluid model more robustly meets both existing GW170817 limits and the tighter Lambda bounds anticipated from next-generation gravitational-wave detectors.

</details>


### [4] [Interacting Supernovae: a Radio and X-ray Strategy to Constrain the Structure of the Circumstellar Medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12464)
*Shunke Ai,Irene Tamborra,Leonardo Dinoi*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了超新星抛射物与非球形环绕星介质（CSM）相互作用所产生的X射线和射电辐射特征，发现非球形CSM会导致射电光变曲线在峰值附近变平、上升段更浅等现象，并基于这些特征提出了利用射电光变曲线峰值附近上升部分来约束CSM结构的方法。将该方法应用于SN 1993j和SN 2023ixf，发现非对称CSM与观测高度吻合，为理解超新星前身星的质量损失历史提供了重要线索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超新星抛射物与致密CSM相互作用可将激波动能转化为多波段辐射，但以往研究多假设CSM为球形结构，现实中CSM几何形状可能更复杂（如沙漏型、盘状等）。理解CSM的几何结构对揭示超新星前身星的质量损失历史至关重要，因此需要系统研究CSM几何形状如何影响可观测的X射线和射电信号。

Method: 通过对比球形、沙漏型和盘状三种CSM几何结构，系统计算了超新星抛射物与非球形CSM相互作用产生的X射线和射电辐射光谱及光变曲线特性。分析了自由-自由吸收对不同方向CSM密度的依赖，并基于射电光变曲线峰值附近的上升和衰减特征建立了约束CSM结构（几何形状及密度轮廓是否为风状）的诊断方法，同时利用X射线信号进行交叉验证。

Result: 发现非球形CSM下射电光变曲线在峰值频率附近因未激波CSM的有效自由-自由吸收而变平；当视线方向CSM密度较大时射电光变曲线上升段更浅，密度较小时射电光变曲线在峰值附近变平且X射线中反向激波成分可忽略。对SN 1993j和SN 2023ixf的应用表明，非对称CSM与射电和X射线观测高度吻合，为非风状场景提供了可行的替代方案。

Conclusion: X射线和射电信号对CSM的几何结构高度敏感，可据此有效约束超新星前身星的质量损失历史。所提出的基于射电光变曲线峰值附近形态的方法能够区分球形与非球形CSM，并判断CSM密度轮廓是否为风状，为超新星环境研究提供了新的诊断工具。

Abstract: The interaction of supernova (SN) ejecta with the dense circumstellar medium (CSM) converts shock kinetic energy into radiation across multiple wavebands. We investigate the dependence of the X-ray and radio emission on the CSM geometry, considering spherical, hourglass, and disk shapes for the CSM. We find that the spectral and light-curve properties, both in X-ray and radio, significantly differ for spherical and non-spherical CSM structures. For a non-spherical CSM, the radio light curve flattens out near the peak frequency, due to efficient free-free absorption by the unshocked CSM. Moreover, the early rise of the radio light curve is shallower when the CSM density along the observer line of sight is larger than that in other directions. If the CSM density is lower along the observer line of sight, the radio light curve flattens near its peak, and the reverse-shock component is negligible in X-rays. Building on these features, we provide a method to constrain the CSM structure based on the rising part the radio light curve in the proximity of its peak; we show that the decay part of the radio light curve, after its peak, carries insight on whether the CSM density profile is wind-like or not. We further adopt the X-ray signal to corroborate the information extracted from radio. We test our strategy on SN 1993j and SN 2023ixf. For both SNe, we find that an asymmetric CSM is in excellent agreement with radio and X-ray observations and provides a viable alternative to non-wind scenarios suggested in the literature. Our findings highlight the crucial insight provided by radio and X-ray signals into the mass-loss history of the SN progenitor.

</details>


### [5] [3D Radiative Transfer of Lyman-series Lines with SKIRT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12527)
*N. Sameshima,A. Lauwers,B. Vander Meulen,M. Tsujimoto,L. -Y. Gu,M. Baes,P. Camps*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文扩展了三维蒙特卡罗辐射传输代码SKIRT，实现了H类离子Lyman系谱线（n≤10，Z=1–30）的共振散射、辐射复合和偏振建模，使其能够自洽地模拟X射线光电离等离子体中的重处理发射。模拟结果与解析预期及Cloudy吻合，并能捕捉传统一维RT代码无法获得的RT效应（如P Cygni轮廓、光学厚介质中的线轮廓畸变），为XRISM和IXPE时代的X射线光谱与偏振诊断提供了有力工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XRISM和IXPE提供的高分辨率X射线光谱与偏振测量，为探测致密天体周围光电离等离子体的几何与运动学提供了新诊断手段，但解释这些重处理X射线发射需要包含光子—离子相互作用的全三维辐射传输（3D RT）模拟，而现有工具在此方面存在不足。

Method: 在MC RT代码SKIRT中实现H类离子（Z=1–30）的Lyman系跃迁（至n=10），包含精细结构劈裂和共振散射的线性偏振；考虑共振散射和辐射复合两个Lyman系谱线产生通道，并将实现结果与解析预期及Cloudy进行对比验证。

Result: 实现与解析预期一致且与Cloudy吻合良好；SKIRT模拟能自然捕捉P Cygni轮廓和光学厚介质中的线轮廓畸变等辐射传输效应（传统一维RT代码无法获得）；在三维几何中，各向异性照射和速度场显著改变Lyman系谱线比和轮廓，这些均可被XRISM观测到。

Conclusion: 扩展版SKIRT为解释光电离等离子体的X射线线光谱与偏振提供了强大框架，特别适合在XRISM和IXPE时代约束致密天体周围的几何与速度结构。

Abstract: Context. High-resolution X-ray spectroscopy and polarimetry provided by XRISM and IXPE offer new diagnostics of the geometry and kinematics of photo-ionised plasmas around compact objects. Interpreting reprocessed X-ray emission in such systems requires full three-dimensional radiative transfer (3D RT) including photon-ion interactions.
  Aims. We extend the Monte Carlo (MC) RT code SKIRT by implementing the Lyman-series lines of H-like ions, enabling self-consistent modelling of resonance scattering, radiative recombination, and polarisation of these lines in X-ray photo-ionised plasmas.
  Methods. We implemented Lyman-series transitions (up to $n=10$) for ions with $Z=$1--30, including fine-structure splitting and linear polarisation in resonance scattering. Two channels for the production of the Lyman-series lines (resonance scattering and radiative recombination) are considered.
  Results. The implementation reproduces analytical expectations and shows good agreement with Cloudy. The SKIRT simulations naturally capture RT effects such as P Cygni profiles and line-profile distortion in optically thick media, which are inaccessible to the conventional 1D RT codes commonly used in X-rays. In 3D geometries, we find that anisotropic illumination and velocity fields significantly modify the Lyman series line ratios and profiles, all of which are observable with XRISM.
  Conclusions. The extended version of SKIRT provides a powerful framework for interpreting X-ray line spectra and polarisation from photo-ionised plasmas. It is particularly suited for constraining the geometry and velocity structure in the vicinity of compact objects in the XRISM and IXPE era.

</details>


### [6] [How Neutron Star Radii Encode the Dense-Matter Equation of State and Hadron-Quark Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12632)
*Bao-An Li,Xavier Grundler*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过贝叶斯框架和元模型EOS，研究未来高精度中子星半径测量R_{1.4}如何编码致密物质状态方程（EOS）中的微观信息，尤其是强子-夸克一级相变及其质量-半径拓扑结构。研究表明不同EOS参数对R_{1.4}的编码强度存在清晰的层级关系，但精确半径测量本身无法独立识别拓扑或确定高密度相变性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着未来高精度中子星半径测量能力的提升，需要系统理解这些测量能够携带哪些关于致密物质EOS的微观信息，特别是强子-夸克相变参数，并评估不同参数的可约束性及其与质量-半径拓扑结构的关联。

Method: 采用贝叶斯框架，使用具有九个微观参数的元模型EOS；对典型中子星半径R_{1.4}=11.9±σ_R km（σ_R=0.9和0.1 km）进行模拟测量分析；引入逆EOS-半径映射，给出每个EOS参数后验均值作为R_{1.4}的函数；通过斜率衡量半径敏感度，通过曲率经Jensen展开确定后验均值的精度依赖性；将映射分解为四种质量-半径拓扑（Connected, Disconnected, Both, No-Quark-Matter）进行分析。

Result: 对称能参数L（斜率）和K_sym（曲率）被R_{1.4}强编码，其后验均值随半径精度提升显著变化；高阶强子参数表现出更强的拓扑依赖性；相变参数中，转变密度ρ_t被R_{1.4}编码最强，而能量密度跃变和夸克物质声速更与完整质量-半径序列的拓扑相关；由于不同拓扑的R_{1.4}分布高度重叠，即使精确半径测量也无法单独识别拓扑或唯一确定高密度转变性质。

Conclusion: 研究提供了参数依赖的信息层级，用于评估未来高精度半径测量的科学回报及高密度互补探测手段的价值；明确指出了半径测量的能力边界——单一R_{1.4}测量难以辨别质量-半径拓扑，需结合其他互补探针。

Abstract: We investigate how future high-precision neutron star (NS) radius measurements encode microscopic information about the dense-matter equation of state (EOS), focusing on a possible first-order hadron--quark phase transition and the resulting mass--radius topology. Within a Bayesian framework using meta-model EOSs with nine microscopic parameters, we analyze mock radius measurements $R_{1.4}=11.9\pmσ_R$ km with $σ_R=0.9$ and $0.1$ km for canonical NSs. We introduce inverse EOS--radius mappings that give the posterior mean of each EOS parameter as a function of $R_{1.4}$. Their slope measures radius sensitivity, while their curvature determines the leading precision dependence of the posterior mean through the Jensen expansion. Resolving the mappings into four mass--radius topologies, Connected, Disconnected, Both, and No-Quark-Matter, reveals a clear hierarchy of information. The symmetry-energy parameters $L$ (slope) and $K_{\rm sym}$ (curvature) are strongly encoded in $R_{1.4}$ and their posterior means shift appreciably with improved radius precision, whereas the higher-order hadronic parameters show stronger topology dependence. Among the transition parameters, the transition density $ρ_t$ is the most strongly encoded in $R_{1.4}$, while the energy-density jump and quark-matter sound speed are more strongly associated with the topology of the full mass--radius sequence. Since the different topologies have strongly overlapping $R_{1.4}$ distributions, even precise radius measurements cannot by themselves identify the topology or uniquely determine the high-density transition properties. These results provide a parameter-dependent hierarchy for assessing the scientific return of future high-precision radius measurements and complementary probes of high-density

</details>


### [7] [An Evolving Leptonic Jet Model for Delayed Radio Flares in Neutrino Blazars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12696)
*Alina Kochocki,Xavier Rodrigues,Nathan Whitehorn*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过考虑物理延展喷流的时空演化，建立了TXS 0506+056的多频时域模型，成功解释了初始高能耀发与三年后射电耀发之间的因果关系，表明秒差距尺度喷流边缘的冷却电子群是射电增强的起源。


<details>

Abstract: NGC 1275 is the central galaxy in the Perseus Cluster. Overall, our results indicate that coordinated, moderate-resolution X-ray imaging and radio monitoring could potentially trace disk-jet connections in the AGN that most vividly impact large-scale structure, and be extended to other sources that impact their hosts.

Motivation: 经典新星在爆发后会产生射电辐射，但此前研究大多依赖多频数据来区分不同的发射机制。本研究旨在利用ASKAP VAST巡天的单频（887.5 MHz）射电光变曲线，结合物理驱动模型，检验能否仅凭单频数据对主导发射机制进行有效约束，进而加深对经典新星星风、激波加速粒子及同步辐射过程的理解。

Method: 从VAST巡天中筛选2021年9月至2025年11月期间在1200平方度银河系巡天覆盖范围内的43颗光学经典新星，进行射电辐射交叉匹配。对探测到的三颗新星，分别采用热自由-自由发射模型（约束喷射质量和喷射速度）和非热同步辐射模型（约束质量损失率、爆炸能量、风速和填充因子），使用马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）方法进行拟合。同时使用分段幂律描述非均匀环双星物质的径向密度结构，替代标准的均匀风密度剖面。

Result: 三颗新星（V6598 Sgr、V1716 Sco和V1723 Sco）均显示非热同步辐射是主导发射机制。分段幂律描述的环双星物质径向密度结构比标准风密度剖面拟合更好。早期强同步辐射是激波驱动粒子加速的有力证据，并与三颗新星可能存在的伽马射线探测一致。这表明单频射电光变曲线结合物理驱动模型能够有效约束主导发射机制。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，即使仅使用单频射电光变曲线，结合适当的物理模型仍能有效区分热与非热发射机制。三颗新星的非热同步辐射主导表明激波加速在经典新星爆发中普遍存在，且与伽马射线发射相关。分段幂律密度剖面为环双星物质结构提供了更好的描述，为未来经典新星射电观测研究提供了新方法。

Abstract: We present a search for radio emission from classical novae at 887.5 MHz using data from the Australian SKA Pathfinder Variable And Slow Transient (VAST) survey. We cross-matched 43 optically discovered classical novae that erupted between 2021 September and 2025 November within the 1200-square-degree Galactic survey footprint, and found three which show significant radio emission: V6598 Sgr, V1716 Sco, and V1723 Sco. To analyse their radio light curves, we use both thermal free-free and non-thermal synchrotron emission models. We fit the data using the Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) method to constrain parameters, including the ejected mass and ejecta velocities for the thermal models, and mass-loss rate, explosion energy, wind velocity, and filling factor for the non-thermal model. All three novae show evidence of non-thermal synchrotron emission as the dominant emission mechanism at this frequency. We use a broken power law to describe the radial density structure of a non-uniform circumbinary material, which provides a better fit than a standard wind density profile. This strong early-time synchrotron emission is strong evidence of shock-driven particle acceleration, which may be related to detections of gamma-rays from all three novae as well. In contrast to earlier studies that used multi-frequency data to distinguish between emission models, our analysis is based on single-frequency radio light curves, which can still provide useful constraints on the dominant emission mechanism when interpreted with physically motivated models

</details>

### [14] [Dynamical uncertainty geometry for nonlinear transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12521)

TL;DR: 本文提出动力不确定性几何（DUG）框架，将后验不确定性、未来观测和模型诱导输运三种经常被混淆的因素分离，通过共同封闭概率坐标索引后验可信集和输运条件子集，利用持久模块的稳定性定理和两个基准问题展示该框架在动力系统中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动力系统中的不确定性往往由后验几何单独无法显现的输运结构组织，传统方法仅在后验分布的单一阈值下分析，无法在不同可信度水平间追踪物理意义明确的类别，且后验不确定性、未来观测和模型诱导输运经常被混为一谈。

Result: 在短弧轨道确定问题中，DUG在连通不确定区域内识别出动态不同的回归类，并揭示了基于信息与基于局部Fisher的观测设计标准之间的差异；在地月平面圆型限制性三体问题中，DUG揭示了连通可信区域内共存的多个首次撞击输运结果，并提供评估其数值分辨率的诊断工具。

Conclusion: 将后验几何的拓扑摘要与输运标签及未来实验耦合，能够提供比单独的后验概率或动力分类更丰富的不确定性描述。稳定性结果保证了数值近似和加权网格上持久性计算的可量化控制。

Abstract: Uncertainty in nonlinear dynamical systems is often organized by transport structures that are not apparent from posterior geometry alone. We introduce Dynamical Uncertainty Geometry (DUG), a framework that separates three ingredients that are frequently conflated: posterior uncertainty, future observations, and model-induced transport. DUG indexes posterior credible sets and transport-conditioned subsets by a common enclosed-probability coordinate, allowing physically meaningful classes to be tracked across credibility levels rather than examined at a single threshold. The framework combines a mass-ranked credible filtration, a description of future experiments through their induced probability laws and Fisher geometry, and a labelled transport skeleton representing dynamically distinct outcomes. We establish stability results for the resulting mass-indexed persistence modules under simultaneous perturbations of posterior density and probability measure, including continuum, refinement, and finite atomic formulations. These results provide quantitative control of persistence computed from numerical approximations and weighted grids. Two benchmark problems illustrate the framework. In a short-arc orbit-determination setting, DUG identifies dynamically distinct return classes within a connected uncertainty region and highlights a difference between information-based and local Fisher-based observation-design criteria. In the Earth-Moon planar circular restricted three-body problem, DUG reveals multiple first-hit transport outcomes coexisting within a connected credible region and provides diagnostics for assessing their numerical resolution. Together, these examples show how topological summaries of posterior geometry, when coupled to transport labels and future experiments, yield a richer description of uncertainty than either posterior probabilities or dynamical classifications alone.

</details>

Abstract: We construct rank-zero irregular vertex operators for the Neveu--Schwarz algebra as linear maps between irregular Verma modules of the same rank satisfying the usual superconformal commutation relations and an irregular asymptotic condition. Under the nondegeneracy assumption $Λ_{2p}\neq0$, we prove their existence and uniqueness. We then extend the decomposition theorem for the Neveu--Schwarz algebra to the irregular setting: the tensor product of the free-fermion Fock module with a rank-$p$ Neveu--Schwarz irregular Verma module decomposes into an infinite direct sum of tensor products of two Virasoro irregular Verma modules. We also prove a compatible decomposition of the irregular vertex operators into tensor products of Virasoro irregular vertex operators. Using suitable pairings and mode insertions, we derive bilinear differential equations for weighted sums of products of Virasoro irregular conformal blocks of types $(0,0,1)$ and $(0,2)$. Abstract: For every Lie--Rinehart algebra, we construct an $F$-manifold algebra on the

  associated symmetric Poisson algebra are Poisson ideals, and relate the
  Leibnizator to the Lie--Rinehart differential. For a finite projective module
  of constant rank, the trace of the Leibnizator recovers the anchor and yields a
  rigidity result for injective anchors. We conclude with algebraic and
  geometric examples.

Abstract: We construct a finite discrete realization of the Smorodinsky--Winternitz I superintegrable system on a triangular region of the two-dimensional square lattice. We show that the resulting model is maximally superintegrable and that its symmetry algebra admits a Hahn-algebra presentation. Its spectral problem is solved exactly in terms of the bivariate dual Hahn polynomials of Tratnik type. Finally, we show that the continuum limit recovers the continuous Smorodinsky--Winternitz I system together with its ladder operators, eigenfunctions and symmetry algebra, thereby establishing the present construction as a genuine finite discrete realization of the continuous model.

Result: 推导出了消除顶点热回流的扩散系数求和规则，并通过Crank-Nicolson有限差分法数值验证了该解析模型，确认了热在网络中平滑、无阻碍的传播。该模型为热扩散控制提供了实用的数学框架。

Conclusion: 该工作将透明边界条件理论与度量图上的PDE理论相结合，提出了一种新颖的数学模型，可作为网络中热扩散的控制工具。它为低维结构中热扩散的可调谐控制和优化提供了实用框架，具有潜在的应用价值。

Abstract: We study transparent boundary conditions (TBCs) for the time-dependent heat equation in a branching, quasi-one-dimensional domain modeled as a metric star graph. By combining the classical concept of TBCs with the theory of partial differential equations (PDEs) on networks, we derive an exact vertex condition that ensures unobstructed thermal flow across junctions. Specifically,we derive a sum rule for the diffusion coefficients that eliminates thermal backflow at the vertex.We demonstrate the validity of this analytical model numerically using a Crank-Nicolson finite-difference method, which confirms smooth, unobstructed heat propagation through the network.These results provide a practical mathematical framework for tunable control and optimization of thermal diffusion in low-dimensional structures. In particular, combining the well-known concept of the TBCs and theory for PDE on metric graphs, we propose a mathematical model providing a control tool for thermal diffusion in networks.

</details>

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对Maxwell–Cattaneo–Vernotte（MCV）方程，在存在热传递边界条件的条件下，建立了非自伴算子的伴随特征值问题，通过双正交系统获得解析无穷级数解，并系统研究了谱结构与参数影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越傅里叶热传导模型（如MCV方程）在实际中常受边界热传递条件约束，但该条件使微分算子变为非自伴，导致标准谱方法失效。已有研究多集中于理想化边界条件，缺乏对非自伴情形下解析解的完备刻画，需要建立可用的解析框架作为数值验证基准。

Result: 获得了MCV方程在热传递边界条件下的无穷级数解析解，分析了三种类型特征值根（实、虚、复共轭）及其渐近分布；与有限差分数值解吻合良好，揭示了谱截断（Gibbs现象）与数值耗散对热波前沿的影响；数值验证了特征函数集的完备性，并解析推导了闪光脉冲初始条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为非线性/非自伴双曲热传导提供了解析基准，阐明了边界热传递与弛豫时间如何控制傅里叶类扩散与热波传播之间的转变，为实验与实际应用中通过热传递研究超越傅里叶热传导现象开辟了途径。

Abstract: The most well-known beyond-Fourier heat conduction model, the Maxwell--Cattaneo--Vernotte equation is solved analytically in the presence of heat transfer boundary condition. In contrast to the corresponding Fourier problem, this boundary condition renders the underlying differential operator non-selfadjoint. With a suitable scalar product, the adjoint eigenvalue problem is established. The resulting left and right eigenfunctions constitute a biorthogonal system, allowing the expansion coefficients to be determined from arbitrary square-integrable initial conditions. This enables a convenient infinite-sum analytical solution, which is presented and thoroughly investigated for various values of the model parameters (including near-Fourier and highly hyperbolic regimes) and for two practically important initial conditions (equilibrium and flash pulse initiated). The spectral structure is analyzed in detail, including the occurrence of real, imaginary, and complex-conjugate eigenvalue roots, their asymptotic distribution, and their dependence on the dimensionless relaxation time and Biot number. We find good agreement with corresponding finite-difference numerical solutions. The comparison also illustrates the effects of spectral truncation (i.e., the Gibbs phenomenon) and numerical dissipation near propagating thermal-wave fronts. Completeness of the eigenfunction set is numerically demonstrated. The initial condition induced by the flash pulse is derived analytically. The results provide an analytical benchmark for non-selfadjoint hyperbolic heat conduction, unveil how boundary heat transfer and relaxation time influence the transition between Fourier-like diffusion and thermal-wave propagation, and open the possibility to find and investigate beyond-Fourier heat conduction via heat transfer in experiments and practical applications.

</details>


### [22] [On Toeplitz determinants with slow Fourier decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13182)
*Nedialko Bradinoff,Maurice Duits*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究傅里叶系数满足 f_k=O(|k|^{-1}) 的符号对应的 Toeplitz 行列式 det T_n(e^f)，该范畴超越 H^{1/2} 并涵盖 Fisher-Hartwig 奇异性。通过基于 Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff 公式的算子方法，将 log det T_n(e^{tf}) 展开中的二次项与高阶项分离，证明二次项控制可能的增长，而每个固定高阶系数保持有界。对具有有界正负傅里叶部分的符号得到去除二次贡献后的行列式双侧界，对更广的 Fisher-Hartwig 类获得一致高阶系数界及 CUE 线性统计的中心极限定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的 Toeplitz 行列式渐近理论（如强 Szegő 极限定理）通常要求符号属于 H^{1/2} 空间。然而许多实际问题中的符号仅满足 f_k=O(|k|^{-1})，且可能具有 Fisher-Hartwig 奇异性，超出了 H^{1/2} 的适用范围。因此需要发展新的算子理论方法来处理这一更宽泛的符号类，并理解其行列式的渐近行为。

Method: 基于 Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff (BCH) 公式的算子理论方法。将 log det T_n(e^{tf}) 的展开分解为二次项 sum_{k=1}^n min(k,n) f_k f_{-k} 与高阶项之和，利用 BCH 公式系统处理算子指数的对易子结构，从而分别控制二次项的增长效应和高阶项的有界性。分析过程中处理了傅里叶系数慢衰减带来的技术困难。

Result: (1) 证明二次项 sum_{k=1}^n min(k,n) f_k f_{-k} 完全刻画可能的增长，所有固定阶高阶系数有界；(2) 对有界正负傅里叶部分的符号，获得去除二次贡献后行列式的双侧估计；(3) 对包含 Fisher-Hartwig 型符号的更广可容许类，获得一致高阶系数界；(4) 建立 CUE 线性统计的中心极限定理，并得到超越特征多项式的 CUE 随机场混合指数矩估计。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Toeplitz 行列式分析推广到 Fourier 系数 O(|k|^{-1}) 的广泛符号类，给出了高阶系数的有界性和二次项的主导作用，并由此推导出 CUE 线性统计的中心极限定理及随机场的矩界。这为理解 Fisher-Hartwig 型符号下 Toeplitz 行列式的精细结构提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We study Toeplitz determinants $\det T_n(e^f)$ for $f$ whose Fourier coefficients satisfy $f_k=O(|k|^{-1})$. This regime extends beyond $H^{1/2}$ and includes symbols with Fisher-Hartwig singularities. We develop an operator-theoretic approach based on the Baker-Campbell-Hausdorff formula that separates the quadratic term \[ \sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\min(k,n)f_kf_{-k} \] from the higher-order terms in the expansion of $\log\det T_n(e^{tf})$. We show that this quadratic term accounts for the possible growth with $n$, while every fixed higher-order coefficient remains bounded. For symbols with bounded positive and negative Fourier parts, our estimates yield two-sided bounds for the determinant after removal of the quadratic contribution. For a broader admissible class, including Fisher-Hartwig-type symbols, we obtain uniform higher-order coefficient bounds and a central limit theorem for the associated CUE linear statistics. We also obtain bounds on mixed exponential moments for CUE-derived random fields beyond the characteristic polynomial.

</details>

Method: 采用辩论形式，两位支持者分别代表proper condensate和Onsager-Penrose判据进行对比阐述；通过序言引出两种判据，尾声对底层resolvent代数中在此情境下具有物理意义的性质进行评论。

Result: 文章以对比分析的方式呈现了两种BEC判据的异同，并通过resolvent代数的性质为这种对比提供了数学物理层面的支撑。

Conclusion: 两种BEC判据之间存在本质性概念差异，理解底层数学结构（即resolvent代数）对于正确评价这些判据的物理适用性具有重要意义。

Abstract: This article contrasts the concepts of a proper condensate and the Onsager-Penrose criterion for Bose-Einstein condensation in the form of a debate between two proponents of the respective concepts. The prologue briefly introduces the two criteria. The epilogue contains remarks on properties of the underlying resolvent algebra that are physically relevant in this context.

</details>


### [24] [Hidden Dyson Universality in Inverse-Spectral Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13475)
*Momo Hayashi,Kazumitsu Sakai*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过逆谱重建将能谱转化为变形谐振子的矩阵表示，发现Dyson普适性在重建算符的矩阵几何中存活。利用距离壳矩作为诊断工具，证明黎曼ζ函数非平凡零点对应的重建算符具有GUE特征，且该特征并非直接来自输入能级统计，而是来自重建算符的几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dyson普适性通常体现在局部本征值统计中，作者试图探究这种普适性是否能在非线性逆谱重建过程后存活下来，并出现在重建算符的矩阵几何中。同时，希望通过一种不依赖直接能级统计的新方法，来验证黎曼ζ函数零点的GUE特征。

Method: 1. 利用dressing变换将每个展开的频谱映射为固定谐振子的形变f(x)，并在公共谐振子基中表示为F_mn=<m|f|n>。2. 将矩阵元权重按固定距离d=|m-n|的壳层分解，对应参考谐振子的能量转移通道。3. 用距离壳矩表征所得分布，先在高斯β系综（GOE, GUE, GSE）上独立校准，这些矩随β平滑变化。4. 将校准后的诊断应用于黎曼ζ函数的非平凡零点。

Result: 距离壳矩可区分GOE、GUE和GSE三个普适类。将该诊断应用于黎曼ζ函数非平凡零点时，重建算符被定位在GUE扇区，恢复了对零点的GUE特征判断。

Conclusion: GUE特征可以从重建算符的距离分辨几何中恢复，而非直接从输入能级统计中获得。这表明Dyson普适性在逆谱重建后依然存活，且为验证黎曼ζ函数零点的随机矩阵性质提供了一种新的独立证据。

Abstract: Dyson universality typically manifests itself in local eigenvalue statistics. Here we show that its signature survives a nonlinear inverse-spectral reconstruction and reappears in the matrix geometry of the reconstructed operator. Using a dressing transformation, we map each unfolded spectrum to a deformation $f(x)$ of a fixed harmonic oscillator and represent it in the common oscillator basis by $F_{mn}=\bra m|f|n\ket$. We resolve the matrix-element weight into shells of fixed distance $d=|m-n|$, corresponding to the energy-transfer channels of the reference oscillator, and characterize the resulting distribution by distance-shell moments. Independently calibrated on Gaussian $β$-ensembles, these moments vary smoothly with $β$ and distinguish the GOE, GUE, and GSE. With this calibration fixed, applying the same diagnostic to the nontrivial zeros of the Riemann zeta function places the reconstructed operators in the GUE sector. Thus, the GUE character of the zeros is recovered not through direct statistics of the input levels, but from the distance-resolved geometry of the reconstructed operator.

</details>

*Ernesto Lupercio,Mikhail Shkolnikov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了自组织临界性原型模型在最大密度区间的仿射对称涌现机制。当底层网格间距的平方倒数远大于随机扰动点的数量时，toppling函数（里程表）的适当标度极限是Monge-Ampère方程在凸区域上带Dirichlet边界条件的唯一凹解，从而可以精确估计任意宏观窗口内密度偏离最大值的程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自组织临界性模型中密度接近最大值时的精细行为，特别是通过toppling函数的标度极限来刻画密度偏差，并揭示仿射对称涌现的深层机制。

Method: 作者通过数值事实和归纳论证（近期已被升级为严格证明）建立机制：将toppling函数的标度极限与最优输运和微分几何中著名的非线性偏微分方程——Monge-Ampère方程联系起来，在凸区域上施加Dirichlet边界条件，以给定概率测度作为无穷扰动态势函数。

Result: 证明了在最大密度区间，toppling函数的标度极限是Monge-Ampère方程的唯一凹解；仿射对称涌现的机制被确立；利用该结果可以在任意宏观窗口内准确估计密度偏离最大值的偏差。

Conclusion: 自组织临界性模型在最大密度区间的仿射对称涌现可由Monge-Ampère方程的解精确描述，该发现连接了自组织临界性、最优输运和微分几何三个领域，为密度偏差的精确估计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We describe a mechanism of affine symmetry emergence in the maximal density regime of the prototypical model of self-organized criticality when the inverse square of the mesh of the underlying lattice is much larger than the number of random perturbation points distributed according to a prescribed probability measure supported in the interior of the ambient convex domain. Moreover, an appropriate scaling limit of the toppling function (aka odometer), which counts the number of operations per site, is a solution to a non-linear partial differential equation well known in the context of optimal transport and differential geometry, making it possible to accurately estimate the deviation of the density from its maximal value in any macroscopic window. The mechanism for the affine symmetry emergence is due to the novel empirical fact, supported in addition by inductive arguments that have recently being upgraded to a rigorous proof, that the scaling limit of the toppling function is the unique concave solution of the Monge-Ampère equation with Dirichlet boundary condition on the convex domain with the potential given by the probability measure used above as the infinite-perturbation profile.

</details>

### [26] [RoboSynChallenge: Mastering Real-World Dexterity via Generalizing Synthesized Manipulation Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12416)
*Runyi Zhao,Ruixin Wu,Chengkun Li,Hongrui Zhang,Ang Li,Ruixing Jin,Yueci Deng,Yingying Guo,Lihe Ding,Shaocong Dong,Tianfan Xue,Yanjun Gao,Yudong Luo,Pascal Poupart,Simo Wu,Kui Jia,Wei-shi Zheng,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboSynChallenge是一个统一基准测试平台，通过大规模合成数据生成与标准化真实世界机器人评估相结合的方式，解决机器人操作中真实数据稀缺和多样性不足的问题，旨在评估和推进操作策略在多种任务、环境和难度下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能中的机器人操作普遍缺乏泛化能力，其核心瓶颈在于真实世界数据的稀缺性和多样性不足。尽管模型架构和学习算法不断进步，但数据层面的限制严重制约了策略的通用性与适应性。

Method: RoboSynChallenge竞赛提出统一基准测试框架，整合两条关键路径：(1)大规模合成数据生成，鼓励参与者利用合成的状态-动作轨迹样本训练通用操作策略；(2)标准化真实世界机器人评估，最终考核仅在未见过的真实操作环境中进行。同时提供基于Transformer、扩散模型、视觉-语言-动作模型以及World-Action-Model的多种基线实现以保证可复现性与可比性。

Result: 通过将可扩展的模拟训练与严格的真实世界验证相结合，RoboSynChallenge提供了一个统一的基准平台来系统衡量不同操作策略的泛化能力，并推动了数据高效、适应性强的机器人操作系统的开发。

Conclusion: RoboSynChallenge通过"合成训练+真实评测"的范式有效缓解了真实数据稀缺问题，为构建通用机器人智能提供了可靠的技术路径和评估标准，为开发更具广泛能力、数据高效和适应性的操作系统奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: Achieving generalizable robotic manipulation remains a central challenge in embodied intelligence. Despite rapid advances in model architectures and learning algorithms, progress is often limited by the scarcity and narrow diversity of real-world data. The RoboSynChallenge competition introduces a unified benchmark to evaluate and advance the generalizability of manipulation policies across a spectrum of tasks, environments, and difficulty levels. To alleviate the shortage of realistic data, the challenge integrates large-scale synthetic data generation with standardized real-world robotic evaluation. Participants are encouraged to leverage synthesized state-action trials to improve general-purpose policy learning, while final assessments are conducted exclusively on unseen real-world manipulation environments. Baseline implementations, including Transformer-, Diffusion-, Vision-Language-Action, and World-Action-Model-based policies, are provided to ensure reproducibility and comparability. By coupling scalable simulation-based training with rigorous real-world validation, RoboSynChallenge aims to foster the development of broadly capable, data-efficient, and adaptable manipulation systems, thereby paving the way toward truly general robotic intelligence.

</details>


### [27] [Attune: A Self-Annotation Tool for Understanding Robot Operator Attention Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12650)
*Puqi Zhou,Sungsoo Ray Hong,David Porfirio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Attune的部署前引导工具，利用操作员的眼球凝视作为机器人行为设计线索，帮助多机器人监督界面的设计者理解并校准机器人行为，以捕获、维持或释放操作员的注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂真实环境中部署机器人车队需要操作员同时监督多个机器人，管理操作员注意力是多机器人监督界面设计的基本挑战。然而，设计者缺乏关于如何改变机器人行为以捕获、维持或释放操作员注意力的实证指导。

Method: 将操作员的眼球凝视视为机器人行为设计的线索，创建了部署前引导工具Attune。该工具自动识别有意义的凝视转移，提供AI辅助标注凝视转移原因，并输出操作员凝视模式摘要供其审查。通过用户研究进行评估，让参与者标注吸引其注意力的视觉触发因素。

Result: 研究结果揭示了凝视模式的个体差异，并展示了Attune如何帮助表征操作员的注意力特征。

Conclusion: Attune为多机器人监督界面的设计者提供了一种基于操作员注视数据来校准机器人行为以适应不同操作员注意力特征的方法，填补了机器人行为设计层面的实证指导空白。

Abstract: Deploying robot fleets in complex, real-world environments requires human operators to supervise multiple robots simultaneously. Managing operator attention is a fundamental challenge of designing multi-robot supervision interfaces, encompassing both feed layout and feed content (i.e., robot behavior design). Thus far, designers lack empirical guidance on the latter-how to change a robot's behavior to capture, sustain, or relinquish operator attention during multi-robot supervision. In our vision of the future, designers should be able to use this guidance to calibrate robot behavior to different operator attention profiles. Treating operator eye gaze as a robot behavior design clue, we created a pre-deployment elicitation tool called Attune. Attune automatically identifies when meaningful gaze shifts occur, provides AI assistance for annotating why shifts occurred, and outputs a summary of operator gaze patterns for operator review. We evaluated Attune through a user study in which participants annotated the visual triggers that drew their attention. Our findings unveil variation in observed gaze patterns and reveal how Attune helps characterize operator attention.

</details>


### [28] [FUSE: Active Functional Affordance Grounding through Adaptive Semantic-Geometric Evidence Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12683)
*Zhou Chen,Sathyanarayanan N. Aakur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出主动功能可供性接地（Active Functional Affordance Grounding）任务，并设计FUSE框架，通过结合显式不确定性驱动的探索与学习的摊销规划器，使具身智能体能主动选择信息量最大的视点，从而在功能线索被遮挡或不完整时仍能识别并空间定位满足功能查询的对象。实验在Habitat基准上验证了FUSE在非神谕接地性能上达到最优，且计算开销比纯显式探索降低1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体常需基于对象功能而非身份进行识别和交互，但现有可供性接地方法仅从固定视点操作，缺乏在功能线索被遮挡或不完整时决定"看向哪里"的主动探索机制。

Method: 提出FUSE框架，将显式不确定性驱动的探索与学习的摊销规划器相结合，自适应地采集语义-几何证据，高效选择信息量最大的视点进行顺序场景探索；同时构建基于Habitat的基准用于评估主动功能接地。

Result: FUSE在非神谕接地性能上达到最高的观察到结果，计算开销较完全显式探索降低1.33倍，并在多种可供性知识源下保持有效性。

Conclusion: 融合显式探索与学习型规划的混合策略能有效平衡性能与计算效率，为主动功能可供性接地任务提供了可行且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Embodied agents must often identify and interact with objects based on their function rather than their identity, requiring them to actively acquire observations that reveal discriminative functional evidence. Existing affordance grounding methods operate from fixed viewpoints and lack mechanisms for deciding where to look when functional cues are occluded or incomplete. We introduce Active Functional Affordance Grounding, a new task in which an agent sequentially explores a scene to identify and spatially ground an object satisfying a functional query. To address this problem, we propose FUSE, an adaptive semantic-geometric evidence acquisition framework that combines explicit uncertainty-driven exploration with a learned amortized planner to efficiently select informative viewpoints. We further introduce a Habitat-based benchmark for evaluating active functional grounding. Experiments show that FUSE achieves the highest observed non-oracle grounding performance while reducing computation by 1.33x relative to fully explicit exploration, and remains effective across multiple affordance knowledge sources.

</details>


### [29] [SAP-Nav: Spatial Semantic Representation Meets Active Perception for Hierarchical Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12707)
*Xuetong Pei,Jian Liu,Vidura Munasinghe,Bo Miao,U-Xuan Tan,Wenrui Ding,Na Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SAP-Nav是一个全在线、零样本的主动感知框架，用于解决层次化开放词汇物体导航（OVON）问题。它通过增量构建可查询的空间语义表示和主动视角验证机制，在部分观测条件下有效完成从场景、房间、区域到实例级别的空间目标定位与目标验证。在LangMap和HM3D-OVON基准上取得最佳性能，区域级导航SR较基于训练的方法提升12.2%，并在真实机器人实验中验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层次化开放词汇物体导航需要代理在未知环境中根据自由形式指令（可能包含场景、房间、区域、实例级别的提示）定位目标。虽然已有工作LangMap形式化了这一设定，但在部分观测条件下可靠解决该问题仍具挑战：空间锚定需要持久的场景级证据，而目标验证需要清晰且具有区分度的候选视角。

Method: SAP-Nav通过两方面的主动感知机制解决上述挑战：(1) 从主动获取的房间视图中增量构建可查询的空间语义表示（Queryable Spatial-Semantic Representation），使代理能从任何已探索位置进行空间语义查询；(2) 采用主动视角验证（Active Viewpoint Verification）判断当前观测是否提供足够证据，必要时将代理重新定位到信息更丰富的视角，再依据类别和属性约束验证候选目标。

Result: 在LangMap和HM3D-OVON基准上取得整体最佳性能，其中区域级导航的成功率（SR）相比基于训练的方法提升12.2%。真实机器人实验进一步验证了方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: SAP-Nav证明了通过主动感知机制（增量空间语义表示构建和主动视角验证），可以在不依赖任务特定训练或预计算场景地图的情况下，同时支持层次化和标准类别级OVON任务，并取得优于训练方法的性能。

Abstract: Hierarchical open-vocabulary object navigation (OVON) requires agents to follow free-form instructions that may specify targets through scene-, room-, region-, and instance-level cues in unseen environments. Although recent work LangMap has formalized this setting, reliably solving it under partial observations remains challenging: spatial grounding requires persistent environment-level evidence, whereas target verification requires clear and discriminative candidate views. We present SAP-Nav, a fully online, zero-shot framework that addresses both requirements through active perception. SAP-Nav incrementally constructs a Queryable Spatial-Semantic Representation from actively acquired room views, enabling spatial semantic queries from any explored location. It further employs Active Viewpoint Verification to assess whether the current observation provides sufficient evidence and, when necessary, reposition the agent to a more informative viewpoint before verifying candidates against category and attribute constraints. Although designed for hierarchical OVON, SAP-Nav supports both hierarchical and standard category-level OVON without task-specific training or precomputed scene maps. Experiments on LangMap and HM3D-OVON show that SAP-Nav achieves the overall best performance, including a 12.2% improvement in SR over training-based methods on region-level navigation. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate its practical feasibility. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [30] [Genetic Fuzzy System-Based Multi-Robot Coordination for Planetary Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12755)
*Daegyun Choi,Donghoon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于遗传模糊系统的多机器人系统（MRS）去中心化方法，用于在非结构化环境中协同执行物体搬运任务，同时最小化系统总路径长度并避开障碍物。方法通过高程图的坡度可通行性分析生成二维可通行性地图，并利用遗传算法在多种场景下优化模糊推理系统（FIS）以生成MRS速度，最终在测试环境中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统在非结构化环境中执行协同物体搬运任务时，面临路径长度优化与避障的双重挑战。现有方法往往难以在复杂地形（如陡坡、局部极小值、障碍物邻近目标等）下兼顾全局路径最优性与实时决策能力，亟需一种能够自适应学习并泛化到多种场景的去中心化控制策略。

Method: 首先，针对给定的高程图进行基于坡度的地形可通行性分析，降低问题维度并将不可通行区域视为障碍物，将地图转换为二维可通行性地图。其次，设计了用于生成MRS搬运物体至目标位置速度的模糊推理系统（FISs），并通过遗传算法在多种训练场景（包括局部极小值、目标靠近障碍物、杂乱环境）下对FIS参数进行优化。训练完成后，将FIS模型应用于测试环境（即转换后的可通行性地图），并在多个场景下进行验证。

Result: 论文在多种训练与测试场景下验证了所提出的遗传模糊系统方法。结果表明，训练后的FIS模型能够有效地在非结构化环境中引导多机器人系统协同搬运物体，实现障碍物规避并最小化系统总路径长度。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了遗传模糊系统在去中心化多机器人协同搬运任务中的有效性，通过离线训练与在线应用的范式，实现了在未知非结构化环境中兼顾避障与路径优化的控制策略。该方法为复杂地形条件下多机器人协作提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a decentralized approach for a multi-robot system (MRS) using a genetic fuzzy system to perform a collaborative object transportation task that minimizes the total path length of the MRS in unstructured environment while avoiding obstacles. For an environment given by an elevation map, terrain traversability analysis with respect to the slope is performed to reduce the dimension and identify non-traversable areas that can be considered as obstacles, and the given map is converted into a traversability map in two dimensional space. In the training process, proposed fuzzy inference systems (FISs) to generate the MRS's velocity for transporting an object to a target position are optimized by a genetic algorithm with several scenarios, such as a local minima, a target that is close to an obstacle, and a cluttered environment. The trained FIS models are applied to the testing environment, which is the converted traversability map, and validated using multiple scenarios.

</details>


### [31] [AirForesight: Current-to-Future Spatial Map Imagination with Cross-Space Planning Consistency for UAV-VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12835)
*Yutong Liu,Xiaojie Li,Mingzhu Xu,Jianlong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AirForesight框架，一种面向无人机视觉-语言导航的"当前到未来"空间地图想象方法。通过结构化当前地图表征学习，结合未来轨迹预测和跨空间规划一致性损失，实现从稀疏多视角观测中进行显式场景感知和未来感知推理，在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S数据集上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机视觉-语言导航方法大多将视觉-语言输入直接映射为动作，缺乏显式的场景锚定和未来感知的空间推理能力，难以应对复杂户外环境下稀疏多视角观测带来的空间理解挑战。

Method: 1) 从多视角观测学习结构化当前地图表征，由当前地图重建和未来轨迹预测联合监督；2) 通过结构化因果注意力将当前空间知识传播到未来地图推理；3) 聚合当前与未来表征预测下一个3D航点；4) 引入跨空间规划一致性损失，促使预测的地图空间轨迹与基于真实航点位移导出的专家动作方向保持一致。

Result: 在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S数据集上，AirForesight相比现有方法取得显著性能提升，广泛的消融实验验证了各模块的有效性和框架的稳定性。

Conclusion: AirForesight通过显式场景锚定和未来感知推理，为无人机视觉-语言导航提供了一种有效的空间想象框架，证明了"当前到未来"空间推理对复杂户外导航任务的重要价值。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Vision-Language Navigation (UAV-VLN) requires agents to follow language instructions, infer spatial structure from sparse multi-view observations, and execute feasible 3D motion in complex outdoor environments. Despite recent progress with large language models, most existing methods still map vision-language inputs directly to actions, providing limited explicit scene grounding and future-aware spatial reasoning. We propose AirForesight, a current-to-future spatial map imagination framework for UAV-VLN. AirForesight first learns a structured current-map representation from multi-view observations. This representation is jointly supervised by current-map reconstruction and future-trajectory prediction, encouraging it to encode both present scene structure and future motion intent. Under structured causal attention, the current spatial knowledge is propagated to future-map reasoning, and the resulting current and future representations are aggregated to predict the next 3D waypoint. To make spatial imagination more relevant to navigation, we introduce a cross-space planning consistency loss that encourages directional agreement between the predicted map-space trajectory and the expert action direction derived from the ground-truth waypoint displacement. Experiments on OpenUAV and AerialVLN-S, together with extensive ablations, demonstrate strong performance and support the effectiveness and stability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [32] [ASPIRE-VINS: Adaptive Spline-based Visual-inertial Navigation System With Robust 3D Measurement Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12840)
*Kwangyik Jung,Eungchang Mason Lee,Taekjun Oh,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ASPIRE-VINS，一个结合自适应节点放置、多分辨率样条和3D测量空间残差的连续时间视觉-惯性导航系统（VINS）。与传统均匀间隔节点的连续时间样条不同，ASPIRE-VINS根据局部运动变化自适应分配样条节点，在切空间添加有界局部细化，并通过3D测量空间残差将对齐特征与校准观测射线进行一致性约束。实验表明其在多样运动和传感条件下取得有竞争力或更低的轨迹误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散时间VINS方法（如IMU预积分）在任意时间戳评估残差或对运动相关的时域分辨率需求上不够灵活；而连续时间样条虽能表示平滑的时间函数，但均匀间隔的节点在快速动态区域欠采样、静态区间又过度参数化。现有方法缺乏自适应节点分配机制来平衡表示精度与计算效率。

Method: 提出三个核心组件的组合：（1）自适应节点放置（AKP）根据局部运动变化分配样条节点；（2）多分辨率样条（MRS）在切空间中添加有界的局部细化，允许不同时间尺度上的精细化表示；（3）3D测量空间残差（3D-MSR）通过将变换后的特征与校准观测射线在3D测量空间中对齐来实现方向一致性。

Result: 实验表明ASPIRE-VINS在多种运动和传感条件下，相比基准方法取得了有竞争力或更低的轨迹误差，验证了运动自适应连续时间轨迹建模的有效性。

Conclusion: 运动自适应的连续时间轨迹建模（通过AKP和MRS）结合3D测量空间残差，能够在不牺牲精度的情况下，克服均匀节点样条在动态范围适应性上的不足，为VINS提供了一种更灵活高效的轨迹表示方案。

Abstract: Visual-inertial navigation systems estimate six-degree-of-freedom motion by fusing visual and inertial data. Modern discrete-time methods with IMU preintegration provide strong accuracy and efficiency, but keyframe-based representations can be less flexible when residuals must be evaluated at arbitrary timestamps or when motion-dependent temporal resolution is needed. Continuous-time splines address this issue by representing the trajectory as a smooth temporal function, but uniformly spaced knots can under-represent rapid dynamics or over-parameterize static intervals. This letter proposes ASPIRE-VINS, a continuous-time VINS framework that combines adaptive knot placement (AKP), multi-resolution splines (MRS), and 3D measurement-space residuals (3D-MSR). AKP allocates knots according to local motion variation, MRS adds bounded local refinement in tangent space, and 3D-MSR provides bearing consistency by aligning transformed features with calibrated observation rays in 3D measurement space. Experiments show that ASPIRE-VINS achieves competitive or lower trajectory errors than the compared baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of motion-adaptive continuous-time trajectory modeling under diverse motion and sensing conditions.

</details>


### [33] [BrainWAM: Action-Space Coordination of Semantic Priors and Predictive Dynamics for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12854)
*Bing Zhan,Shuyao Shang,Jiahao Gu,Shuo Lu,Yuan Xu,Zhao Wang,Yida Wang,Xueyang Zhang,Kun Zhan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BrainWAM是一种结构化动作空间协调框架，将VLA模型的语义推理与World Action Model的预测动力学统一到自动驾驶规划中，通过将两者转化为专门的动作导向通路并在紧凑动作表示层面对齐，解决了联合token级注意力中的注意力分配不匹配问题，同时在NAVSIM v1和v2上取得最先进表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶方法要么只强调语义约束（VLA模型），要么只强调预测动力学（WAM模型），缺乏统一两者的有效方案。而简单通过联合token级注意力组合两者会导致注意力分配不匹配：语义捷径主导共享注意力空间，压制预测动力学信号。

Method: BrainWAM提出三个核心设计：(1) 结构化动作空间协调框架，受神经科学中功能特化系统协调行为的启发，将语义推理与预测世界建模转化为两条专门的动作导向通路；(2) 在紧凑动作表示层面对齐两条通路，而非在高维token级注意力层面融合；(3) 异步rectified-flow推理策略，采用解耦的视频与动作去噪过程，缩短推理延迟同时保留规划相关的预测上下文。

Result: BrainWAM在NAVSIM v1上达到89.5 PDMS，在NAVSIM v2上达到89.6 EPDMS，均为最先进水平，并持续优于仅用VLA或仅用WAM的方法。

Conclusion: 通过结构化的动作级协调而非粗暴的token级注意力融合，BrainWAM证明语义先验与预测动力学可以互补共进，为自动驾驶系统提供了一个实用且有前景的统一规划方向。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires planning under both semantic constraints and predictive dynamics. Existing end-to-end driving approaches, however, typically emphasize only one side of this requirement: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exploit VLM priors for semantic reasoning, while World Action Models (WAMs) provide future-aware prediction through generative world modeling. This naturally motivates a unified planner that can leverage both semantic priors and predictive dynamics. However, we find that a naive combination through joint token-level attention suffers from an attention-allocation mismatch, where semantic shortcuts dominate the shared attention space and suppress predictive dynamics. Inspired by neuroscience evidence that complex behavior arises from coordination among functionally specialized systems, we propose BrainWAM, a structured action-space coordination framework that converts semantic reasoning and predictive world modeling into two specialized action-oriented pathways, and aligns them at the level of compact action representations. We further introduce an asynchronous rectified-flow inference strategy with decoupled video and action denoising, which shortens inference latency while preserving planning-relevant predictive context. BrainWAM reaches state-of-the-art performance on both NAVSIM v1 (89.5 PDMS) and NAVSIM v2 (89.6 EPDMS), consistently outperforming VLA-only or WAM-only methods, highlighting BrainWAM as a practical and promising direction for autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [34] [HumanoidVLN: A Physics-Grounded Simulator and Benchmark for Vision-Language Navigation Across Diverse Humanoid Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12860)
*Quan-Dung Pham,Anh Dao,The-Anh Nguyen,Minh Nguyen-Dinh,Phuong Nam Dang,Tri Pham,Hung Tran,Bach Dao,Tuyen P. Le,Truong Nguyen,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HumanoidVLN，一个基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim的物理仿真VLN基准平台，支持多形态双足人形机器人（Unitree G1/H1及两个内部平台），涵盖10-12自由度下肢和1.17-1.80m身高范围。平台通过层级控制栈（RL运动策略+PD/MPC路径跟踪器）实现物理约束下的视觉语言导航，并验证了与NaVILA、DualVLN、StreamVLN、JanusVLN等模型的兼容性，以及模拟到真实的强相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN基准未考虑人形机器人的独特挑战：双足运动引入轮式代理不具备的物理约束、平台间形态差异大、步态引起的摄像机动态会扭曲第一人称观察。这些问题导致现有基准无法有效评估人形机器人的VLN性能。

Method: 构建物理仿真平台HumanoidVLN：1）基于Isaac Sim，采用层级控制栈（RL运动策略+可互换PD或MPC路径跟踪器）支持10-12自由度、1.17-1.80m的四种人形机器人；2）在艺术家设计场景和3D高斯泼溅重建环境中筛选超100平方米导航区域；3）用生成器-审阅器-改写器的多智能体流水线配合人工验证生成933条碰撞感知参考场景，每条配一个细粒度指令和三种粗粒度风格变体；4）进行20场景模拟到真实试点实验。

Result: 在四种模型和四种形态的评估中，JanusVLN达到最高平均成功率43.55%和nDTW 48.38。模拟到真实试点中，DualVLN配合Unitree G1的导航误差强相关（r=0.935），平均绝对差0.68m，平均轨迹相似度0.782（±0.188）nDTW。

Conclusion: 结果表明VLN模型、控制器与人形形态在物理执行下的交互效应显著，HumanoidVLN为研究这种交互提供了标准化平台。代码、基准和数据将在录用后开源。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) for humanoid robots poses challenges existing benchmarks fail to address: bipedal locomotion imposes physical constraints absent from wheeled agents, humanoid morphologies vary across platforms, and egocentric observations are distorted by locomotion-induced camera dynamics. We present HumanoidVLN, a physics-grounded simulator and benchmark for VLN across diverse humanoid embodiments. Built on NVIDIA Isaac Sim, our platform supports an extensible set of humanoid configurations, demonstrated on four robots (Unitree G1, Unitree H1, Internal-A, Internal-B) spanning 10-12 lower-body DoF and heights from 1.17m to 1.80m, via a hierarchical control stack combining a reinforcement learning locomotion policy with interchangeable PD or MPC path trackers. New robots and VLN models integrate with minimal effort; we demonstrate compatibility with NaVILA, DualVLN, StreamVLN, and JanusVLN. Environments are drawn from artist-designed scenes and 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructions, filtered for navigable areas exceeding 100 square meters. Instructions are generated by a dual generator-reviewer plus paraphraser multi-agent pipeline with human-in-the-loop verification, yielding 933 collision-aware reference episodes, each paired with one fine-grained instruction and three coarse-grained stylistic variants (formal, natural, casual). Across four models and four embodiments, JanusVLN achieves the highest mean success rate of 43.55% and nDTW of 48.38. In a 20-episode sim-to-real pilot with DualVLN and the Unitree G1, navigation errors correlate strongly (r=0.935), with a mean absolute difference of 0.68m and mean trajectory similarity of 0.782 (+/-0.188) nDTW. These results highlight the interaction between VLN models, controllers, and humanoid embodiments under physical execution. Code, benchmark, and data will be released upon acceptance at https://humanoid-vln.github.io/.

</details>


### [35] [AMR-Pose: An Active LED Marker-Based Relative Pose Estimation Framework With Probabilistic Switching PnP for Cooperative AUVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12866)
*Zeyu Sha,Xiaorui Wang,Mingyang Yang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AMR-Pose，一种基于主动LED标记的自主水下航行器（AUV）相对位姿估计框架，通过一个中心红色LED和三个蓝色外围LED组成的紧凑标记模块，以及结合李群位姿传播、概率标记关联和可见性自适应测量融合的PSwPnP估计器，在水箱实验中实现了鲁棒的六自由度相对位姿估计，并通过闭环主从实验验证了实时应用的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV之间的可靠相对位姿估计对协作海洋探索、采样和多机器人协调至关重要，但水下环境中严重的视觉退化（浊度、光照变化、反射、特征间歇性遮挡）使得基于视觉的相对定位极具挑战，亟需一种鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 提出AMR-Pose框架：(1)开发紧凑的有源LED标记模块，由一个红色中心LED和三个蓝色外围LED组成，集成到领航AUV上以提供水下复杂环境中的独特视觉特征；(2)构建概率切换PnP估计器（PSwPnP），将SE(3)上的李群位姿传播、概率标记关联和可见性自适应测量融合相结合，实现鲁棒的六自由度相对位姿估计；(3)框架根据标记可见性动态调整估计过程，在部分观测和可见性转换期间保持几何一致性和时间稳定性。

Result: 大量水箱实验（以运动捕捉系统为真值）验证AMR-Pose在挑战性水下条件下实现了准确、平滑且鲁棒的相对位姿估计；闭环主从领航实验进一步证明了其在协作水下机器人中实时相对位姿反馈的可行性。

Conclusion: AMR-Pose通过主动LED标记与概率切换PnP估计器的有机结合，有效解决了水下视觉退化环境下的AUV相对位姿估计难题，为协作水下机器人系统的实时相对定位提供了可行且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable relative pose estimation between autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) is critical for cooperative ocean exploration, sampling, and multi-robot coordination. However, achieving robust vision-based relative localization in underwater environments remains challenging due to severe optical degradation, including turbidity, illumination variations, reflections, and intermittent feature occlusions. This paper presents AMR-Pose, an active LED marker-based relative pose estimation framework for cooperative AUVs. A compact marker module consisting of one red central LED and three blue peripheral LEDs is developed and integrated onto the leader AUV to provide distinctive visual features under complex underwater conditions. Building upon the detected marker observations, a probabilistic switching Perspective-n-Point estimator (PSwPnP) is developed by combining Lie-group pose propagation on $SE(3)$, probabilistic marker association, and visibility-adaptive measurement fusion for robust six-degree-of-freedom relative pose estimation. The proposed framework dynamically adapts the estimation process according to marker visibility, maintaining geometric consistency and temporal stability during partial observations and visibility transitions. Extensive water-tank experiments with motion-capture ground truth validate that AMR-Pose achieves accurate, smooth, and robust relative pose estimation under challenging underwater conditions. Closed-loop leader-follower experiments further demonstrate its feasibility for real-time relative pose feedback in cooperative underwater robotics.

</details>


### [36] [Temporal GRPO: Beyond Trajectory-Level Credit in Vision-Language-Action Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13026)
*Yao Zhou,Hang Gao,Fengge Wu,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Temporal GRPO，一种面向视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略后训练的时间级GRPO方法。它通过构建可检测的任务阶段、将rollout与阶段特定的动作区间对齐，并仅比较进入同一阶段的rollout，从而在时间维度上细粒度地分配优势，解决了轨迹级信用混叠（trajectory-level credit aliasing）问题，在RoboTwin 2.0和LIBERO-Long基准上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结果驱动的强化学习（如GRPO）在VLA策略后训练中，通常对轨迹中的所有动作施加一个统一的rollout级优势。当一个rollout完成了多个有效阶段但在后期失败时，前期的正确动作也会被错误地惩罚，这称为轨迹级信用混叠（trajectory-level credit aliasing）。这种粗粒度的信用分配方式限制了策略学习的效率和准确性。

Method: Temporal GRPO通过以下三个关键步骤解决信用混叠问题：(1) 构建可检测的任务阶段（detectable task stages）；(2) 将每个rollout与阶段特定的动作区间（stage-specific action intervals）对齐；(3) 仅比较进入同一阶段的rollout，计算阶段优势（stage advantages）并应用于对应的动作区间，在单次策略更新中完成所有阶段的优化。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0基准上，Temporal GRPO显著提升了任务成功率和样本效率，并在不同任务长度上保持一致的性能增益。在LIBERO-Long上的受控更新实验表明，该方法能够保留共享的前置阶段，并将改进集中在rollout结果首次出现分歧的阶段。

Conclusion: Temporal GRPO通过将信用分配从轨迹级细化为阶段级，有效解决了VLA策略后训练中的轨迹级信用混叠问题，实现了更精细、更高效的策略优化，为面向长时程任务的VLA后训练提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Outcome-driven reinforcement learning offers a scalable way to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) policies from sparse task-success feedback. In common GRPO-based VLA post-training, one rollout-level advantage is applied to every action in the trajectory. A rollout that completes several valid stages but fails later can therefore penalize the actions that produced its earlier progress. We call this trajectory-level credit aliasing. Temporal GRPO addresses this problem by constructing detectable task stages, aligning each rollout with stage-specific action intervals, and comparing only rollouts that have entered the same stage. The resulting stage advantages are applied to their corresponding intervals in a single policy update. On RoboTwin 2.0, Temporal GRPO improves task success and sample efficiency, with consistent gains across task horizons. Controlled updates on LIBERO-Long preserve shared prerequisite stages and concentrate improvement at the first stage where rollout outcomes diverge.

</details>

*Dingyi Rong,Yue Shi,Chaofan Ma,Jiezhang Cao,Zongrui Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Yao Mu,Guangtao Zhai,Ning Liu*

TL;DR: 本文提出H2R-Bench基准，用于评估跨具身（human-to-robot）操作视频生成任务，即从人类第一人称演示视频生成指定机器人具身条件下的操作视频，并从五个维度系统评估视频世界模型的跨具身迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建H2R-Bench基准，每个实例包含人类演示视频、目标具身约束以及源grounding标注（任务目标、动作事件、功能性接触、物体响应）。从五个维度评估生成视频：目标状态完成度、动作事件完成度、功能性接触迁移、具身正确性及一般视频质量。对11个SOTA视频生成模型在6个操作类别和2种机器人具身上进行基准测试。

Result: 评测发现当前视频世界模型在人到机器人操作迁移中能力有限：即使最先进的模型在具身一致性、功能性交互和任务执行方面仍常失败。

Conclusion: H2R-Bench提供了系统化的诊断框架，用于评估视频世界模型能否弥合人-机器人具身差距，将人类操作观察转化为面向机器人的训练资源。

Abstract: Large-scale manipulation data is essential for robot learning, yet collecting robot demonstrations remains expensive and difficult to scale. Meanwhile, abundant egocentric human manipulation videos provide rich behavioral experiences, but transferring them across embodiments remains challenging due to differences between human hands and robotic end-effectors. Recent advances in video world models offer a promising pathway to synthesize robot-centric manipulation videos from human observations, while their cross-embodiment transfer capability remains largely unexplored. Therefore, we introduce H2R-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating cross-embodiment human-to-robot manipulation video generation, where models transform egocentric human demonstrations into robot manipulation videos under specified embodiments. Each benchmark instance contains a human demonstration video, target embodiment constraints, and source-grounded annotations covering task goals, action events, functional contacts, and object responses. H2R-Bench evaluates generated videos through five dimensions, including goal-state completion, action-event completion, functional contact transfer, embodiment correctness, and general video quality. We benchmark eleven state-of-the-art video generation models across six manipulation families and two robot embodiments. Our evaluation reveals that current video world models remain limited in human-to-robot manipulation transfer: even leading models often fail in embodiment consistency, functional interaction, and task execution. H2R-Bench provides a systematic diagnostic framework for evaluating whether video world models can bridge the human-to-robot embodiment gap and convert human manipulation observations into robot-centric training resources.

</details>

*Nico Heider,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Katarzyna Wasielewska-Michniewska,Przemysław Hołda,Martin Schieck,Marcin Paprzycki,Maria Ganzha,Bogdan Franczyk*

TL;DR: 本文提出使用语义辐射场（SRF）作为具身智能体空间推理的训练与评估模拟器。SRF将预训练视觉模型的2D语义分割结果提升到3D辐射场中，联合编码几何、外观与逐类语义信息，从真实场景的带位姿RGB图像中重建，支持新视角合成、语义查询和自由空间查询，兼顾真实感与语义标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模拟器存在两难：合成模拟器具有真实语义标注但缺乏真实感；基于真实场景重建的模拟器外观真实但默认缺乏语义标注。空间推理智能体的训练与评估需要既几何保真又可语义查询的多样化环境。

Method: 将预训练视觉模型的2D语义分割提升为3D辐射场（SRF），联合编码几何、外观与语义身份，从带位姿的真实场景RGB捕获中重建。该表示支持在单一grounded表示中进行新视角合成、语义查询和自由空间查询，并可与物理引擎配合驱动模拟器。

Result: 以果园苹果采摘任务为例，展示了SRF驱动的模拟器框架：辐射场为物理引擎提供相机渲染、语义真值和占用查询，实现高效生成多样化真实环境用于训练和评估空间推理模型。

Conclusion: SRF作为统一表示弥合了合成模拟器与真实场景重建模拟器之间的差距，为空间推理具身智能体提供既真实又具语义标注的模拟环境生成途径。

Abstract: Training and evaluating spatial reasoning in embodied agents requires diverse environments that are both geometrically faithful and semantically queryable. Synthetic simulators offer ground truth semantics but sacrifice realism; simulators based on reconstructions of real-world environments have realistic appearance but lack ground truth semantics by default. We propose using Semantic Radiance Fields (SRF) as simulators for spatial reasoning agents. SRFs are a representation that unifies these requirements by lifting 2D semantic segmentations from pretrained vision models into a 3D radiance field that jointly encodes geometry, appearance, and per-class semantic identity. The resulting fields are reconstructed from posed RGB captures of real scenes and support novel-view synthesis, semantic and free-space queries within a single grounded representation. This enables the efficient generation of diverse real-world environments to train and evaluate spatial reasoning models. As an example application, we outline an SRF-driven simulator for an orchard apple-reaching task, in which the radiance field supplies camera rendering, semantic ground truth, and occupancy queries to a physics engine.

</details>


### [39] [S2-HWM: Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model for Long-Horizon Surgical Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13103)
*Shuzhe Zhang,Xin Zhu,Yinling Qian,Qiong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出S2-HWM（稀疏事件结构分层世界模型），通过学习原始潜在轨迹中的稀疏事件证据，协调事件级管理器和原始步工人，实现长视野手术机器人操控。在SurRoL PegTransfer任务上达到98.7%成功率，超越平面基线GAS DreamerV3达22.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野手术机器人操控中，任务奖励稀疏且有意义的交互变化发生在不规则的间隔处。现有的世界模型智能体通常以原始步分辨率进行想象，难以显式建模可变时长的任务进度；手动指定的阶段边界难以与状态依赖的交互转换对齐。

Method: S2-HWM包含三个核心组件：(1) 从原始潜在轨迹中学习稀疏事件证据，用于调度事件级管理者（manager）的目标更新； (2) 每个选定的潜在目标条件化工人（worker）的原始步动作直至下一次更新； (3) 事件证据形成可变时长分段，供事件转换模型（ETM）预测下一个边界随机状态、分段时长和累计分段奖励。链式事件级预测提供超出原始想象视野的可变时长延续用于管理者学习，而工人保留原始步的演员-评论家学习。

Result: 在SurRoL-based PegTransfer任务上，S2-HWM达到98.7%的成功率，相较于平面的GAS DreamerV3基线提升22.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过稀疏事件结构化的分层世界模型，有效解决了长视野任务中稀疏奖励和可变交互间隔带来的挑战，显著提升了手术机器人操控的成功率。

Abstract: Long-horizon surgical robot manipulation is challenging because task rewards are sparse, while meaningful interaction changes occur at irregular intervals. Existing world-model agents typically imagine at primitive-step resolution, leaving variable-duration task progress implicit. Manually specified stages can provide intermediate structure, but their task specific boundaries are difficult to align with state-dependent interaction transitions. We propose S2-HWM, a Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model that learns sparse event evidence from primitive latent trajectories to coordinate an event-level manager and a primitive-step worker. The event evidence schedules manager goal updates, and each selected latent goal conditions the worker's primitive actions until the next update. The learned event evidence also forms variable-duration segments for an Event Transition Model (ETM), which predicts the next?boundary stochastic state, segment duration, and accumulated segment reward. Chaining these event-level predictions provides a variable-duration continuation beyond the primitive imagination horizon for manager learning, while the worker retains primitive-step actor-critic learning. On a SurRoL-based PegTransfer task, S2-HWM achieves a success rate of 98.7%, outperforming the flat GAS DreamerV3 baseline by 22.7 percentage points.

</details>


### [40] [FAM-DQ: A Dual-Quadrotor-Based Fully Actuated Aerial Manipulator for High-Torque Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13220)
*Xuwei Yang,Ruoyu Ren,Ziqian Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FAM-DQ，一种基于双四旋翼的完全驱动空中机械臂，通过将两个四旋翼推进模块安装在中央框架两端并使用齿轮驱动舵机调节指向方向，实现6自由度解耦的末端执行器控制，具备全向操作能力和高扭矩输出（最大扭矩1.019 N·m，扭矩质量比2.28 N·m/kg）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统欠驱动空中机械臂存在强位置-姿态耦合，而完全驱动平台设计常面临结构复杂、有效载荷有限及扭矩输出不足的问题，难以满足空中物理交互对大相互作用力和扭矩的需求。

Method: 提出FAM-DQ平台，将两个四旋翼推进模块通过被动关节安装在中央框架两端，利用齿轮驱动舵机调节指向方向，从而实现6自由度末端执行器控制的解耦，同时具备全向操作能力和增强的扭矩输出。

Result: 通过轨迹跟踪、姿态跟踪、静态扭矩测量和拧螺丝等实验验证了设计的有效性。FAM-DQ实现最大扭矩1.019 N·m，总质量仅0.447 kg，扭矩质量比达2.28 N·m/kg。

Conclusion: FAM-DQ成功解决了传统设计方案中位置-姿态耦合与复杂结构、有限扭矩之间的矛盾，为高空物理交互任务提供了一种高扭矩、轻量化且具备全向操作能力的可行解决方案。

Abstract: Aerial physical interaction requires aerial manipulation platforms to generate large interaction forces and torques while maintaining precise end-effector control. However, conventional underactuated aerial manipulators suffer from strong position-attitude coupling, whereas fully actuated platform designs often face structural complexity, limited payload capacity, and insufficient torque output. This paper presents FAM-DQ, a dual-quadrotor based fully actuated aerial manipulator designed for high-torque physical interaction tasks. By mounting two quadrotor propulsion modules at the ends of a central frame through passive joints, while using a gear-driven servo to regulate the pointing direction, FAM-DQ achieves decoupled $6$-DoF end-effector control with omnidirectional manipulation capability and enhanced torque output. Experiments including trajectory tracking, attitude tracking, static torque measurement, and screw driving validate the proposed design. FAM-DQ achieves a maximum torque of $1.019~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m}$ with a total mass of $0.447~\mathrm{kg}$, corresponding to a torque-to-mass ratio of $2.28~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m/kg}$.

</details>


### [41] [Manufacturing Complex Airtight Soft Pneumatic Actuators for Soft Robotics: Process Evaluation and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13233)
*Mohammed Abboodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对制造复杂软体气动执行器的多种加工路线（热缩成型、硅胶浇注、粉末/液体基增材制造及FDM）进行了系统评估，发现FDM因缺陷可通过工艺优化逐步消除而最具适应性，并提出了以工艺选择与执行器架构兼容性为核心的设计制造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造复杂软体气动执行器需要同时实现几何保真度、柔顺性、结构完整性和气密性，这是一项具有挑战性的任务。现有加工路线各有限制，但缺乏系统性的制造导向评估来区分工艺固有局限与可修正缺陷，因此需要建立设计制造的实践指导方法。

Method: 通过过程筛选、基线制造、失效分析和工艺改进四个步骤，系统评估了热缩成型、硅胶浇注、粉末基与液体基增材制造以及FDM等制造路线。考察了各方法的几何顺应性、模具可达性、残余材料滞留、材料性能与后处理要求，并针对FDM进行工艺优化以消除主导缺陷。

Result: 热缩成型受几何顺应性限制，浇注受模具可达性和粘接界面限制，粉末基方法受封闭通道内残余材料困扰，数字光处理受材料属性和后处理要求限制。FDM是最具适应性的路线，其主导缺陷可通过工艺优化逐步减少。此外发现气密性不仅取决于名义壁厚，还与挤出路径架构相关；无支撑几何在内部后处理受限时至关重要。

Conclusion: 提出了以执行器架构与制造约束兼容性为指导的实践性设计制造方法，为开发复杂、柔顺且气密的软体气动执行器提供了参考，适用于软体机器人应用。

Abstract: Manufacturing complex soft pneumatic actuators remains challenging because geometric fidelity, compliance, structural integrity, and airtightness must be achieved simultaneously. This study presents a manufacturing-focused evaluation of several fabrication routes for complex pneumatic structures, including heat-shrink forming, silicone casting, powder- and liquid-based additive manufacturing, and fused deposition modeling (FDM). The processes were assessed through process screening, baseline fabrication, failure analysis, and process improvement to distinguish inherent process limitations from correctable manufacturing defects. Heat-shrink forming was limited by geometric conformity, casting by mold accessibility and bonded interfaces, powder-based methods by residual material trapped within enclosed passages, and digital light processing by the material properties and post-processing requirements of the investigated system. FDM provided the most adaptable route because its dominant defects could be progressively reduced through process optimization. The results further showed that airtightness depends not only on nominal wall thickness but also on extrusion-path architecture, while support-free geometry is important when access for internal post-processing is limited. These findings establish a practical design-for-manufacturing approach in which process selection is guided by the compatibility between actuator architecture and manufacturing constraints. The proposed approach provides practical guidance for developing complex, flexible, and airtight soft pneumatic actuators for soft robotic applications

</details>


### [42] [Predictive Relative-Velocity Steering for Safe Robotic Manipulator Teleoperation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13284)
*Changhao Hu,Zeyi Liu,Songqiao Hu,Shuang Liu,Zihan Meng,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量模块化的主动碰撞规避框架，直接作用于末端执行器速度指令层。通过基于时间到碰撞（TTC）的预测和Rodrigues旋转公式偏转相对速度向量，在保持速度大小不变的同时改变方向，避免了传统人工势场法常见的死锁问题，仿真和实物实验均验证了更高的避碰成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作机器人虽能实现类人灵巧运动，但操作者在网络延迟或注意力受限情况下难以及时有效地规避突然出现的障碍物，存在安全隐患。现有方法（如APF）存在死锁问题，且复杂遥操作流水线引入的点云处理延迟未得到很好补偿，因此需要一种高效、实时且能主动规避碰撞的框架。

Method: 该框架在末端执行器速度指令层面轻量模块化运行：(1) 预处理点云；(2) 基于时间到碰撞（TTC）并整合过冲保护预测潜在碰撞；(3) 使用Rodrigues旋转公式偏转相对速度向量，仅改变方向而保持幅值不变，从而规避死锁问题。预测模块补偿复杂遥操作流水线引入的点云处理延迟，轻量设计支持高频控制。

Result: 多种场景的仿真结果表明，所提方法实现了比基线方法更高的末端执行器避碰成功率；在实体机器人系统上的实验进一步验证了其避碰有效性。

Conclusion: 该速度指令级别的主动碰撞规避框架能有效应对遥操作中的突发障碍物规避问题，通过TTC预测与速度方向偏转机制的结合，在保证速度幅值的前提下实现高效避碰，且轻量化的特性使其适用于高频实时遥操作系统。

Abstract: Recent advances in teleoperation have enabled robotic manipulators to perform dexterous, human-arm-like motions. However, human operators may fail to avoid suddenly appearing obstacles promptly and effectively, particularly under network latency or limited attention, thereby creating safety risks. To address this issue, we propose a lightweight and modular framework for proactive collision avoidance, operating directly at the end-effector velocity-command level. After preprocessing the point cloud, the framework first predicts potential collisions based on time-to-collision (TTC) with integrated overshoot protection, and subsequently rotates the relative-velocity vector using Rodrigues' rotation formula. The deflection changes only the direction of the relative velocity while preserving its magnitude, thereby mitigating the deadlock problem commonly encountered by conventional artificial potential field (APF) methods. The prediction module compensates for point-cloud processing latency introduced by complex teleoperation pipelines, while the lightweight design enables the high-frequency control required for teleoperation. Simulations across diverse scenarios show that the proposed method achieves a higher end-effector collision avoidance rate than the baseline methods. Experiments on a physical robotic system further validate its collision-avoidance effectiveness.

</details>


### [43] [NestDex: Nested Policy Learning with Copilot Assisted Teleoperation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13362)
*James Zhao,Jinhe Tang,Mingyuan Ba,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NestDex是一个嵌套策略学习框架，通过让操作员用单自由度离合器控制手臂并调节预学习的手部技能来完成灵巧操作演示收集，从而减轻了灵巧操纵任务中演示采集的负担。学到的演示可用于训练独立的视触觉策略，在部署时不依赖内层策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作能极大丰富机器人与物理世界的交互，但学习这类行为受限于收集一致且完整的任务演示的困难。与平行夹爪操作不同，灵巧任务要求操作员在整个任务过程中协调手臂运动与精确的接触丰富的手指行为，这使直接指定完整手指轨迹的演示采集变得极其困难。

Method: NestDex采用嵌套策略学习框架：内层手部策略根据最新的本体感觉历史自适应调整动作，视觉-语言选择器为每个任务阶段激活合适的技能；操作员通过单自由度离合器控制手臂并调节主动手部技能，而非直接指定完整手指轨迹；手部动作使用变分自编码器（VAE）提供紧凑的手部动作目标，同时保留关节空间中的手臂指令；训练得到的演示用于训练一个独立的外层视触觉策略，部署时同时控制手臂和手部而不需要内层策略。

Result: 在真实世界的灵巧操作实验中，NestDex提高了演示采集的可靠性和效率，经验评估支持有效的自主策略学习。

Conclusion: NestDex通过嵌套策略框架有效缓解了灵巧操纵中演示采集的困难，利用预学习的手部技能辅助演示收集，并能学得可部署的自主策略，展示了将人类监督降低到单自由度层面的高效灵巧操作学习路径。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation promises substantially richer robot interaction with the physical world, but learning these behaviours remains constrained by the difficulty of collecting consistent, complete-task demonstrations. Unlike parallel-jaw manipulation, dexterous tasks require the operator to coordinate arm motion with precise, contact-rich finger behaviour throughout the task. We introduce NestDex, a nested policy-learning framework that reduces this burden by using learned hand skills to assist demonstration collection. The operator controls the arm and regulates the active hand skill through a single-DoF clutch, rather than directly specifying the full finger trajectory. The inner hand policy adapts its motion from the latest proprioceptive history, while a vision-language selector activates the appropriate skill for each task stage. The resulting demonstrations train a separate outer visuomotor policy that controls both the arm and hand without the inner policies at deployment. A hand-action variational autoencoder provides compact hand-action targets while retaining arm commands in joint space. Across real-world dexterous manipulation experiments, NestDex improves demonstration reliability and efficiency, and the resulting empirical evaluations support effective autonomous policy learning. Video Demo are available at project website https://aus.bot/research/nestdex.

</details>


### [44] [FIRE-VLA: Failure-Informed Self-Evolution for Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13395)
*Hao Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FIRE-VLA提出一种基于失败信息的自进化框架，通过从冻结的轮次起始教师副本进行自蒸馏，将GRPO未能解决的失败样本转化为下一轮的监督信号，在不增加外部教师的情况下降低自动驾驶VLA模型的规划误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO在轨迹评价中仅依赖组内相对奖励差异，当所有采样轨迹都较差时，相对信号只能对失败进行排序而无法识别失败区域之外的行为，导致失败无法被有效纠正。

Method: FIRE-VLA框架包含：(1) 检测低奖励、低多样性的失败组；(2) 触发从冻结的轮次起始教师副本进行自蒸馏，其中教师与学生的参数规模相同，但教师仅能观察隐藏的未来轨迹；(3) 监督信号跟随学生生成的前缀并仅限制在答案token上；(4) GRPO在每个组中持续激活；更新后的策略作为下一轮的教师，使失败分布随策略演化。

Result: 在6,019个来自150个保留nuScenes场景的样本上，FIRE-VLA保持可比的单样本规划，将G=4平均L2误差从1.848降至1.500米，并将评估持续失败率从13.03%降至11.20%。平均误差的降低主要来自罕见的严重滚动样本，而非均匀改进。

Conclusion: FIRE-VLA证明通过失败信息引导的自蒸馏可以有效弥补GRPO在全部失败场景下的信号不足，且无需引入更大的外部教师模型，即可显著改善自动驾驶VLA的规划性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning improves autonomous-driving vision-language-action (VLA) models by evaluating trajectories sampled from the current policy. Group relative policy optimization (GRPO) learns from reward differences within each rollout group. When all sampled trajectories are poor, this relative signal can rank failures without identifying behavior outside the failed region. We introduce FIRE-VLA, a failure-informed self-evolution framework that converts such unresolved failures into privileged supervision for the next policy. Low-reward, low-diversity groups trigger self-distillation from a frozen round-start copy of the same model. Teacher and student have the same parameter scale, but only the teacher observes the hidden future trajectory. Supervision follows the student's generated prefix and is restricted to answer tokens, while GRPO remains active for every group. The updated policy supplies the teacher for the next round, allowing the routed failure distribution to change with the policy without requiring a larger external teacher. Starting from the same Qwen2.5-VL-3B SFT checkpoint, the comparison matches student rollout and policy-update counts. On 6,019 examples from 150 held-out nuScenes scenes, FIRE-VLA retains comparable single-sample planning, reduces G=4 mean L2 from 1.848 to 1.500 m, and lowers evaluation-persistent failure prevalence from 13.03% to 11.20%. The reduction in mean error arises mainly from rare severe rollouts rather than uniform improvement across ordinary trajectories.

</details>
