*Gang Zhang,Yufu Qiu,Junyan Yan,Wenhui Zeng,Wenlong Lu,Shing Shin Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于"驱动-感知协同设计"（actuation-perception co-design）思想的一体化设计-建模-传感框架，通过在电机安装支架中引入柔性元件实现缆绳张力实时测量，并基于多体短厚梁模型的并行计算框架实现实时形状与力感知，最终在单段和双段结构的缆绳驱动连续体手术机器人上完成了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在紧凑型缆绳驱动连续体手术机器人中，由于狭小的capsule（绞盘）装配空间内难以获取缆绳张力信息，形状感知和力感知长期是关键技术瓶颈，这限制了机器人的集成化感知能力。

Method: （1）在驱动系统的电机安装支架中引入柔性元件，使其在缆绳反作用力下产生微变形，从而在不占用紧凑绞盘空间的前提下实现缆绳张力的实时测量；（2）针对空间缆绳布线策略带来的非传统关节构型，提出基于多体短厚梁模型的并行计算框架，捕捉短梁段的剪切效应和多缆协同相互作用，并实现实时性能；（3）引入近端多轴力/力矩传感器作为额外的测量锚点，共同实现稳定的形状与力感知。最终开发了单段和双段配置的连续体手术机器人。

Result: 在单段和双段连续体机器人上均验证了所提框架的有效性，实现了实时尖端位姿估计以及接触力和接触位置的感知。实验结果表明该框架能够在保持紧凑绞盘架构不变的前提下实现缆绳张力反馈和集成化感知。

Conclusion: 所提出的设计-建模-传感框架在不牺牲紧凑性的前提下，使缆绳驱动连续体手术机器人具备集成的形状与力感知能力，为其在微创手术等场景中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Shape and force sensing have long been critical bottlenecks in the development of compact capstan-driven continuum surgical robots, primarily due to the difficulty of obtaining cable tension information within the confined capstan assembly. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents an integrated design-modeling-sensing approach based on the concept of actuation-perception co-design. A compliant element is introduced into the motor mounting bracket of the drive system, enabling micro-deformation under the cable reaction force and thereby allowing real-time cable tension measurement without occupying the compact capstan space. To address the modeling complexity arising from unconventional joint configurations introduced by the spatial cable routing strategy, a parallel computation framework based on a multibody short-thick-beam model is proposed, which captures shear effects in short beam segments and synergistic multi-cable interactions while achieving real-time performance. Building on this framework, stable shape and force sensing is achieved by incorporating a proximal multi-axis force/torque sensor as an additional measurement anchor. Following this design-modeling-sensing framework, capstan-driven continuum surgical robots with single- and dual-segment configurations are developed. Experimental results validate the proposed framework in both single- and dual-segment continuum robots, demonstrating real-time tip pose estimation together with contact force and location perception. By enabling cable tension feedback without compromising the compact capstan architecture, the proposed framework makes integrated perception feasible for capstan-driven continuum surgical robots.

</details>


### [46] [Deliberate Practice: Learning Robot Skills under a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13415)
*Shivam Vats,Sudarshan Harithas,Mete Tuluhan Akbulut,Arvind Raghunathan,George Konidaris*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种名为Deliberate Practice (DP)的主动技能学习算法，在有限练习预算下实现预算最优的技能分配，通过双线性规划精确求解，使机器人能最大化长时程任务中的累积奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习顺序任务技能时练习预算有限，需要解决如何在有限练习时间下选择最能提升长期任务累积奖励的技能进行练习的问题。

Method: 提出Deliberate Practice算法，估计技能掌握所需时间及技能解锁任务计划的累积奖励，将预算最优分配问题建模为双线性规划，利用现成求解器精确求解。

Result: 在长时程操作任务的仿真和真实世界实验中，该方法使机器人能在有限练习时间内最优获取有用策略并改善长时程规划。

Conclusion: DP算法提供了可证明的预算最优技能分配方案，有效解决了有限练习预算下顺序任务技能学习的挑战，并展示了在长时程操作任务中的实用价值。

Abstract: We consider the problem of autonomously learning robot skills under a limited practice budget for sequential tasks. We propose an active skill learning algorithm, \emph{Deliberate Practice (DP)}, that computes a provably \emph{budget-optimal} allocation---practicing skills that maximize expected cumulative reward while being learnable within the budget. DP estimates both the time needed to master skills and the cumulative reward of the task plans that the skills unlock. Computing a budget-optimal allocation is challenging as it requires reasoning about combinatorially many skill plans over a large practice budget. Our key contribution is a bilinear program that can compute this exactly using off-the-shelf solvers. Through simulated and real-world experiments on long-horizon manipulation tasks, we show that our approach allows robots to optimally use limited practice time to acquire useful policies and improve long-horizon planning.

</details>


### [47] [Attention from Action, for Action: Emergent Visual Bottlenecks for Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13422)
*Zheyu Zhuang,Ruiyu Wang,Nick Heppert,Johannes Fabian Hahn,Abhinav Valada,Florian T. Pokorny,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Seeker，一种任务和状态条件化的视觉读取机制，从动作监督中学习注意力。Seeker在冻结的DINOv3特征上迭代更新查询，产生随任务进展变化的兴趣区域（ROI），作为RGB裁剪、掩码引导背景增强和点云过滤的空间接口。在仿真和实验中，Seeker相比无裁剪、增强和动作派生裁剪基线，显著提升了数据效率和鲁棒性，真实机器人上域内成功率从最佳基线的48.3%提升至76.7%，光照/背景变化下的成功率从20.0%提升至60.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉瓶颈方法通常依赖外部空间标签（如注视、物体类别、可通行性标注）来定位兴趣区域，这会引入额外标注成本。而标签自由的替代方案（如从轨迹中检测夹爪或运动事件并居中裁剪）虽然无需额外标签，但其固定的裁剪时机、代理点和尺度选择依赖于固定启发式，当控制所需的视觉证据偏离末端执行器或随任务进展连续变化时，这些裁剪会失准。因此需要一种无需外部标签、且能自适应任务进展的学习型注意力机制。

Method: Seeker从冻结的DINOv3特征出发，通过迭代更新一个查询向量来聚合视觉证据，仅通过动作监督就能产生与任务进展相关的兴趣区域（ROI）。该学习得到的ROI作为空间接口，服务于三个下游用途：RGB裁剪、掩码引导的背景增强（background augmentation）、以及点云滤波。整个过程不依赖任何外部空间标签，完全由动作信号驱动。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的实验中，Seeker相对于无裁剪、仅增强以及动作派生裁剪等基线，在数据效率和鲁棒性方面均有提升。在真实机器人上，Seeker将域内平均成功率从最佳基线的48.3%提高到76.7%，在光照/背景变化场景下的成功率从20.0%提高到60.0%。

Conclusion: Seeker验证了从动作监督中学习任务和状态条件化注意力的有效性，它克服了传统动作派生裁剪固定不灵活的局限，同时避免了外部空间标签的依赖。该方法的显著性能提升表明，学习型空间读取机制可以成为视觉运动学习中高效且鲁棒的空间接口。

Abstract: Visual bottlenecks that focus policy inputs on regions of interest (ROIs) can improve data-efficient visuomotor learning by separating where to look from how to act. Many ROI interfaces rely on external spatial labels, such as gaze, object classes, or affordance annotations. Label-free alternatives often derive crops from trajectories by detecting gripper or motion events and centering a fixed crop at the projected end-effector. Such action-derived crops are useful spatial priors that require no additional labels, but they encode fixed choices about event timing, proxy points, and crop scale. When the visual evidence needed for control lies away from the end-effector or changes continuously with task progress, these crops can become misaligned. We propose Seeker, a task- and state-conditioned readout that learns attention from action. Starting from frozen DINOv3 features, Seeker iteratively updates a query with gathered visual evidence, producing progression-aware ROIs solely from action supervision. The learned ROI serves as a spatial interface for RGB cropping, mask-guided background augmentation, and point-cloud filtering. In simulation and the real world, Seeker improves data efficiency and robustness over no-crop, augmentation, and action-derived crop baselines. On real robots, Seeker raises average in-domain success from the best baseline's 48.3% to 76.7% and success under lighting/background shifts from 20.0% to 60.0%.

</details>


### [48] [ContactGuard: Pre-Contact Execution Monitoring with Action-Conditioned Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13438)
*Gehan Zheng,Matthew Johnson-Roberson,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ContactGuard，一种面向分块视觉运动策略的接触前执行监控器。它在潜视觉空间中预测策略动作块的短期后果，并在接触发生前中止可能失败的操作，无需修改底层策略即可在真实机器人上作为接触前中止信号使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务通常在机器人已经与物体接触后才检测到失败，这在腕部相机设置中尤为受限：虽然近距离的夹爪-物体视图有助于观察接触，但糟糕的接近动作可能在传统检测器反应之前就已造成推挤、脱靶、滑动或扰动。因此需要一种能够在接触发生之前预测和拦截失败的方法。

Method: ContactGuard包含两个核心组件：(1) 一个从无标签机器人轨迹中训练得到的潜在世界模型，用于在给定规划动作的条件下预测紧凑的多视角视觉嵌入，避免像素级视频预测；(2) 一个从小规模带标签的接触前片段中训练的轻量级失败探针。部署时，ContactGuard在即将到来的接触事件之前锚定预测，使用策略自身的动作滚动前向模型，并验证预测的接触后潜表示。

Result: 在真实的接触丰富操作任务上，ContactGuard比直接预测和损坏动作的消融基线在失败预测上更准确，并且能够作为接触前中止信号迁移到实时机器人系统，同时不修改底层策略。

Conclusion: ContactGuard通过潜空间世界模型实现了接触前失败预测与拦截，证明在接触发生之前进行执行监控是可行且有效的，为接触丰富操作任务提供了一种无需修改底层策略的安全执行保障机制。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation failures are often detected only after the robot has committed to contact. This is especially limiting in wrist-camera setups: close gripper--object views help observe contact, but a poor approach may already push, miss, slip, or disturb the object before conventional detectors react. We introduce \emph{ContactGuard}, a pre-contact execution monitor for chunked visuomotor policies. Given the policy's planned action chunk, ContactGuard predicts its short-horizon consequence in latent visual space and aborts if the predicted future latent indicates likely failure. Its latent world model is trained from unlabelled robot trajectories to predict compact multi-view visual embeddings under planned actions, avoiding pixel-level video prediction. A lightweight failure probe is then trained from a small labelled set of pre-contact clips. At deployment, ContactGuard anchors prediction before an imminent contact event, rolls the model forward under the policy's own actions, and verifies the predicted post-contact latent. Across real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, ContactGuard predicts failure more accurately than direct and corrupted-action ablations, and transfers to live robot as a pre-contact abort signal without modifying the underlying policy.

</details>


### [49] [Mind the Context: Continual Learning of Socially Appropriate Robot Actions via Environmental-Social Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13448)
*Rafal Robert Karpinski,Fethiye Irmak Dogan,Nikhil Churamani,Yiming Luo,Maartje M. A. de Graaf,Davide Dell'Anna,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出显式解耦双分支（EDD）框架，用于社交机器人在多个室内环境中的域增量持续学习，通过显式分离环境知识与社交代理知识，并结合基于重放的复习机制来缓解灾难性遗忘，从而学习在不同上下文中（如客厅、会议室、办公室、走廊）判断机器人行为（如清洁、服务、开启对话）的社交适当性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交机器人需要部署在多样化的环境中，相似的环境布局在不同场景下可能对应不同的社交适当行为（例如在拥挤的家中开启对话是合适的，但在办公室会议中则是干扰性的）。由于无法预先知晓所有规范和场景，机器人需要持续学习能力。现有研究虽已探讨社交适当行为的持续学习，但未覆盖域增量设置——即机器人增量地遭遇不同环境上下文，其中环境线索（如空间开放或家具密集）和社交线索（如他人位置）共同决定行为适当性。

Method: EDD框架采用显式解耦双分支结构：一个分支负责环境相关知识，另一个分支负责社交代理相关知识，两者显式分离。同时使用基于重放的复习（replay-based rehearsal）机制来缓解学习新域时的灾难性遗忘。在多个室内域（客厅、会议室、办公室、走廊）中学习机器人行为（清洁、服务、开启对话）的适当性。

Result: 实验表明EDD在多个评估指标上优于若干最先进的基线方法；消融研究进一步评估了不同解耦策略的效果，以及对域顺序的敏感性。

Conclusion: EDD框架通过显式解耦环境与社交知识并结合重放机制，有效解决了社交机器人领域中的域增量持续学习问题，为在不同室内环境中学习社交适当行为提供了可行方案，代码已公开。

Abstract: Social robots are expected to operate across diverse environments, where similar arrangements can imply different socially appropriate actions, e.g., starting a conversation may be acceptable in a crowded home but disruptive in an office meeting. Because such norms and environments cannot all be anticipated in advance, robots require continual learning (CL) to adapt from sequential experience while retaining previously acquired knowledge. Prior work has studied CL for generating socially appropriate robot actions, but it has not addressed domain-incremental settings in which the robot incrementally encounters diverse contexts (e.g., living room, meeting room, office, hallway), where both environmental (e.g., whether the space is open or cluttered with furniture) and social cues (e.g., how people or other agents are positioned around the robot) jointly shape the appropriateness of robot actions. We address this gap with the Explicit Disentanglement Dual-Branch (EDD) framework. EDD explicitly separates environmental and social-agent related knowledge and uses replay-based rehearsal to mitigate forgetting while learning the appropriateness of robot actions (e.g., cleaning, serving, starting a conversation) across several indoor domains. Experiments show that EDD outperforms several state-of-the-art baselines, and ablation studies further evaluate different disentanglement strategies and the sensitivity to domain ordering. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Cambridge-AFAR/Mind-the-Context.git.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）正快速走向通用操作策略的部署，但当前缺乏理解这些模型内部表征的基本工具，也缺乏运行时监控手段。作者旨在利用机制可解释性的思路，探索VLA残差流中是否存在可读的内部语义信号，以支持模型监控。

Result: 任务进度信号在残差流中线性可读，且预训练骨干（未接触机器人数据）中已存在该信号；当用多提示数据训练时，单个线性探针可泛化到未见任务并在语言反事实下变化，但无法实现有效的策略引导；该探针作为简单无标签OOD检测器可检测任务停滞，性能与最先进方法相当。

Conclusion: VLA模型内部存在丰富的、线性可读的语义表征（如任务进度），读取这些信号为部署中的视觉运动策略监控提供了一条轻量、可解释的路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) are moving rapidly towards deployment as general-purpose manipulation policies, but we currently lack basic tools for understanding what these models represent internally or for monitoring them at runtime. Leveraging ideas from mechanistic interpretability, we probe the residual stream of $π_{0.5}$ and find that task progress, the normalized time remaining in a trajectory, is linearly readable from the activations. We find that this signal is present in the pretrained PaliGemma backbone prior to training on any robot-specific data. A single linear probe generalizes to unseen tasks and varies under language counterfactuals when trained on multi-prompt data, but does not enable meaningful steering of the policy. These properties make the signal directly useful for instrumenting deployed VLAs. We use the probe as a simple label-free OOD detector, which detects stalled task progress, and find it competitive with state-of-the-art methods. Our results suggest that VLAs have rich, linearly readable internal representations of semantic quantities like task progress, and that learning to read these signals offers a lightweight, interpretable path toward monitoring deployed visuomotor policies.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的水下滑翔机规划器评估依赖稀缺的实体滑翔机、操作人员、布放回收资源，且海洋条件无法为竞争算法重置，导致评估成本高且不可复现。缺乏一种无须部署、可重复、公平比较不同规划器的测试环境。

Method: 构建一个浏览器原生的数字测试场，引导用户完成从区域选择、任务定制化测深、时间/深度强迫、科学目标设定、可选任务分解、路径规划、流场输送执行、观测生成到评分的完整流程。核心创新是"计划到观测"契约——统一车辆、传感和评估假设于一个通用接口，支持人工航线、内置算法以及导入的传统或学习型规划器。通过OSSE实验（五种规划器×两个场景×三个确定性种子×60小时校准时间窗）进行评估，并使用Pyodide/WebAssembly实现GliderFlight 1.2.0的原生到浏览器源码级移植与一致性验证。

Result: 54个任务全部完成并回收且无硬性违规；规划器排名与潜水策略效应揭示了运行-科学权衡。真实公共部署提供了参考审计以界定当前运动学边界。GliderFlight 1.2.0实现原生与浏览器端行为一致。实验结果确认该操作系统在科学上可追溯、组件级合格，适合任务规模部署前的实验。

Conclusion: 该浏览器原生数字测试场通过统一契约和可复现的四维实验框架，显著降低了滑翔机规划器评估的资源门槛，建立了一条高保真多层级仿真路径，为任务规模预部署实验提供了科学的、组件可资质的验证平台。

Abstract: Repeated in-situ evaluation of ocean-glider planners requires scarce vehicles, operators, deployment and recovery resources, and ocean conditions that cannot be reset for competing algorithms. We present a guided, installation-free browser-native digital test range that transforms a selected region into a reproducible four-dimensional experiment. The system leads users from regional domain selection through mission-scoped bathymetry, time/depth forcing, science objectives, optional task decomposition, route specification, current-advected execution, observation generation, and scoring. Its primary contribution is a common plan-to-observation contract unifying vehicle, sensing, and evaluator assumptions across manual routes, transparent built-in algorithms, and imported classical or learned-planner outputs, while exported artifacts form dataset-ready records. A controlled Observing System Simulation Experiment (OSSE) evaluates five classical planners in two episodes, three deterministic seeds, and a calibrated 60-hour horizon. All 54 missions completed and recovered without hard violations, while planner rankings and dive-policy effects revealed operational-scientific tradeoffs. An authentic public deployment supplied a field-referenced audit to scope current kinematic boundaries. Separately, source-locked GliderFlight 1.2.0 achieved native-to-browser parity through Pyodide/WebAssembly, establishing a pathway for high-fidelity multi-tier simulation. The resulting operational space is scientifically traceable and component-qualified for mission-scale pre-deployment experimentation.

</details>


### [52] [HumanTracker: Towards Comprehensive and Human-Aligned Motion Tracking Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13555)
*Dairu Liu,Zekun Qi,Jiayu Zeng,Ruixi Yu,Yu Guan,Yintianrun Zhang,Xuchuan Chen,Sikai Liang,Zekai Li,Chenghuai Lin,Xinqiang Yu,Wenyao Zhang,He Wang,Li Yi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HumanTracker基准与HumanScore指标，用于使仿人运动跟踪评估与人类感知对齐并具备可扩展性。基准包含约153小时光学运动轨迹，HumanScore通过12K运动对训练，能更好预测人类偏好并揭示运动学指标遗漏的接触与稳定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿人运动跟踪评估常与人类视觉感知不一致：运动学误差按帧计算位姿差异，却忽略了人眼最关注的物理缺陷（如不稳定支撑、脚部滑步、错误触地时机等）。同时现有测试集规模小、多样性不足，难以覆盖接触密集和长期行为。

Method: 1) 构建HumanTracker基准：包含约153小时来自多名专业表演者的光学运动轨迹，组织为四大运动类别，并带文本标签以支持细粒度诊断；2) 提出HumanScore：一种偏好对齐的评估指标，基于12K运动对（含24K个动作）训练而成。

Result: 在多个代表性最先进跟踪器上，HumanScore比传统运动学指标更能预测人类偏好，并能有效揭示运动学指标常遗漏的接触（contact）与稳定性（stability）失败。

Conclusion: HumanTracker与HumanScore为仿人运动跟踪提供了在感知一致性和规模上均有提升的评估方案，弥补了传统运动学指标在物理合理性评价方面的不足。

Abstract: Humanoid motion tracking is central to teleoperation and whole-body imitation, yet evaluation often disagrees with what people perceive in videos. Kinematic errors average per-frame pose differences but miss the physical artifacts that matter most, particularly unstable support and incorrect contacts such as foot skating and mistimed touch-downs. Meanwhile, widely used test suites are small and lack the diversity needed to stress contact-rich, long-horizon behaviors. We introduce HumanTracker to make humanoid tracking evaluation both perceptually aligned and scalable. The HumanTracker benchmark contains approximately 153 hours of optical motion trajectories from multiple professional performers, organized into four motion families with text labels for fine-grained diagnosis. We further propose HumanScore, a preference-aligned metric trained on 12K motion pairs containing 24K motions. Across representative state-of-the-art trackers, HumanScore better predicts human preferences and reveals contact and stability failures that kinematic metrics often miss.

</details>

### [53] [Nilpotent orbits of sl(n,R) admit no Gibbs state](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12608)
*Jérémie Pierard de Maujouy,Guillaume Neuttiens*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究了SL(n,R)的幂零轨道上的Gibbs态存在性问题，证明了当n>2时，这些幂零轨道不承认任何Gibbs态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs态是定义在Hamiltonian G-流形上的概率分布，由Lie代数g中的开集参数化，是G作用系统下的自然平衡态。作者希望探究SL(n,R)幂零轨道上是否存在这样的Gibbs态，以理解这些几何结构的平衡态性质。

Method: 作者聚焦于SL(n,R)的幂零轨道，通过分析其几何与动力系统结构，系统地检验Gibbs态的存在性条件，最终对n>2的情形给出了否定性结论。

Result: 证明了当n>2时，SL(n,R)的幂零轨道上不存在任何Gibbs态。

Conclusion: SL(n,R)（n>2）的幂零轨道无法承载Gibbs态，这表明在这些几何结构上不存在由Lie代数参数化的自然平衡态。

Abstract: Gibbs states are probability distributions defined on Hamiltonian G-manifolds. They are parametrized by an open set of the Lie algebra g and arise as natural equilibrium states with regard to the action of G on the system. In this paper, we focus on nilpotent orbits of SL(n,R). We show that for n>2, those orbits do not admit any Gibbs state.

</details>


### [54] [Minimal Nilpotent Orbits of type G2, F4 and E8](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12985)
*Boming Jia*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文证明对于复单李代数 G_2、F_4、E_8 类型，其最小幂零轨道闭包不能表示为任何光滑拟仿射簇 X 的余切丛仿射化 (T^*X)^aff，且不需要假设同构是等变的或泊松的；同时证明了 A_1 和 A_2 类型也具有相同结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究最小幂零轨道闭包是否可视为某个光滑拟仿射簇的余切丛仿射化，旨在揭示这些几何对象在非等变、非泊松意义下的结构限制。

Method: 针对 G_2、F_4、E_8 类型的复单李代数，通过分析其最小幂零轨道闭包的几何与奇点性质，结合代数几何工具证明其不能同构于任何光滑拟仿射簇 X 的 (T^*X)^aff；类似方法推广至 A_1 和 A_2 类型。

Result: 证明了在 G_2、F_4、E_8 类型以及 A_1、A_2 类型中，最小幂零轨道闭包都不是任何光滑拟仿射簇余切丛的仿射化，且结论在不假设等变或泊松同构的情况下依然成立。

Conclusion: 最小幂零轨道闭包在相当大的李代数类型范围内（包括例外型 G_2、F_4、E_8 以及小秩经典型 A_1、A_2）都不具备余切丛仿射化的结构，这为理解幂零轨道闭包的几何分类提供了新的限制。

Abstract: Let $\mathfrak g$ be a complex simple Lie algebra of type $G_2$, $F_4$, or $E_8$. We prove that the closure $\overline{\mathcal O}_{\min}(\mathfrak g)$ of its minimal nilpotent orbit is not isomorphic to $(T^*X)^{\mathrm{aff}}$ for any smooth quasi-affine variety $X$. We do not assume that the isomorphism is equivariant or Poisson. We also prove the same statement in types $A_1$ and $A_2$.

</details>


### [55] [Absolutely flat algebras in tensor categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13137)
*Kevin Coulembier,Alexander Sherman*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究张量范畴（刚性abel幺半范畴）的ind-完备化中半单代数、artinian单代数和artinian绝对平坦代数之间的关系，证明在中等增长对称张量范畴中（若接受领域主要猜想则涵盖所有情形），这三种代数在交换代数限制下是重合的；同时给出反例说明一般情况下这些概念会分化，有别于域上代数的经典情形。附录一首次给出具有有限维态射空间但对象具有无限长度的张量范畴例子；附录二将Deligne定理推广到代数学闭域上tannakian范畴的中性化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典代数中，域上的代数理论中半单代数、artinian代数和绝对平坦代数之间存在密切关系。本文旨在将这一经典关系推广到更为广泛的张量范畴（而非仅限于域上向量空间范畴）的ind-完备化框架中，探索在这些更一般的monoidal范畴中上述代数概念之间的关联是否仍然成立。

Method: 作者在张量范畴（刚性abel幺半范畴）的ind-完备化中系统地比较三类代数：半单代数、artinian单代数和artinian绝对平坦代数。通过限制在中等增长（moderate growth）的对称张量范畴中考虑交换代数，并结合领域内主要猜想（推测涵盖所有这类范畴），证明三者重合。同时构造具体反例说明在一般的张量范畴中这些概念会分道扬镳。

Result: 主要结果：在中等增长对称张量范畴的交换代数中，半单代数、artinian单代数和artinian绝对平坦代数三者相一致（在主要猜想成立时可推广到所有对称张量范畴）。同时构造了反例表明在一般张量范畴中这三个概念彼此不同，这与域上代数的经典情形形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 论文建立了几类代数概念在张量范畴ind-完备化中的关系结构，确认在好的条件下（中等增长的对称张量范畴）经典等价关系可以保持，但也揭示了在更一般的设定下这些概念具有本质差异。附录中两个独立结果也具有重要意义：其一提供了具有有限维态射空间但有无限长度对象的张量范畴的首个例子；其二将Deligne关于tannakian范畴在代数闭域上中性化的经典定理进行了推广。

Abstract: We study the relation between semisimple algebras, artinian simple algebras, and artinian absolutely flat algebras in ind-completions of tensor categories (rigid abelian monoidal categories). Our main results show that the three types of algebras coincide, if we restrict to commutative algebras in certain (all, if we accept the main conjectures in the field) symmetric tensor categories of moderate growth. We also provide examples to show that in general these notions tend to diverge, contrary to the classical case of algebras over a field. In one appendix we give the first, to the best of our knowledge, example of a tensor category with finite-dimensional morphism spaces but objects of infinite length. In a second appendix we establish a generalisation of Deligne's theorem that tannakian categories are neutral over algebraically closed fields.

</details>

*Erlend D. Børve,Maximilian Kaipel*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文扩展了g-向量扇的定义，使其适用于Hom-有限Krull-Schmidt的0-Auslander k-线性extriangulated范畴，并证明了该扇具有由厚子范畴诱导的允许划分，从而可定义picture范畴。同时建立了由presilting对象生成的厚子范畴与τ-垂直子范畴之间的双射，统一了先前所有picture范畴和τ-丛态射范畴的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前g-向量扇和picture范畴的构造仅限于有限维代数的范畴，作者希望将这些概念推广到更一般的extriangulated范畴框架下，并统一先前分散的picture范畴与τ-cluster态射范畴的构造。

Method: 作者将g-向量扇从表示有限维代数的范畴推广到Hom-有限Krull-Schmidt的0-Auslander k-线性extriangulated范畴，以投射silting对象T为工具定义扇结构。通过厚子范畴诱导允许划分，并引入划分扇的态射概念来连接不同范畴之间的函子性关系。核心证明是通过建立厚子范畴与τ-垂直子范畴之间的双射来实现统一。

Result: 建立了厚子范畴（由包含所有投射-内射对象的presilting对象生成）与T的端射k-代数的τ-垂直子范畴之间的双射；证明了g-向量扇存在由厚子范畴诱导的允许划分；由此定义了extriangulated范畴的picture范畴，并引入了划分扇的态射框架。

Conclusion: 该构造统一了有限维代数的picture范畴和τ-丛态射范畴的所有先前构造，并提供了划分扇态射的统一框架来刻画不同代数和范畴之间的picture范畴的函子性关系。

Abstract: We extend the notion of $\mathbf{g}$-vector fan so that it is defined for a Hom-finite Krull-Schmidt 0-Auslander $k$-linear extriangulated category $\mathcal{C}$ with a projective silting object $T$. Moreover, we show that the $\mathbf{g}$-vector fan admits an admissible partition, in the sense of the second-named author, which is induced by thick subcategories. One can thus define the picture category of $\mathcal{C}$. We establish a bijection between thick subcategories of $\mathcal{C}$ generated by presilting objects containing all projective-injective objects and $τ$-perpendicular subcategories of the endomorphism $k$-algebra of $T$. This shows that our construction unifies all previous constructions of picture categories and $τ$-cluster morphism categories of finite-dimensional algebras. We introduce morphisms of partitioned fans to provide a common framework for the functorial relationships between picture categories of different algebras and categories.

</details>


### [57] [Homogeneous Weights on Semigroup Algebras of Finite Commutative Semigroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13180)

Abstract: The determinant of a finite semigroup is closely related to the Frobenius property of its corresponding semigroup algebra, and it plays a significant role in coding theory applications. In this paper, we study determinants of twisted contracted semigroup algebras over finite fields. We extend Steinberg's determinant theory to this setting by establishing a Frobenius criterion for twisted contracted semigroup algebras over finite fields. We also develop a structural theory for two-generated twisted contracted monoid algebras. In particular, we identify classes for which the determinant of the associated matrix depends only on its zero--nonzero pattern, and is therefore independent of the values of the cocycle. Consequently, it suffices to verify the determinant condition for the trivial character, from which the corresponding result for all characters follows. Under suitable assumptions, these semigroups are completely determined by a small set of defining identities. Motivated by the character-theoretic construction of homogeneous weights on finite Frobenius rings, we construct homogeneous weights for Frobenius twisted contracted semigroup algebras and investigate the non-Frobenius case. In particular, we characterize the principal ideals on which the character-average weight satisfies the averaging property of homogeneous weights and thereby extend the classical notion of homogeneous weight to a prescribed family of principal ideals.

That is strange since both items are deeply rooted in Harish-Chandra's representation theory of semisimple Lie groups.

Abstract: While the necessity of a moiré potential for fractional Chern insulators (FCIs) in rhombohedral graphene-hBN superlattices, first predicted in Ref. 1, is now grounded in experiments, a theory of its origin and importance remains at large. We present a mechanism---the moiré capacitor effect---that enhances the moiré potential by electrostatically imprinting the valence charge density onto the conduction bands. We derive the analytical form of this term and reveal its crucial role in stabilizing a parent state with Chern number $C=1$ at filling $ν=1$. We propose a parent state theory which posits that stability of the Chern insulator and flatness of its hole excitations are necessary for obtaining FCIs upon doping. We then perform multi-band exact diagonalization calculations to confirm the emergence of FCIs at $ν= 2/3$ in the presence of the moiré capacitor effect. Our FCI state is stabilized by inter-band fluctuations, unlike in the moire-free case which collapses with band-mixing. We provide the first consistent theory for this state in aligned samples and explain its absence in unaligned ones.

</details>

Abstract: We study a spin-$\frac12$ impurity coupled to the boundary of a strongly correlated spin-$s$ Takhtajan--Babujian chain, an integrable model whose low-energy physics is described by a perturbed $SU(2)_{2s}$ Wess--Zumino--Witten conformal field theory. While boundary conformal field theory determines the low-energy universality class of the weak-coupling regime, exact Bethe Ansatz methods reveal a sequence of boundary quantum phase transitions in which impurity-bound states emerge and reorganize the Hilbert space into multiple excitation towers built on distinct boundary configurations. This tower restructuring provides the organizing principle for a rich boundary phase diagram extending beyond the conventional Kondo regime. Weak antiferromagnetic coupling realizes the overscreened $2s$-channel Kondo universality class, whereas stronger couplings generate localized boundary modes and qualitatively new screening mechanisms. To describe the resulting thermodynamics, we develop a generalized thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz framework that captures the multi-tower structure across all regimes. The impurity entropy reproduces the boundary conformal field theory prediction in the overscreened Kondo regime but develops pronounced nonmonotonic temperature dependence once boundary-bound states appear, in quantitative agreement with large-scale finite-temperature matrix-product-operator simulations. Complementary dynamical calculations reveal sharp threshold features in the impurity spectral function that directly track the underlying tower structure. Together, boundary conformal field theory, exact Bethe Ansatz, generalized thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz, and tensor-network simulations provide a unified description of impurity screening, boundary-bound-state formation, and excitation-tower reconstruction in a correlated spin-$s$ chain.

Abstract: We develop a lattice characterization of fermionic 't Hooft anomalies of finite internal symmetries in (1+1)D and (2+1)D, formulated in terms of obstructions to symmetric short-range-entangled (SRE) states. We consider lattice systems formed by tensor product of onsite fermionic and bosonic Hilbert spaces, and finite internal symmetry given by a central extension $\mathbb Z_2^F\to G_f\to G_b$. We extract a hierarchy of fermionic anomaly indices for a given symmetry operator. In (1+1)D, an exact lattice symmetry is characterized by a pair of cohomological data $(n_2,ν_3)$. For $G_f=G_b\times\mathbb Z_2^F$, we show that a symmetry with trivial anomaly indices $(n_2,ν_3)$ is onsiteable and hence admits a symmetric SRE state, establishing that these indices faithfully detect the lattice anomaly. Comparing with continuum QFT, we find that exact lattice symmetries do not realize the additional $H^1(BG_b,\mathbb Z_2)$ anomaly layer in continuum QFT. In particular, for $G_b=\mathbb Z_2$, exact lattice symmetries realize only the even $\mathbb Z_4$ subgroup of the continuum $\mathbb Z_8$ classification. In (2+1)D, we identify three successive anomaly layers of cohomological data $(n_2,n_3,ν_4)$. We show that a nontrivial value of any layer obstructs symmetric SRE. For $G_f=G_b\times\mathbb Z_2^F$, it also forbids a symmetric invertible state. When the bosonic group $G_b$ is non-trivially extended by fermion parity, the lattice obstruction to SRE states does not generally coincide with the continuum 't Hooft anomaly. We explicitly construct a $\mathbb Z_4^F$ lattice symmetry in (2+1)D with nontrivial lattice anomaly index that forbids symmetric SRE states, even though its continuum anomaly is trivial. Our results highlight a mismatch between lattice and continuum anomalies and motivate a systematic study of which continuum anomalies admit exact microscopic lattice realizations.

</details>

Abstract: The gapped Kondo effect describes the screening of an $S=\frac12$ impurity spin locally coupled via an antiferromagnetic exchange interaction to a conduction-electron system exhibiting a finite hard gap. Using a combination of a Lanczos transformation and a self-consistent configuration-interaction scheme, we numerically investigate the local phase diagram. Furthermore, we show that the different phases can be characterized by several topological invariants: the conventional momentum-space Chern number of the underlying two-dimensional host system, corresponding to a Chern insulator; the B-space Chern number, defined by coupling the impurity spin to a fictitious local magnetic field $\boldsymbol B$, in the limit $B \to 0$; and the S-space Chern number, defined for a classical impurity spin, i.e., a vector of fixed length. The classical-spin limit is obtained for $B \to \infty$. By varying the field strength, we can therefore continuously interpolate between quantum-impurity-spin and classical-impurity-spin Hamiltonians and investigate whether the corresponding phase diagrams are likewise continuously connected. The gapped underscreened Kondo effect is studied for an impurity spin $S>\frac12$ as well as in the classical-spin limit approached via $B \to \infty$ or $S \to \infty$. Different variants of scattering theory are employed to interpret the resulting phases. Finally, the gapped two-channel overscreened Kondo effect, realized by coupling a quantum impurity spin equally to the local electron spins of both orbitals within a unit cell, is shown to be characterized by spontaneous particle-hole symmetry breaking. This leads to a highly nontrivial quantum-classical phase diagram.

We then validate the most promising formulations in self-consistent equilibrium and nonequilibrium DMFT calculations and demonstrate calculations up to the third order in the strong-coupling expansion. Finally, we extend the solver to impurity models with retarded density-density interactions and apply it within nonequilibrium extended DMFT. Our results show that tensor cross interpolation substantially reduces the cost of evaluating higher-order diagrams and provides a controlled, systematically improvable framework for nonequilibrium quantum impurity calculations.

Abstract: Combined with numerical analytic continuation techniques, quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) methods enable the extraction of real-frequency dynamical properties from imaginary-time correlation functions. For off-diagonal operators, we derive estimators that integrate directly into the existing SSE directed-loop or cluster updating schemes, introducing only minimal computational overhead. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的变化PDE识别方法通常采用有限元逼近来表示变化系数，但这类方法可能计算量大或对局部扰动敏感。作者希望探索一种更高效的局部方法（Local WeakIdent），并在此基础上克服其局部扰动敏感性问题，同时融入全局信息以提高识别稳定性与准确性。

Method: SLW-Ident框架包含三个主要步骤：(1) 在整个给定域中采样多个局部补丁；(2) 对每个采样补丁分别进行局部方程识别（基于局部弱形式，即Local WeakIdent）；(3) 利用这些识别方程在全局残差上的误差来确定不同方程之间的转换/过渡区域。其中，被识别方程支撑集不变的区域定义为一个"单一方程区域"，在每个区域内选择出现频率最高的方程作为该区域的识别结果，并可进一步识别常系数及变系数的PDE。该方法还通过不确定性量化理论建立了主导支撑集选择的统计误差与补丁数量之间的关系。

Result: 数值实验表明，SLW-Ident能够准确恢复"单一方程区域"的边界，并能在含噪声数据的条件下正确识别变化PDE（包括变系数情形）的控制方程。

Conclusion: SLW-Ident通过局部采样与全局残差验证相结合的方式，提供了一种稳定、计算高效且具有理论保障的变化PDE识别方法，可适用于含噪声的数据场景。

Abstract: We propose Sampled Local WeakIdent (SLW-Ident), a framework for identifying changing governing equations from a single set of given data. Different from a typical approach of using finite element based approximation to represent varying coefficients, this paper explores a local approach in identification of differential equations. First, we present the power of Local WeakIdent which gives good local identification and is also computationally efficient with a small patch size, yet it can be sensitive to local perturbations. We propose SLW-Ident which stabilizes the identification process and also incorporates global information: we first sample patches in the whole given domain, identify equations for each sampled patch, then use residual error of these equations to find the transitions between different equations. We refer to a region where the support of the identified equation does not change to be a region of one equation. Within each region of one equation, we pick the most frequently identified equation as the identified equation, and find constant as well as varying coefficient PDEs within each region of one equation. This is justified by an uncertainty quantification theory that gives the relation between statistical error of dominant support selection and the number of patches. We provide various numerical experiments showing that SLW-Ident accurately recovers the regions of one equation and the governing equations for changing PDEs with varying coefficients even with noisy given data.

</details>

Abstract: The homogeneous Dirichlet boundary value problem captures the core difficulty of solving Dirichlet problems with non-interpolatory methods. We propose BTF-PINN (Boundary-Training-Free Physics-Informed Neural Network), an interior-only strategy for solving homogeneous Dirichlet boundary value problems that requires no boundary training, boundary penalties, or boundary-conforming parametrizations. The key idea is to embed the essential boundary condition into a newly designed boundary-free loss function. We prove the equivalence between the proposed interior-only variational formulation and the original boundary value problem, establish a sharp threshold condition for the residual weight, and develop a convergence analysis based on the coercivity of the functional. Numerical experiments on high-dimensional problems, irregular geometries, and anisotropic elliptic equations demonstrate the effectiveness of BTF-PINN. Comparisons with standard boundary-penalty PINNs further show that BTF-PINN achieves superior boundary trace accuracy.

</details>

  also studied. The analysis is set in a framework based on analytic

Abstract: The classical Strang projector-splitting integrator is widely observed to converge with order two, whereas the error analysis that remains uniform as the smallest singular value retained in the low-rank approximation tends to zero proves only order one. We show that this gap is intrinsic under the standard assumptions. We construct $3\times3$ matrix differential equations that are $C^2$ in time and smooth in the matrix variable, with rank-two initial data that satisfy uniform boundedness, Lipschitz, tangency-defect, and regularity bounds. Nevertheless, the exact-subflow Strang method has a nonzero $h^2$ term in the local error over one periodic forcing cycle consisting of four Strang steps. Repetition of that cycle rules out a global second-order bound whose constant and stepsize threshold are independent of the retained singular values. The mechanism is a rapid rotation of the factor directions associated with the small singular value: first-order consistency is preserved, but the changing projection spaces prevent the cancellation normally expected from a symmetric Strang composition. Hence the robust first-order result cannot, under these assumptions alone, be upgraded to robust second order for the classical projector-splitting method.

</details>

Abstract: Neural ODE reduced-order models often achieve comparable local prediction accuracy, yet their long-horizon extrapolation behavior can differ substantially. To analyze this discrepancy, we develop a path-integral identity that separates local discrepancy injection from amplification in the learned latent dynamics. The associated multi-step Jacobian norms quantify transport sensitivity and distinguish different propagation regimes. Experiments on the Burgers and Gray--Scott systems exhibit two distinct patterns of error evolution. In Burgers systems, prediction errors remain bounded and are primarily associated with persistent local discrepancies. In contrast, Gray--Scott systems exhibit pronounced amplification during extrapolation, where Jacobian norms serve as sensitivity diagnostics rather than direct indicators of physical prediction accuracy.

Abstract: Using arbitrary-precision arithmetic provided by the GNU Multiple Precision Floating-Point Reliable Library, we implement AQE11D---that is, Ninomiya's adaptive 9-point Newton--Cotes rule extended with a sequence of higher-order rules---and Takahasi and Moris' double exponential (DE) formula. We evaluate them for Kahaner's 21 test problems. For both absolute tolerances $10^{-50}$ and $10^{-100}$, AQE11D attains target accuracy on all 21 problems; however, for strong endpoint singularity such as $1/\sqrt{x}$, it requires about $5.4\times10^{7}$ function evaluations at $10^{-100}$, roughly $7\times10^{4}$ times as many as the DE formula. The formula converges on 18 problems at both tolerances, demonstrating its strength against endpoint singularities but also its failure, as it stands, on problems with a singularity inside the integration interval.

Abstract: We study the stochastic resistive Hall--magnetohydrodynamic (Hall--MHD) system on bounded convex polyhedral domains, subject to nonlinear perfectly conducting boundary conditions. The system is driven by multiplicative Gaussian forcing in the momentum equation together with structured curl-type noise in the induction equation arising from stochastic perturbations of the resistive and Hall parts in the generalised Ohm law. We develop a fully discrete, linearly implicit, structure-preserving mixed finite element method based on a compatible discrete de Rham complex, which preserves the magnetic Gauss law exactly. We prove subsequential convergence of the discrete solutions to a finite-energy weak martingale solution, thereby obtaining a constructive existence result for the stochastic Hall--MHD system with curl-type noise. Numerical experiments illustrate the stochastic dynamics, exact divergence preservation, and convergence of the method.

*Hadi Hajieghrary,Benedikt Walter,Chaitanya Shinde,Miguel Hurtadoand Jerry Lopez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动驾驶规则手册中 lexicographic 规则排序在模型预测控制中的实现问题，指出常用的加权和启发式（w1≫w2≫…≫wL）回答了一个错误的问题。作者证明了在凸优先级排序问题中，加权和能精确复现 lexicographic 最优解的充要条件是其权重（在加上单位性能系数后）支持 achievement map 在 lexicographic 点处的上图像，可容许权重构成单位性能切片的外法锥。在铰链惩罚下该切片是投影缩放 KKT 系统得到的多面体，线性规划可返回带认证裕度的内部权重；而在平方铰链惩罚下，当极限乘子非零时任何有限权重都无法精确，违反沿局部极小分支以 O(1/w) 衰减。在 nuPlan 闭环实验（25条规则）中，标定权重在十一个场景类中九个具有近似相等的层级分量，合法层级事件精度约为匹配启发式权重的两倍。然而该证书是点态的：没有任何单一权重在整个 episode 的所有采样 tick 上有效，中位寿命仅为一个采样间隔，持续性取决于活动集稳定性。这促使采用选择性级联回退的受监控加权求解方案，但合规模式监控器只能检测到部分测得的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶规则手册按字典序排列规则违规，MPC 通过加权和方法近似实现这一排序。现有启发式调参（w1≫w2≫…≫wL）缺乏理论基础，作者旨在严格刻画加权和精确复现 lexicographic 最优解的条件，并揭示启发式方法的根本缺陷。

Method: 利用凸分析中的上图像与法锥理论，建立加权和精确等价 lexicographic 最优的充要条件（单位性能切片的外法锥）；对铰链与平方铰链惩罚分别推导精确权重存在性的完整刻画（多面体投影/KTT 系统与极限乘子条件）；通过线性规划求解带认证裕度的内部权重；在 nuPlan 闭环实验和持有日志上进行标定与评估，并分析权重的点态有效性。

Result: 单位性能切片的外法锥给出精确权重集合的几何刻画；铰链惩罚下可算 LP 求内点权重；平方铰链下当极限乘子非零时无有限精确权重，违反按 O(1/w) 衰减。nuPlan 实验中九/十一个场景类权重近等差级，合法层级事件精度约为启发式两倍。但证书点态有效：中位权重寿命仅一个采样间隔，持久性受活动集稳定性制约，监控器仅覆盖部分失效。

Conclusion: 加权和启发式在理论上是可精确实现的（铰链惩罚下），但实际中精确权重仅点态有效、缺乏跨 tick 泛化性，不能作为独立的长期策略。作者主张采用受监控的加权求解加选择性级联回退机制，同时承认现有合规监控器覆盖不全，需要进一步改进监控指标。

Abstract: Automated-driving rulebooks rank rule violations lexicographically, and model predictive control enforces that ranking either exactly, through $L{+}1$ sequential programs per tick, or approximately, through a weighted sum tuned by the separation heuristic $w_1\gg w_2\gg\cdots\gg w_L$. We show the heuristic answers the wrong question. For a convex priority-ordered program, a weighted sum reproduces the lexicographic optimum precisely when its weight, augmented by a unit performance coefficient, supports the upper image of the achievement map at the lexicographic point; the admissible weights form the unit-performance slice of an outward normal cone. Under hinge penalties this slice is a polyhedron obtained by projecting a scaled-KKT system, and a linear program returns an interior weight with a certified margin; under squared-hinge penalties no finite weight is exact whenever the limiting multiplier is nonzero, with violation along the local minimizer branch decaying as $O(1/w)$. Calibrated on held-out logs, the resulting weights have near-equal tier components in nine of eleven calibration-eligible scenario classes and roughly double legal-tier event precision against a matched heuristic weight in closed-loop nuPlan experiments on a 25-rule rulebook. The certificate is, however, pointwise: no single weight is valid across the sampled ticks of an episode, the median lifetime is one sampling interval (zero subsequent ticks at the native rate), and persistence tracks active-set stability. These findings motivate monitored weighted solves with selective cascade fallback, although the compliance-pattern monitor detects only a subset of measured lapses.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管储热在区域供热网络中的益处已被广泛认可，但由于供热网络在热源、生产和消费模式、网络热损失及燃料成本等方面的高度多样性，确定最优储热规模仍然具有挑战性。

Conclusion: 提出的基于物理的整体优化方法优于常用的简化储热规模确定方法，能够确保可行设计、准确成本评估和最优储热规模确定，体现了该方法在多样化DHN系统中应用的通用性和优势。

Abstract: Integrating heat storages in district heating networks (DHNs) supports managing dynamic characteristics such as heat-demand variations and changing energy prices, and the intermittency of renewable sources. A common application are DHNs with combined heat and power (CHP) units, where a storage allows shifting heat extraction to periods with favorable electricity prices. Although the benefits of heat storage in DHNs are well established, determining the optimal storage size remains challenging due to the diversity of DHNs in terms of heat sources, production and consumption patterns, network heat losses, and fuel costs. This paper presents a scalable, automated methodology for optimizing short-term heat storage in DHNs using mathematical optimization. The nonlinear, physics-based approach models the DHN, heat producers, and storages simultaneously to minimize total economic cost through optimal storage sizing, explicitly considering time-varying heat-production costs, heat demands, and heat losses. The methodology is demonstrated on a 3rd-generation CHP-DHN in Belgium with 30 consumers, exceeding the scale of previous physics-based storage-sizing studies. For this system, optimal storage integration reduces the 20-year cost by 730 k EUR (16.5%), from 4.41 M EUR to 3.68 M EUR. The reduction results from a 1.07 M EUR decrease in heat-production cost achieved by shifting heat extraction to periods of low electricity prices, while the storage investment amounts to 323 k EUR. A comparison to a commonly used simplified storage sizing method, which fails to identify the optimal storage size, demonstrates the advantage of the proposed holistic, physics-based optimization approach in ensuring feasible designs, accurate cost assessments, and optimal storage sizing.

</details>


### [84] [A Local-Linearly Convergent Algorithm for Nonconvex Equality-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12665)
*Frank E. Curtis,Lingjun Guo,Daniel P. Robinson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Gradient-Eigenstep算法的分析，证明其在强二阶驻点附近以足够小的步长参数和足够大的惩罚参数初始启动时可获得局部线性收敛速率；并展示了该算法可作为渐进采样策略下等式约束优化问题的迭代高效子问题求解器，相比直接求解全样本问题具有更优的最坏情况样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goyens等人提出的Gradient-Eigenstep算法是求解非凸等式约束优化问题的高效迭代方法，但该算法缺乏局部收敛速率分析，且尚未被充分利用于大规模样本均值定义的优化问题中，需要进一步的理论分析和应用拓展。

Method: 本文通过两个核心结果进行分析：(1) 证明当算法从强二阶驻点附近初始化时，在充分小的步长参数和充分大的惩罚参数条件下，算法退化为对Fletcher增广Lagrangian函数应用梯度下降，并建立局部线性收敛速率；(2) 将Gradient-Eigenstep算法嵌入渐进采样策略框架中，作为子问题求解器，将第一个结果用于保证子问题求解的收敛性，从而改善最坏情况样本复杂度。

Result: 建立了Gradient-Eigenstep算法在强二阶驻点附近的局部线性收敛速率；证明了该算法在渐进采样策略下作为子问题求解器的有效性，获得了比直接求解全样本问题更优的最坏情况样本复杂度。

Conclusion: Gradient-Eigenstep算法不仅自身具有局部线性收敛保证，而且在渐进采样框架下展现了显著的样本效率优势，为大规模等式约束优化提供了一种迭代高效且样本高效的求解方案。

Abstract: For solving nonconvex equality-constrained optimization problems, a recent Gradient-Eigenstep Algorithm by Goyens et al.~is an iteration-efficient approach, based on minimizing Fletcher's augmented Lagrangian function, for finding an approximate second-order stationary point from an arbitrary starting point. In this paper, the analysis of this algorithm is extended, offering a two-fold contribution. First, it is shown that a local-linear rate of convergence can be obtained by this method if it is initiated sufficiently close to a strong second-order stationary point and employs a sufficiently small step-size parameter and sufficiently large penalty parameter. In this case, the algorithm reduces to a gradient descent algorithm applied to minimize Fletcher's augmented Lagrangian. Second, as a particularly useful application of the first result, it is shown that the Gradient-Eigenstep algorithm can be used as an iteration-efficient subproblem solver in the context of a progressive sampling strategy for solving equality-constrained optimization problems when the objective and constraint functions are defined by large sample averages, ultimately offering an algorithm with an improved worst-case sample complexity when compared to an approach that solves a full-sample problem directly.

</details>


### [85] [Stochastic Mixed-Integer Optimization of Dynamic Electricity Tariffs with Consumer Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12688)
*Ananya Kale,Mohit Apte,Chhaya Gosavi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究日前住宅电价设计中的"保护代价"问题：在要求电价收入接近固定电价基准并限制用户账单涨幅的约束下，峰值削减会损失多少。基于5,566户伦敦低碳家庭共1.678亿条半小时验证读数，通过准实验估计价格响应，用bootstrap需求情景求解随机混合整数规划。结果显示，在全部73个合格测试日上，分段保护随机电价平均削减2.29%的模拟峰值（95%置信区间[2.11,2.48]），收入变化-2.26%，最差分段账单涨幅2.48%。移除分段账单上限只将峰值削减提高到2.37%，但最差分段账单涨幅升至6.81%——平均保护代价很小但尾部风险显著；家庭95分位账单涨幅达8.61%（CVaR95为14.76%），说明分段平均上限不能约束家庭尾部。代表性家庭（尾部感知）变体可将家庭p95从8.61%降至7.05%，而平均峰值削减仅从2.29%微降至2.28%。作者报告了完整的保护代价前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日前居民电价必须在需求观测前公布，激进的定价虽能在模拟中削减峰值，但可能抬高用户账单。文章旨在量化在要求收入接近固定电价基准并限制账单涨幅时，峰值削减效果会损失多少。

Method: 利用5,566户Low Carbon London家庭（1.678亿条经验证的半小时读数）数据，对照固定电价对照组进行准实验价格响应估计；构建bootstrap需求情景；使用HiGHS求解随机混合整数规划问题；比较分段保护与代表性家庭（尾部感知）两种电价设计变体；在73个合格留出测试日上评估。

Result: 分段保护随机电价在73个测试日平均削减模拟峰值2.29%（95% CI [2.11, 2.48]），收入变化-2.26%，最差分段账单涨幅2.48%。移除分段账单上限仅将峰值削减提至2.37%，但最差分段账单涨幅升至6.81%。家庭95分位账单涨幅为8.61%（CVaR95为14.76%）。代表性家庭变体将家庭p95从8.61%降至7.05%，平均峰值削减仅从2.29%变为2.28%。

Conclusion: 在本样本中，强平均保护对峰值削减性能的损失很小，但分段平均账单上限无法约束家庭层面的尾部风险；尾部感知的代表性家庭约束能更有效地控制家庭极端账单涨幅，且几乎不牺牲峰值削减性能。所有优化结果均为基于模型的、在可选试验情境下的反事实估算，由于缺乏批发成本数据，未对利润进行优化。

Abstract: Day-ahead residential tariffs must be posted before demand is observed. Aggressive high prices can cut peaks in simulation, but they can also raise customer bills. This paper measures how much peak reduction is lost when a tariff design problem is required to keep revenue near a flat-tariff baseline and to limit bill increases. Using half-hourly data from 5,566 Low Carbon London households (167.8 million validated readings), we estimate quasi-experimental price response against the standard-tariff comparison group, form bootstrap demand scenarios, and solve stochastic mixed-integer programs with HiGHS. On all 73 eligible held-out test days, a segment-protected stochastic tariff reduces simulated peak demand by 2.29% on average (95% day-bootstrap CI [2.11, 2.48]), with revenue change -2.26% and mean worst-segment bill increase 2.48%. Removing the segment bill cap raises peak reduction only to 2.37%, while the worst segment bill increase rises to 6.81%: in this sample, substantial average protection costs little peak-shaving performance. The same schedules leave a household 95th-percentile bill increase of 8.61% (CVaR95 14.76%), so segment-average caps do not bound household tails. We report the full price-of-protection frontier under consistent household simulation and compare segment protection with a representative-household (tail-aware) variant: the latter cuts household p95 from 8.61% to 7.05% while changing mean peak reduction only from 2.29% to 2.28%. All optimized outcomes are model-based counterfactuals under an opt-in trial; wholesale costs are unavailable, so we do not optimize profit.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优反馈控制算法中的性能指标包含多个自由参数（状态惩罚、控制惩罚和终端状态惩罚矩阵），传统上需要通过反复实验与迭代来选择这些权值矩阵。然而，用于优化权值矩阵的过程通常需要多次数值积分，计算成本高昂，亟需更高效的方法。

Method: 该方法利用时变Riccati矩阵、状态轨迹、状态转移矩阵及最优性能指标的闭式解（closed-form solutions）来替代数值积分；所有偏导数计算通过解析代数方程完成，并据此生成用于优化步骤的解析梯度。优化策略以最小化反馈控制问题的终端状态值为目标。

Result: 给出了数值算例，验证了所提出的优化算法的有效性。所提出的计算流程显著降低了计算开销，相比传统需要多次数值积分的方法更具效率。

Conclusion: 该优化流程有望在科学与工程中的控制理论应用中被广泛使用，为最优反馈控制器的权值矩阵选择提供了一种高效的解析化解决方案。

Abstract: The necessary conditions for formulating optimal feedback control algorithms have been known for many years. Free parameters exist in the performance index in the form of state and control penalty and terminal state penalty matrices for tuning the performance of the optimally controlled system. The selection process is typically experimental and iterative. To generate the weight matrices for optimal feedback control algorithms, an optimization process is proposed. Typically, the optimization process for the weight matrices requires several numerical integration processes that are computationally expensive; this work overcomes the classical high computational cost by exploiting closed-form solutions for the time-varying Riccati matrix, state trajectories, state transition matrix, and the optimal performance index. Closed-form algebraic equations are used to generate all partial derivative calculations, and no numerical integration is required. The closed-form partial derivatives are used to generate analytic gradients for the optimization steps. The optimization strategy seeks to minimize the terminal state values for the feedback control problem. A numerical example is presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed optimization algorithm. The resulting computational procedures are expected to be broadly useful for control theory applications in science and engineering.

</details>


### [87] [Efficient Hessian-Free Methods for Multi-Objective Bilevel Optimization with Nonconvex Lower Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12704)
*Yicong Jiang,Feihu Huang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出MOMEHA算法族，解决下层级为非凸的多目标双层优化问题。通过Moreau包络将原问题转化为带包络约束的多目标单层优化，结合光滑加权Tchebycheff标量化实现单循环且免Hessian计算，并提出动量变体MB-MOMEHA处理随机场景，在理论收敛性和few-shot元学习、神经架构搜索实验中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标双层优化在AI领域（如自动学习、多任务元学习）有广泛应用，但现有方法都依赖下层级问题为（强）凸的假设。实际上这些多目标双层学习问题通常是非凸的，尤其是下层级问题往往也非凸，现有方法无法处理这一情况，存在空白亟待填补。

Method: 1) 利用Moreau包络将原始多目标双层优化问题转化为带包络约束的多目标单层优化；2) 引入光滑加权Tchebycheff标量化，使得算法在保持单循环结构的同时无需计算Hessian矩阵；3) 提出动量变体MB-MOMEHA用于处理随机多目标双层学习问题。

Result: 在确定性和随机两种设定下均给出了算法的收敛性保证。在few-shot元学习和神经架构搜索实验中，MOMEHA和MB-MOMEHA在Pareto前沿上均优于现有方法，验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MOMEHA系列算法首次解决了下层级非凸的多目标双层优化问题，兼具单循环和免Hessian的计算优势，且理论与实验双方面验证了其优越性，为该领域提供了更通用的求解框架。

Abstract: Multi-objective bilevel optimization has wide applications in the AI area such as automated learning and multi-task meta-learning. Although recently some works have been begun to study the multi-objective bilevel optimization, the proposed methods rely on the (strongly) convex lower level problems. In fact, these multi-objective bilevel learning problems are generally nonconvex, and particularly their lower level problems are nonconvex. To fill this gap, we propose a class of Multi-Objective Moreau Envelope based Hessian-free Algorithms (MOMEHA) to solve the multi-objective bilevel learning problems with nonconvex lower level. Specifically, our method uses the Moreau envelope to convert the original problem into a multi-objective single-level optimization with an envelope constraint. In particular, our method retains computational advantages of being single-loop and Hessian-free in the multi-objective setting by incorporating a smooth weighted Tchebycheff scalarization. Furthermore, we propose a momentum-based variant of MOMEHA (i.e., MB-MOMEHA) method to solve the stochastic multi-objective bilevel learning problems. In theory, we provide the convergence properties of our algorithms under both deterministic and stochastic setting. Some experiments on few-shot meta-learning and neural architecture search demonstrate that our methods outperform the existing approaches in Pareto front, validating its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>


### [88] [Shape Design for Degenerate Hyperbolic Equation with Degenerate Boundary and Its Application to Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12754)

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类退化双曲型方程的可观性与可控性问题，其控制区域与退化集相交。通过引入基于形状设计的近似方法，将退化方程逼近为一族一致双曲方程，利用谱近似、弱解估计和乘子法，建立了对近似参数一致的可观性不等式，并通过极限过程获得了原退化方程的可观性不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于退化双曲型方程的可控性/可观性研究主要处理控制区域与退化集分离的情形，而控制区域与退化集相交的情况更为困难且尚未被充分研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出一种基于形状设计的近似方法，将退化方程通过一族一致双曲方程来逼近；证明依赖谱近似、弱解的精确估计以及乘子法。首先建立逼近方程的可观性不等式（常数与逼近参数无关），然后通过退化极限过程得到原退化方程的可观性不等式。

Result: 获得了原退化双曲方程在控制区域与退化集相交情形下的可观性不等式，进而推出相应的可控性结果。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将可观性/可控性分析扩展到了控制区域与退化集相交的更一般情形，所提出的形状设计近似方法为处理退化方程提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the observability and controllability of a class of degenerate hyperbolic equations with a control region intersecting the degenerate set. Unlike the existing results that mainly deal with control regions separated from the degeneracy, we consider the case where the control region reaches the degenerate part. To handle the difficulty caused by the degeneracy, we introduce a shape-design-based approximation method based on shape design by approximating the degenerate equation with a family of uniformly hyperbolic equations. The proof relies on the spectral approximation of the associated operators, precise estimates for weak solutions, and the multiplier method. We first establish observability inequalities for the approximating equations with constants independent of the approximation parameter. Then, by passing to the degenerate limit, we obtain the observability inequality for the original degenerate equation.

</details>


### [89] [Adaptive Schauder Stochastic Mirror Descent in Banach Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13121)
*Jinhui Bai,Shuai Lu,Lei Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文将近端随机镜像下降（SMD）扩展到无穷维Banach空间，用于求解一类仅通过采样获取随机梯度信息的风险泛函最小化问题，提出了基于Schauder基的自适应有限维子空间正则化策略，实现了O(n^{-1/p_1})的收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将随机优化算法从有限维扩展至无穷维Banach空间是理论优化与统计学习交叉领域的重要问题。现有SMD方法主要针对欧几里得空间或有限维空间设计，难以直接处理抽象Banach空间中的风险泛函最小化问题，尤其是在非均匀凸空间和仅有采样梯度信息的设定下缺乏有效的算法框架与收敛性分析。

Method: 首先依据Banach空间的均匀凸性选择Bregman距离；对于非均匀凸空间L_{ρ_X}^1(Ω)，构造由熵函数诱导的Bregman距离。基于Schauder基引入一族适应样本量n的有限维子空间，在每次SMD迭代中将子问题限制到对应的有限维子空间，并将随机梯度投影到有限维对偶空间，形成新的正则化策略，显式求解子问题并实现偏差-方差权衡。

Result: 算法时间复杂度为O(n^{1+θ})，空间复杂度为O(n^θ)，其中θ>0在最小化器具有充分正则性时可取任意小。在命题失配（misspecified）设定下即最小化器仅满足较弱正则性条件时，算法仍收敛。收敛率达到O(n^{-1/p_1})（含对数因子），其中p_1≥2由底层空间的凸性决定。算法进一步扩展到逆问题，并通过数值实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为无穷维Banach空间上的风险泛函最小化提供了计算可行且理论完备的随机优化框架，通过有限维子空间正则化实现了计算效率与统计精度之间的平衡，并推广至逆问题领域，具有重要的理论价值和实际应用前景。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend stochastic mirror descent (SMD) to infinite-dimensional Banach spaces for solving a class of risk functional minimization problems, where stochastic gradient information is only available through sampling. We first choose the Bregman distance according to the uniform convexity properties of the Banach space. For the non-uniformly convex space $\mathcal{L}_{ρ_X}^1(Ω)$, we instead construct a Bregman distance induced by the entropy function. Based on a Schauder basis of the Banach space, we introduce a family of finite-dimensional subspaces that adapt to the sample size $n$. At each SMD iteration, we restrict the subproblem to the corresponding finite-dimensional subspace and project the stochastic gradient onto the associated finite-dimensional dual space, thereby introducing a new regularization strategy. This regularization strategy allows us to explicitly solve the SMD subproblem efficiently and to achieve a bias--variance trade-off. The algorithm requires $\mathcal{O}(n^{1+θ})$ time and $\mathcal{O}(n^θ)$ memory, where $θ>0$ can be chosen arbitrarily small when the minimizer has sufficient regularity. By developing a new analytical framework, we prove that the proposed algorithm achieves a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}\left(n^{-1/p_1}\right)$ up to logarithmic factors, where $p_1\geq2$ is determined by the convexity properties of the underlying space. In the misspecified setting where the minimizer satisfies only weaker regularity conditions, we show that the proposed algorithm still converges to the minimum. We further extend the algorithm to inverse problems and validate its effectiveness in numerical experiments.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉普拉斯正则化最小化是信号处理和机器学习中的基础工具，但图拉普拉斯伪逆是稠密且病态的（尽管拉普拉斯矩阵本身是稀疏的），导致直接计算在大规模下不切实际。此外，伪逆学习比拉普拉斯学习更具挑战性。

Method: 在给定图拉普拉斯的前提下，提出DCR图学习框架，通过正则化最大似然估计（MLE）近似拉普拉斯伪逆的谱作用。利用拉普拉斯正则化非负最小二乘（LR-NNLS）的对偶表示，将伪逆学习与实例级推断解耦，并通过可微的对偶引导学习方案进行高效的原始解重构。

Result: 建立了DCR算法的稳定性和唯一不动点存在的理论保证。数值实验表明，该方法在性能上优于凸优化求解器和图滤波基线，并且在多种图拓扑下均表现稳健。

Conclusion: DCR框架为大规模图拉普拉斯伪逆学习提供了一种无需直接求逆的高效替代方案，同时具备理论保障和良好的经验性能。

Abstract: Laplacian-regularized minimization is fundamental in signal processing and machine learning, but is limited by the dense and ill-conditioned nature of the graph Laplacian pseudoinverse. While the Laplacian itself is sparse, its pseudoinverse is dense and often ill-conditioned, rendering direct computation impractical at scale. Moreover, pseudoinverse learning is more challenging than Laplacian learning. To address this challenge, this paper considers the setting where the graph Laplacian is given and proposes a Difference-of-Convex Regularizer (DCR) graph learning framework that approximates the spectral action of the Laplacian pseudoinverse without direct inversion via regularized Maximum Likelihood Estimation (MLE). By reformulating Laplacian-Regularized Nonnegative Least Squares (LR-NNLS) through a dual representation, DCR decouples pseudoinverse learning from instance-specific inference and enables efficient primal solution reconstruction via a differentiable dual-guided learning scheme. We establish theoretical guarantees on stability and the existence of a unique fixed point for DCR algorithm. Numerical experiments demonstrate improved performance over convex solvers and graph filtering baselines and robust performance across diverse graph topologies.

</details>


### [91] [A penalty-type method for relaxed inverse optimal control problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12769)
*Floriane Mefo Kue,Patrick Mehlitz,Thorsten Raasch*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种用于求解逆最优控制中一类双层优化问题的罚类型方法，通过对松弛值函数约束进行惩罚、解耦上下层变量更新，构建替代问题序列以计算相关驻点，算法无需线搜索、仅需单次下层评估且罚参数无需趋于无穷，在合理假设下获得子序列收敛结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆最优控制问题可建模为双层优化问题，但这类问题通常难以数值求解。现有方法往往需要线搜索、多次下层问题求解或强制罚参数趋于无穷，这些要求在计算上代价高昂或理论上受限。作者旨在设计一种更高效、更具实用性的求解框架。

Method: 提出一种罚类型方法：将松弛值函数约束作为惩罚项纳入目标函数，构造一系列替代问题；上下层决策变量在更新时解耦处理；罚参数仅在迭代未带来足够可行性改善时增大，无需线搜索，下层问题每轮仅评估一次。

Result: 在合理假设下建立了子序列收敛性结果，证明算法可收敛到松弛值函数重构问题的驻点。数值实验中同时驱动松弛参数趋于零，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法因避免线搜索、仅需单次下层评估、罚参数不必趋于无穷而具有显著计算优势，同时仍保持收敛性保证，为逆最优控制问题的数值求解提供了务实高效的途径。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the introduction and analysis
  of a penalty-type method for the numerical treatment of
  a class of bilevel optimization problems arising from inverse optimal control.
  The algorithm is designed to compute stationary points of the associated relaxed value function reformulation.
  This is achieved by determining a sequence of stationary points associated with a sequence of surrogate problems
  where the relaxed value function constraint is penalized,
  where the updates of upper- and lower-level decision variables are decoupled,
  and where the penalty parameter is enlarged only in those iterations
  which do not come along with a sufficient improvement of some feasibility measure.
  The resulting method does not comprise any linesearch, the lower-level problem has to be evaluated just once per iteration,
  and the penalty parameter does not need to be driven to infinity.
  Nevertheless, subsequential convergence results are obtained under reasonable assumptions.
  Numerical experiments, where the relaxation parameter is also driven to zero, visualize effectiveness of the approach.

</details>

Result: 建立了近似Stackelberg均衡的存在性理论结果，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟进行了敏感性分析。

Conclusion: 该模型为全球碳排放治理提供了兼顾层级决策、异质主体互动与硬性排放约束的理论分析框架，为政策制定中价格调控与强制减排手段的有效性提供了量化依据。

Abstract: Global warming, driven by anthropogenic carbon emissions with transboundary pollution characteristics and irreversible damage, poses an existential threat to human society. This paper develops a novel two-level Stackelberg game with mean field interaction of controls and common noise which integrates hierarchical decision-making under a state-reflected emission dynamics. A central regulator (leader) adjusts product prices to guide $n$ heterogeneous competing regions (followers) while enforcing a hard emission cap via a reflection mechanism that models emergency reductions through a local time process. We establish the existence of an approximate Stackelberg equilibrium and perform sensitivity analysis via Monte Carlo simulations.

Abstract: We consider a countably infinite collection of linear, scalar control systems, where each system is represented by the pair $(a_n,b_n)$ with $a_n>0$ for $n\in \mathbb{N}$, and all systems are forced by a common scalar control input. We refer to this collection of systems as a discrete linear ensemble system. The ensemble system is feedback stabilizable if there exists a common feedback control input that asymptotically stabilizes every system simultaneously. Unlike finite-dimensional linear systems, an infinite-dimensional linear system is not guaranteed to be stable if its poles lie in the open left half-plane. Stability is guaranteed, however, if (i) its poles are contained in the closed left half-plane and (ii) its infinitesimal generator is diagonalizable. In this paper, we provide necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of a static, linear feedback control law that ensures the closed-loop ensemble system satisfies (i) and (ii). In particular, we show that the exponential decay of the sequences $(|b_n|)_{n\in \mathbb{N}}$ and $(a_n/|b_n|)_{n\in \mathbb{N}}$ is necessary and, under an assumption on the desired poles, sufficient.

</details>

*Víctor Hernández-Santamaría*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文证明了二维和三维Boussinesq系统在使用减少数量局部控制（温度方程上施加控制，速度方程仅N-2个分量受控）时的局部零能控性，基于Lebeau-Robbiano方法的谱途径，通过混合可观测性估计和级联结构克服了耦合系统缺乏公共谱分解的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Boussinesq系统是描述流体热对流的经典模型，研究其能控性具有重要理论意义。以往结果通常需要较多控制，如何在减少控制数量的情况下实现能控性是一个挑战。由于速度方程（由Stokes算子支配）和温度方程（由Dirichlet拉普拉斯算子支配）的自然谱分解不同，不存在适应于耦合系统的共同谱局部化，导致经典的逐分量谱论证无法直接应用，需要发展新的技术方法。

Method: 采用Lebeau-Robbiano谱方法框架。关键创新包括：(1)利用线性化系统的级联结构克服谱分解不匹配的困难；(2)建立混合可观测性估计，仅对Stokes分量进行谱局部化，而热分量不做频率限制处理；(3)结合Stokes高频部分的耗散性完成Lebeau-Robbiano迭代；(4)通过时间迭代论证将线性能控性估计推广到非线性系统。

Result: 实现了二维和三维Boussinesq系统的局部零能控性，控制仅作用于温度方程和N-2个速度分量。控制的小时间成本被C exp(C/T)界住，恢复了预期的抛物阶，同时保持了减少的控制数量。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了即使耦合系统的两个算子缺乏共同谱结构，仍可通过利用系统的级联结构和混合频域处理策略实现减少控制的能控性，其小时间成本达到抛物系统的预期最优阶。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the local null controllability of the Boussinesq system in dimensions two and three with a reduced number of localized controls. More precisely, the controls act on the temperature equation and on only $N-2$ components of the velocity equation. Our proof is based on a spectral approach in the spirit of the Lebeau--Robbiano method.
  The main difficulty comes from the coupled structure of the system. The velocity and temperature equations are governed by the Stokes operator and the Dirichlet Laplacian, respectively. Their natural spectral decompositions are different, and there is no common spectral localization naturally adapted to the coupled system. Therefore, the usual componentwise spectral argument cannot be applied directly. We show that the cascade structure of the linearized system makes it possible to overcome this difficulty. The main ingredient is a mixed observability estimate in which only the Stokes component is spectrally localized, while the heat component is treated without any frequency restriction. Combining this estimate with the dissipation of the high Stokes frequencies allows us to carry out a Lebeau--Robbiano iteration for the linearized system. The resulting linear controllability estimate is transferred to the nonlinear Boussinesq system through a time-iteration argument. As an important consequence, the controls have a small-time cost bounded by $C\exp(C/T)$, which recovers the expected parabolic order while preserving the reduced number of controls.

</details>


### [95] [Distribution Steering via Sliced Optimal Transport Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12828)
*Kaito Ito,Anqi Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于切片最优传输（Sliced Optimal Transport）的有限时域控制框架，用于设计驱动状态分布从初始分布到目标分布的反惯定律。通过在每个采样时刻利用投影最优传输映射定义方向终端条件，并取最小能量实现与方向平均，得到确定性切片反馈。该反馈在单积分器动力学下保证切片Wasserstein距离单调不增，对高斯端点分布是仿射且保持高斯性，并给出线性衰减速率与显式控制能量刻画。此外还证明了随机控制器在采样周期趋零时收敛到平均切片流，并将构造推广到线性系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布控制的目标是设计使系统的状态分布从给定初始分布演化到目标分布的反馈律。最优传输是自然的几何途径，但在全状态空间中需要构造传输映射或耦合，计算代价高。切片最优传输通过一维投影避免了全维构造，但投影映射仅给出方向位移，无法直接定义可实现的反惯律。本文旨在弥合这一缺口。

Method: 在每个采样时刻，利用一维投影最优传输映射定义方向终端条件；对单积分器动力学，构造该方向条件的最小能量随机单方向控制器；对所有投影方向取平均得到确定性切片反馈。对高斯端点分布推导仿射反馈结构并分析稳定性与能量。利用可达性归一化坐标和局部可控性Gramian将构造推广到线性系统。

Result: 在单积分器动力学下，平均切片反馈使切片Wasserstein距离非增；对高斯端点分布，反馈是仿射的、保持高斯性，并驱动均值与协方差到目标值；识别了与分布相关的增益可实现切片Wasserstein距离的线性衰减并给出显式控制能量刻画；随机控制器在采样周期趋零时收敛到平均切片流；该构造可推广至线性可控系统。

Conclusion: 该工作为分布控制提供了基于切片最优传输的可行反馈设计框架，兼顾了计算效率与可实现性，并通过理论保证（单调性、高斯保持、收敛性）和数值示例验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Distribution steering seeks feedback laws that drive the state law of a dynamical system between prescribed initial and terminal distributions. Optimal transport provides a natural geometric approach, but its implementation generally requires a transport map or coupling in the full state space. Sliced optimal transport avoids this full-dimensional construction through one-dimensional projections. Yet, the resulting projected maps specify only directional displacements and do not by themselves prescribe a realizable feedback law. To this end, we develop a finite-horizon control framework based on sliced optimal transport. At each sampling instant, a projected optimal transport map defines a directional terminal condition, whose minimum-energy realization yields a randomized single-direction controller. Averaging over projection directions gives a deterministic sliced feedback. For the single-integrator dynamics, the averaged feedback makes the sliced Wasserstein distance to the target non-increasing. For Gaussian endpoint laws, it is affine, preserves Gaussianity, and steers the mean and covariance to their prescribed terminal values. We further identify a law-dependent gain that yields linear decay of the sliced Wasserstein distance together with an explicit characterization of the control energy. We also prove that the randomized controller converges to the averaged sliced flow as the sampling period vanishes. Finally, we extend the construction to linear dynamical systems. Reachability-normalized coordinates allow instantaneous realization of the sliced velocity for uniformly fully actuated systems, while local controllability Gramians provide exact finite-step realization for general controllable systems. Numerical examples illustrate the resulting distributional flows.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于距离的编队控制大多假设系统为简单积分器模型或已知动态，而实际系统往往具有相对阶大于1和未知非线性动态。此外，预设瞬态性能（如漏斗约束）在距离编队控制中尚未得到充分研究。本文旨在弥补这一空缺，为更一般且更贴近实际的系统模型提供理论保证。

Method: 将控制对象建模为相对阶大于1的未知非线性动态系统（如欧拉-拉格朗日系统）。引入虚拟领导者作为参考轨迹，指定部分智能体对其进行跟踪。对树图情形，直接基于距离误差设计漏斗控制律；对最小且无穷小刚性图情形，在编队控制基础上进一步加入质心跟踪目标。控制律采用无模型（model-free）设计，不依赖系统动态的显式知识，通过漏斗变量对误差施加时变约束。

Result: 针对两种图结构分别建立了分布式无模型控制律，证明了所形成的编队距离误差及跟踪误差始终满足预设的漏斗约束，同时保证所有闭环信号的有界性。数值仿真验证了所提控制律的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为具有未知高阶非线性动态的多智能体系统提供了带预设瞬态性能的分布式距离编队控制方案，兼顾了树图与刚性图两种通信拓扑，并扩展了质心跟踪功能。未来工作可考虑放宽通信图条件或进一步推广到更复杂的系统动态。

Abstract: We consider distributed distance-based formation control with prescribed transient performance for multi-agent systems modeled by unknown nonlinear dynamics of relative degree greater than one. We introduce a virtual leader whose trajectory is tracked with prescribed transient behavior by a designated subset of agents. The undirected communication graph is either a tree graph or a minimally and infinitesimally rigid graph. In the latter case, we further consider the objective of centroid tracking. In each setting, a distributed and model-free control law is developed, and we establish the satisfaction of the prescribed funnel constraints on the formation and tracking errors, and boundedness of all closed-loop signals. Numerical simulations illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed control laws.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随访CT调度研究仅优化扫描仪利用率或患者等待时间，忽视了报告活动和多利益相关者间的公平性平衡需求，缺乏一个同时兼顾患者时间偏好与放射科医生工作量公平性的综合框架。

Method: 1) 构建患者特定ML模型预测检查时长与报告时长；2) 将预测嵌入多目标MILP模型，采用字典序min-max公平准则平衡放射科医生报告工作量；3) 在ε-约束框架中推导支配性约简性质以加速Pareto前沿生成；4) 通过下游优化遗憾评估预测模型的实际决策支持效果。

Conclusion: 预测-预设框架能有效平衡患者时间偏好与放射科医生工作量的公平性，通过适度调度灵活性实现双赢；在不牺牲Pareto最优性的前提下大幅提高求解效率；选择预测模型时应评估其下游决策影响而非仅看传统预测误差指标。

Abstract: Scheduling follow-up Computed Tomography (CT) examinations requires balancing two competing objectives: assigning patients as close as possible to their recommended examination dates while ensuring an equitable distribution of radiologists' workload. Existing approaches optimize scanner utilization or patient waiting times, overlooking reporting activities and the need to balance fairness across multiple stakeholders. This paper proposes a predictive-prescriptive framework for fairness-aware follow-up CT scheduling. Patient-specific Machine Learning (ML) models are first developed to predict both examination and reporting durations. These predictions are then embedded into a multi-objective Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) model that simultaneously minimizes deviations from patients' preferred examination dates and balances radiologists' reporting workloads through a lexicographic min-max fairness criterion. We derive a dominance reduction property within an $ε$-constraint framework that substantially reduces the number of optimization problems required to generate the Pareto frontier. Computational experiments based on data from a real-world emergency radiology department show that allowing patients a scheduling flexibility of only one to two days is sufficient to substantially improve workload equity among radiologists while preserving timely access to follow-up examinations. The proposed dominance reduction strategy eliminates most $ε$-constraint evaluations without affecting the Pareto frontier. Finally, evaluating predictive models through downstream optimization regret demonstrates that XGBoost provides the most effective support for scheduling decisions, outperforming models that achieve lower prediction errors according to conventional predictive metrics.

</details>

Conclusion: 本文为边界控制扩散系统提供了完整、解析且实用化的正性控制与估计解决方案，兼具理论完备性与数值可实施性。

Abstract: This paper provides a complete analytical study of the positive stabilization, positive state estimation, and observer-based positive stabilization problems for a generic boundary control diffusion system with point observation. In particular, optimal design problems are solved analytically, and an original strategy for observer-based feedback stabilization is proposed. The three problems are also investigated for an arbitrary-order spatial discretization of the nominal PDE system enabling numerical implementation together with rigorous comparisons with the nominal solutions. In both the continuous and discretized settings, explicit and readily implementable solutions are derived.

Result: 在不假设任何约束规格的条件下，证明了生成的序列至少存在一个聚点，该聚点为KKT点、近似KKT（AKKT）点或相关可行性问题的稳定点。数值实验表明该方法对退化问题具有有效性。

Abstract: We propose a stabilized sequential quadratic programming (SQP) method for degenerate constrained optimization problems on Riemannian manifolds. The problem considered in this study is a Riemannian nonlinear programming problem (RNLP) with equality and inequality constraints, where classical constraint qualifications may fail. While existing Riemannian SQP methods guarantee global convergence only under constraint qualifications, their convergence behavior is not ensured for degenerate problems. To address this limitation, we extend the stabilized SQP framework from Euclidean spaces to Riemannian manifolds. Without assuming any constraint qualification, we prove that the generated sequence has an accumulation point that is a Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) point, an approximate KKT (AKKT) point, or a stationary point of an associated feasibility problem. Finally, we conduct numerical experiments to confirm the effectiveness of the proposed method for degenerate problems.

</details>


### [100] [Distributed and Dynamic Hub Network Operation Planning in a Hyperconnected Less-Than-Truckload Operating System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13419)

TL;DR: 本文针对零担运输（LTL）网络中运营效率低、网络碎片化的问题，基于物理互联网（PI）原理，设计了一套多枢纽运营规划协议，支持多个区域枢纽网络之间的合作，通过智能分配车厢与调度拖车来提升整体物流系统的效率与可持续性。


<details>
