Result: 通过多场景下的仿真实验验证了所提协议在运营商多枢纽网络中的有效性，表明该协议能够实现合作性结果，满足货件请求，提升了PI网络的实用性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究增强了基于物理互联网的网络系统的实用性和鲁棒性，促进了枢纽网络间的更大合作，有助于构建更高效、更可持续的物流系统。

Abstract: The less-than-truckload (LTL) industry plays a vital role in enhancing the efficiency and sustainability of logistics systems, as LTL shipments offer greater consolidation opportunities than full-truckload shipments. Despite of this flexibility, the average cost of LTL shipments remains considerably higher due to less efficient operations and highly fragmented networks of small and medium-sized carriers. Building on our ongoing effort to develop a distributed and dynamic logistics hub network system grounded in the Physical Internet (PI) principles of modular containers and open resource sharing, this study focuses specifically on inter-hub and in-hub operations, with cooperation among multiple regional hub networks. Therefore, a shipment may traverse multiple cooperating hub networks. With respect to each hub network each shipment enters, it is defined by its expected arrival time at the entry hub and its latest arrival time at the exit hub. Based on the defined shipment information, we design a set of multi-hub operation planning protocols for distributed hub operators. In their operating networks, operators use our smartly designed protocol separately to plan in-hub shipments' assignments to destination-specific trailers and inter-hub trailers' dispatch schedules. With carefully designed interconnections between hub networks, the aggregated hub network system is well-positioned to achieve cooperative outcomes and fulfill shipment requests. We evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed protocol through a simulation-based experiment under multiple scenarios in an operator's multi-hub network. Overall, this research improves the practicality and robustness of PI-based networks and supports greater cooperation among hub networks toward more efficient and sustainable logistics systems.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [On Waring--Goldbach problem with mixed powers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12397)
*Huimin Wang,Shuangrui Tian*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了对于每一个足够大的奇数n，方程 n = p₁² + p₂² + p₃³ + p₄⁴ + p₅⁵ + p₆⁶ + p₇⁸ 在素数范围内可解，改进了Cai和Mu在2015年的工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在改进Cai和Mu在2015年关于Waring-Goldbach问题的结果，证明更一般形式的素数幂次和方程的可解性，进一步推进经典Goldbach猜想的推广方向。

Method: 论文通过解析数论中的圆法（circle method）等技术手段，构造指数和的估计，利用素数密度和指数和的算术性质来证明该方程对充分大的奇数存在素数解。

Result: 证明了对于所有足够大的奇数整数n，该七项混合幂次素数方程均有素数解，该结果提炼并改进了Cai和Mu于2015年取得的成果。

Conclusion: 该结果将Waring-Goldbach问题扩展到更一般的混合幂次组合，丰富了该领域的已有结论，并为后续改进提供一个更精细的研究方向。

Abstract: It is proved that for every sufficiently large odd integer $n$, the equation $$ n=p_1^2+p_2^2+p_3^3+p_4^4+p_5^5+p_6^6+p_7^8 $$ is solvable in primes. This refines the work of Cai and Mu in 2015.

</details>


### [102] [Generalized Addition Theorems for the Jacobi Elliptic Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12399)
*Efe Gürel*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文推广了Jacobi椭圆函数的经典加法定理，给出了多种隐式和显式公式，包括涉及多个Jacobi函数的行列式恒等式、单个函数的显式加法公式，以及全新的4、5、6、7项和一般的n项加法公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Jacobi椭圆函数的加法定理是数学物理中的重要工具，但已有的加法公式形式有限，需要推广以获得更一般、更灵活的公式形式，以满足更广泛的数学和应用需求。

Method: 首先推导涉及多个Jacobi椭圆函数的行列式恒等式；随后证明单个函数的显式加法公式；在此基础上给出4、5、6、7项的新型加法公式，并推广至一般的n项加法公式。

Result: 获得了普遍化的Jacobi椭圆函数加法公式体系，包括隐式和显式两类公式，以及从4项到任意n项的多项加法公式。

Conclusion: 本文成功将Jacobi椭圆函数的加法定理从经典的二项形式推广到多项形式，建立了统一的公式框架，为后续数学和物理应用提供了更强大的工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we generalize the classical addition theorems for the Jacobi elliptic functions, giving numerous implicit and explicit formulas. We first present determinant identities involving several of the Jacobi functions. Then, we prove explicit addition formulas for a single function. Novel 4-,5-,6- and 7-term addition formulas are also given alongside a general $n$-term addition formula.

</details>


### [103] [A conditional arithmetic obstruction to the prime numbers as a spectrum of a probability measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12400)
*Zhi-Yi Wu,Qian Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明：若每个充分大的偶数都可表示为两个素数之差（Polignac猜想），则素数集合不能作为任何ℝ上Borel概率测度的指数正交基（谱）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究素数集合是否可能成为L²(μ)中指数正交基的频谱，这是一个涉及数论与调和分析交叉的问题，旨在揭示素数结构的算术性质对其作为频谱可能性的限制。

Method: 借助素数差覆盖性质（即Polignac猜想中每个充分大偶数可表示为两素数之差）来构造矛盾论证，证明若素数集是某个概率测度的谱，则与该算术性质相矛盾，从而推得素数集不能是任何概率测度的频谱。

Result: 在Polignac猜想成立的条件下，素数集合P不能成为ℝ上任何Borel概率测度μ的L²(μ)指数正交基（即频谱）。

Conclusion: 素数集合在算术结构上不具备作为指数正交基频谱的充分条件，这一结果将数论猜想与调和分析中的谱理论建立了新的联系。

Abstract: Let $\Pp=\{2,3,5,7,\ldots\}$ denote the set of prime numbers. We prove that if every sufficiently large positive even integer can be represented as a difference of two primes, then there is no Borel probability measure $μ$ on $\R$ for which \(\left\{e^{2πi p x}:p\in\Pp\right\}\) is an orthonormal basis of $L^2(μ)$. In particular, under the Polignac conjecture, the prime numbers $\Pp$ cannot be a spectrum (i.e., the set of frequencies of an exponential orthonormal basis) of any probability measure on $\R$.

</details>


### [104] [Finding all cospectral mates over a number field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12410)
*Alexander Van Werde*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种由代数数域参数化的整数矩阵谱相似性概念，探讨在给定数域中通过正交矩阵共轭能否产生新的整数矩阵，并给出了谱确定性判定的充分条件及正交矩阵的约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典谱图论中，谱确定问题关注哪些图由其邻接矩阵的特征谱唯一确定。本文将这一概念推广到代数数域参数的整数矩阵框架下，探究在特定数域中允许的正交共轭变换是否可能产生新的整数矩阵，从而深化对谱确定性与数域结构之间联系的理解。

Method: 利用特征多项式的判别式和Krylov子空间的性质，推导谱确定性的充分条件；当条件不适用时，给出正交矩阵的结构约束。在此基础上设计了在给定小域上寻找所有谱伴矩阵的算法，并提供了实现。

Result: 得到了谱确定性的充分条件，以及对潜在正交矩阵的限制条件。开发了能够在给定数域上枚举所有谱伴矩阵的算法并公开了实现代码。

Conclusion: 本文建立了由数域参数化的整数矩阵谱相似性理论框架，结合特征多项式判别式与Krylov子空间工具给出了谱确定性的判据，并通过算法实现了对小域场景下谱伴矩阵的完整枚举。

Abstract: We investigate a notion of cospectrality for integer matrices that is parameterized by algebraic number fields. Given a number field and a symmetric integer matrix, we wonder when conjugating the integer matrix by an orthogonal matrix with entries in the given field can produce new integer matrices.
  Our results concern sufficient conditions for the associated notion of spectral determination, and we give constraints on the orthogonal matrices when the conditions are not applicable. The results use the discriminant of the characteristic polynomial and properties of Krylov subspaces. We leverage the theory to develop an algorithm to find all cospectral mates over a given (small) field. An implementation of the algorithm is made available.

</details>


### [105] [Special Values of Shifted Dirichlet Series from an Adjoint Map on Almost Holomorphic Modular Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12542)
*Raveena Ganash*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文找到了几乎全纯模形式空间上某个映射的伴随算子，该算子与拟模形式空间上的广义Ramanujan-Serre导数类似，并利用此伴随算子显式地求出了与拟模形式相关的某些移位Dirichlet级数的特殊值，同时证明了这些移位Dirichlet级数的有理性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拟模形式上的广义Ramanujan-Serre导数已有深入研究，但对应的几乎全纯模形式空间上的类比映射及其伴随算子尚未被系统研究。寻找该伴随算子的动机在于，它可以作为工具来推导移位Dirichlet级数的特殊值及有理性质，从而加深对拟模形式相关解析量的理解。

Method: 作者在几乎全纯模形式空间上定义了与广义Ramanujan-Serre导数相类比的映射，并计算了该映射的伴随算子。利用这一伴随算子，通过系统的代数与解析论证，显式地推导了特定移位Dirichlet级数在特殊点处的取值，进而证明了这些级数的有理性质。

Result: 成功找到了几乎全纯模形式空间上类Ramanujan-Serre映射的伴随算子，并以此为工具显式求出了若干与拟模形式相关的移位Dirichlet级数的特殊值，同时证明了这些移位Dirichlet级数取值的有理性。

Conclusion: 本文通过构造几乎全纯模形式上的伴随算子，建立了一种新的方法来确定移位Dirichlet级数的特殊值及其有理性质，推广并深化了Ramanujan-Serre导数理论在这一方向上的应用。

Abstract: We find the adjoint of a map on the space of almost holomorphic modular forms, analogous to the generalized Ramanujan-Serre derivative on the space of quasi-modular forms. As applications, we explicitly find special values of certain shifted Dirichlet series associated to quasi-modular forms and prove a rationality result for the shifted Dirichlet series.

</details>


### [106] [Algebraic constructions of point sequences with quasi-uniform two-dimensional projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12690)
*Takashi Goda*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了d维单位立方体中可扩展点集（extensible point sets）的代数构造，使得其所有二维坐标投影都是拟均匀的（quasi-uniform）。作者提出了两种构造方法：基于三次数域的Kronecker序列，以及基于实二次域和p-adic嵌入的嵌套秩-1格点设计。两种构造均以射影直线P^1(Q)上的有限有理方向配置为共同参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究受到序贯空间填充设计（sequential space-filling designs）的启发。在计算机实验中，需要构造可扩展的点集，即对于任意初始段长度N>=2，其所有二维坐标投影的网格比（mesh ratio）都能保持一致的有界性，这对序贯实验的均匀性至关重要。

Method: 1) 利用三次数域构造显式Kronecker序列，使其所有二维投影的网格比对每个初始段N>=2均匀有界。2) 利用实二次域、分裂素数和兼容的p-adic嵌入构造嵌套秩-1格点设计，在每一嵌套层级都保持均匀投影性质。证明结合了代数范数估计与联立/对偶丢番图逼近之间的传递原理，得到分离半径的下界和覆盖半径的上界，且这些界在点数上达到最优阶，对所有坐标对一致成立。此外，作者还将有理投影系数的最优选择归结为P^1(Q)上有限配置的极小极大问题。

Result: 两种构造都实现了所有二维坐标投影拟均匀的可扩展点集：Kronecker序列和嵌套秩-1格点设计均对任意初始段长度N>=2保持网格比一致有界。分离半径和覆盖半径的估计达到最优阶。同时揭示了一个关于P^1(Q)上有限配置的极小极大优化问题，涉及有理投影系数的选择对网格比的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了具有一致受控二维几何的可扩展点集的系统构造方法，将数论工具（数域、p-adic嵌入、丢番图逼近）与实验设计理论相结合，为序贯空间填充设计提供了新的理论保障和构造框架。

Abstract: Motivated by sequential space-filling designs for computer experiments, we study algebraic constructions of extensible point sets in the $d$-dimensional unit cube whose two-dimensional coordinate projections are all quasi-uniform. Our two constructions share a common parametrization in terms of finite configurations of distinct rational directions on the projective line $\mathbb{P}^1(\mathbb{Q})$. First, using a cubic number field, we construct explicit Kronecker sequences for which the mesh ratios of all two-dimensional coordinate projections remain uniformly bounded over every initial segment of length $N\ge 2$. Second, using a real quadratic field, a split prime, and a compatible $p$-adic embedding, we construct nested rank-1 lattice designs with the same uniform projection property at every nesting level. The proofs combine algebraic norm estimates with transference principles between simultaneous and dual Diophantine approximation, yielding lower bounds for the separation radii and upper bounds for the covering radii, both of optimal order in the number of points, uniformly over all coordinate pairs. We also investigate how the choice of rational projective coefficients affects the resulting mesh ratios. This leads to a minimax problem for finite configurations on $\mathbb{P}^1(\mathbb{Q})$, in which one seeks to minimize the maximum mesh ratio over all two-dimensional coordinate projections. These constructions provide extensible point sets with uniformly controlled bivariate geometry.

</details>


### [107] [Density bounds for permutations avoiding monotone arithmetic progressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12604)
*Jesse Geneson*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了关于单调ℓ项算术级数的ω-置换存在性问题，证明了自然数和整数集合上三项上密度参数的下界至少为2/3，并构造了整数子集证明四项下密度参数等于1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在加强已有文献中关于三项上密度参数的已知下界，并首次确定四项下密度参数的精确值，从而深化对ω-置换与单调等差数列之间关系的理解。

Method: 通过构造具体的集合与置换结构，证明α_N(3)≥2/3和α_Z(3)≥2/3；并通过构造四可置换（four-permutable）的整数子集，使其下对称密度趋近于1，从而证明β_Z(4)=1。

Result: 证明了α_N(3)≥2/3和α_Z(3)≥2/3，并且通过构造证明β_Z(4)=1，即整数集合中存在下对称密度任意接近1的四可置换子集。

Conclusion: 文章加强了三项上密度参数的下界，并确定了四项下密度参数的极值，推进了对具有无单调等差数列性质的ω-置换集合结构的刻画。

Abstract: For $X\in\{\mathbb{N},\mathbb{Z}\}$, let $α_X(\ell)$ and $β_X(\ell)$ denote the supremal upper and lower densities of subsets of $X$ admitting $ω$-permutations without monotone $\ell$-term arithmetic progressions. We strengthen the published lower bounds for the three-term upper-density parameters by proving \[
  α_{\mathbb{N}}(3)\geq\frac23,\qquad
  α_{\mathbb{Z}}(3)\geq\frac23. \] We also prove $β_{\mathbb{Z}}(4)=1$ by constructing four-permutable subsets of the integers whose lower symmetric densities approach one.

</details>


### [108] [Escalations and criteria over real quadratic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12648)
*Jakub Krásenský,Giuliano Romeo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究全实数域上的通用二次型判定准则集。针对仅在Q(√5)上有显式判定集、其他域缺乏相关结果的现状，作者将escalation方法推广到数域上，理论证明了准则集的有限性，并提供了计算工具；通过对Q(√2)和Q(√3)的显式计算提出了关于通用性准则的猜想，同时发展了对角二次型的escalation理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 著名的15-定理和290-定理完全刻画了有理数域上的通用二次型，类似的定理虽然对所有全实数域都存在，但只有Q(√5)上判定准则集是显式已知的。因此需要一种系统的方法来研究和计算其他数域上的判定准则集。

Method: 1. 将escalation（升级）方法从有理数域推广到一般数域，提供准则有限性的简洁证明及实用计算工具；2. 对Q(√2)和Q(√3)进行显式计算，提出通用性准则猜想并证明多个escalator格的通用性；3. 为对角二次型建立独立的escalation理论，获得Q(√5)的对角判定准则集以及对Q(√2)、Q(√3)的猜想。

Result: 给出了数域上escalation方法的完整理论框架和计算实现；对Q(√2)和Q(√3)得到了相应的通用性准则猜想，证明了大量escalator格的通用性；建立了对角二次型的escalation理论，得到了Q(√5)的对角判定准则集及Q(√2)、Q(√3)的猜想。

Conclusion: 本文从理论和计算两个层面推进了数域上通用二次型判定准则的研究，将escalation方法成功推广到数域场景，填补了除Q(√5)外全实数域上判定准则缺乏显式结果的空白，并为后续更一般数域的研究提供了可行的方法论。

Abstract: The famous 15-Theorem and 290-Theorem fully characterise universal quadratic forms over $\mathbb{Q}$. Similar theorems exist for every totally real number field, but only over $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt5)$ the criterion set is explicitly known. We study criterion sets both theoretically and computationally: We develop the method of escalation over number fields, thus providing a simple proof of finiteness of the criteria and, more importantly, a practical tool for computing them. We illustrate this by explicit computations for $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt2)$ and $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt3)$, obtaining a conjecture about the corresponding universality criteria; we also prove universality of many escalator lattices. Moreover, we develop the somewhat different theory of escalations for diagonal quadratic forms, obtaining the diagonal criterion set for $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt5)$ and conjecturally for $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt2)$ and $\mathbb{Q}(\sqrt3)$.

</details>


### [109] [Uniform bounds on prime levels of abelian division fields of elliptic curves over number fields without rationally defined CM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12709)
*Hansol Kim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文去除了广义黎曼猜想（GRH）的假设，证明了定义在任意数域上且无有理定义复乘（CM）的椭圆曲线，其素数阶分圆域为阿贝尔这一性质，实际上等价于该曲线没有有理定义的CM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: González-Jiménez和Lozano-Robledo证明了有理数域上椭圆曲线n分圆域为阿贝尔的n存在一致上界。Allen和Genao在GRH假设下将此结果部分推广到任意数域（无有理定义CM且限制在素数阶）。本文的目标是移除GRH假设，将这一结果强化为充要条件。

Method: 通过移除GRH假设，采用更精细的代数数论和椭圆曲线算术方法，证明Allen-Genao所建立的性质（素数阶分圆域为阿贝尔）与椭圆曲线不存在有理定义CM是等价的，从而将原充分条件提升为充要条件。

Result: 成功去除了GRH假设，并证明对于任意数域上定义的椭圆曲线，素数阶分圆域为阿贝尔这一性质成立当且仅当该曲线无有理定义的复乘。

Conclusion: 这一结果将Allen和Genao在GRH假设下的部分推广强化为无条件成立的充要条件定理，从本质上刻画了椭圆曲线分圆域为阿贝尔的性质，并完全由CM的存在性所决定。

Abstract: Enrique Gonz{á}lez-Jim{é}nez and {Á}lvaro Lozano-Robledo proved that there exists a uniform bound on the levels $n$ for which the $n$-division field $\mathbb{Q}\left(E\left[n\right]\right) / \mathbb{Q}$ of an elliptic curve $E/\mathbb{Q}$ defined over $\mathbb{Q}$ is abelian. Assuming GRH (Generalized Riemann Hypothesis), Allen and Genao partially generalized this result by restricting to prime levels while allowing arbitrary number fields without rationally defined CM. In this paper, we remove the GRH assumption and prove that the property established by Allen and Genao is in fact equivalent to the absence of rationally defined CM.

</details>


### [110] [Finite-Sum Realization of Archimedean Asai and Exterior-Square $L$-Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12722)
*Yeongseong Jo,Akash Yadav*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了两类局部L-因子可用局部zeta积分的有穷和表示：一是GL_n(C)不可约一般表示对应的Archimedean Asai L-因子可表示为Flicker局部zeta积分的有穷和；二是Archimedean局部域F上GL_m(F)不可约一般表示对应的外平方L-因子可表示为Jacquet–Shalika局部zeta积分的有穷和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Langlands纲领和自守形式理论中，L-因子的积分表示（Rankin–Selberg方法）是核心工具。Asai L-因子和外平方L-因子在函子性研究中具有重要地位，而此前它们在Archimedean情形下缺乏类似的局部zeta积分表达，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者通过构造并分析Archimedean情形下的Flicker局部zeta积分和Jacquet–Shalika局部zeta积分，利用不可约一般表示的结构性质及Archimedean调和分析工具，证明了这些积分的有穷线性组合恰好等于对应的L-因子。

Result: 成功证明了Archimedean Asai L-因子可由Flicker局部zeta积分的有穷和表示，Archimedean外平方L-因子可由Jacquet–Shalika局部zeta积分的有穷和表示。

Conclusion: 该结果将Rankin–Selberg方法推广到了Archimedean Asai和外平方L-因子的情形，为相关L-因子的进一步研究和应用提供了积分表示的途径。

Abstract: We prove that the archimedean Asai $L$-factor attached to an irreducible generic representation of $\operatorname{GL}_n(\mathbb{C})$ can be expressed as a finite sum of Flicker local zeta integrals. For an archimedean local field $F$, we also prove that the exterior-square $L$-factor attached to an irreducible generic representation of $\operatorname{GL}_m(F)$ can be expressed as a finite sum of Jacquet--Shalika local zeta integrals.

</details>


### [111] [On the Lorenz-Fibonacci sequences and substitutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12739)
*Bernardo San Martín,Víctor Sirvent*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了两族整数序列——第一类和第二类$(k,r)$-Lorenz-Fibonacci序列，其特征多项式为$p_{k,r}(x)=x^k-x^{k-1}-\cdots-x^r+x^{r-1}+\cdots+x+1$（其中$k\geq 2r+1$，$k\geq 3$）。当$r=0$时，这些序列退化为著名的$k$-bonacci序列。这些序列源自Lorenz吸引子动力学研究。文中引入了与这两族序列相关联、共享相同特征多项式的两族替换（substitution），并研究了这些替换的主要组合性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这些整数序列在Lorenz吸引子的动力学研究中自然出现，对其组合结构的理解有助于揭示相关动力系统的深层性质。此外，作为$k$-bonacci序列的推广，研究这些新序列及其对应的替换系统具有重要的理论价值。

Method: 作者定义了$(k,r)$-Lorenz-Fibonacci序列（第一类和第二类），其特征多项式为$p_{k,r}(x)$。在此基础上，在$k$个符号的字母表上构造了两族替换（substitution），使得它们分别与两族整数序列相关联，并共享同一特征多项式。随后对这些替换的组合性质进行了系统研究。

Result: 成功引入两族替换，并建立了它们与$(k,r)$-Lorenz-Fibonacci序列（第一类和第二类）之间的对应关系，同时证明了这些替换与两族整数序列共享相同的特征多项式$p_{k,r}(x)$。

Conclusion: 本文通过构造两族与$(k,r)$-Lorenz-Fibonacci序列相关联的替换，建立了整数序列与组合替换系统之间的桥梁，为理解Lorenz吸引子动力学提供了新的组合工具。

Abstract: In the present article, we consider two families of integer sequences, the $(k,r)$-Lorenz-Fibonacci sequences of the first and second kind, whose characteristic polynomial is $$p_{k,r}(x)=x^k-x^{k-1}-\cdots- x^r+x^{r-1}+\cdots +x+1,$$ where $k\geq 2r+1$, and $k\geq 3$. These families include the well-known $k$-bonacci sequence, when $r=0$. These sequences arise naturally in the study of the dynamics of the Lorenz attractor. We introduce two families of substitutions (in an alphabet of $k$ symbols) so that they are associated with each of the families of integer sequences and share the same polynomial. We study the main combinatorial properties of these substitutions.

</details>


### [112] [Cyclic permutations of large subsets with polynomial values in multiplicative subgroups of finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12758)
*Hai-Liang Wu,He-Xia Ni*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域\mathbb{F}_p上子集A的Hamilton圈问题：对于非恒定整数系数多项式f(t)（判别式非零）和整数k≥2，在素数p≡1(mod k)足够大时，建立了阈值c(p,k,f)，使得任何大小达到该阈值的子集A都能将其中元素排成一个循环排列，使得相邻两项之和经f映射后是k次幂剩余。文章还给出了该最小可能阈值的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题的动机在于将有限域上的加法结构与幂剩余/幂剩余类相结合，研究在什么样的密度条件下能保证存在满足特定代数约束（多项式值落在k次幂集合中）的循环排列（Hamilton圈）。这联系了加性数论、有限域上指数和以及谱图理论多个领域。

Method: 方法上综合运用了以下工具：（1）有限域上的混合指数和（mixed exponential sums）来估计相关图（依赖于f的值的k次幂剩余性质）的边分布；（2）离散傅里叶分析来处理旋转不变性和谱分解；（3）谱图理论（spectral graph theory）来证明所构造图的Hamilton圈存在性，通过Cheeger不等式或谱间隙估计得到边扩展性质。

Result: 对于每个足够大的素数p≡1(mod k)，建立了阈值c(p,k,f)，使得|A|≥c(p,k,f)的子集A总存在满足条件f(a_i+a_{i+1})∈\mathbb{F}_p^{*k}的循环排列。此外，给出了该最小可能阈值精确的上下界估计。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，当有限域\mathbb{F}_p中边的加法结构满足f-像落入k次幂集合时，足够稠密的子集必将包含Hamilton圈，推广和统一了相关文献中关于power residues图上的Hamilton圈存在性结果。

Abstract: Let $f(t)\in\mathbb{Z}[t]$ be a nonconstant polynomial with nonzero discriminant and let $k\ge2$ be an integer. For every
  sufficiently large prime $p\equiv1\pmod{k}$, by applying mixed exponential sums over finite fields, discrete Fourier analysis and the spectral graph theory, we establish a threshold $c(p,k,f)$ such that any subset $A\subseteq\mathbb F_p$ with $\#A\ge c(p,k,f)$ admits a permutation $a_1, a_2,\cdots, a_{\#A}$ of $A$ satisfying $f(a_i+a_{i+1})\in\{x^k: x\in\mathbb{F}_p^*\}$ for any $1\le i \le \#A$, where $a_{\#A+1}=a_1$. Also, we give lower and upper bounds for the least possible threshold.

</details>


### [113] [A study of degenerate Bernoulli and Euler numbers via operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12927)
*Taekyun Kim,Dae san Kim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入新型算子并将其应用于特殊数的研究，利用这些算子推导了退化欧拉数和第二类退化欧拉数的显式表达式，并建立了退化伯努利数与退化欧拉数之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在通过引入新的算子类来研究特殊数（特别是退化欧拉数、第二类退化欧拉数以及退化伯努利数），以获得这些数的显式表达式并揭示它们之间的内在联系。

Method: 论文引入了一类新算子，利用这些算子研究退化欧拉数和第二类退化欧拉数的显式表达式，并进一步探索退化伯努利数与退化欧拉数之间的关系。这些算子是在研究显式表达式和关系的过程中自然涌现的。

Result: 作者成功推导出退化欧拉数和第二类退化欧拉数的显式表达式，并建立了退化伯努利数与退化欧拉数之间的数学关系。

Conclusion: 本文提出的新算子类为研究特殊数提供了自然的工具，不仅得到了退化欧拉数的显式表达式，还揭示了退化伯努利数与退化欧拉数之间的深刻联系，这些算子具有进一步推广到其他特殊数研究的潜力。

Abstract: The paper introduces novel classes of operators and investigates their applications to the study of special numbers. We utilize these operators to derive explicit expressions for degenerate Euler numbers and type 2 degenerate Euler numbers. Furthermore, we establish a relationship between degenerate Bernoulli numbers and degenerate Euler numbers. We note here that these operators emerge naturally when we study such explicit expressions and such a relationship

</details>


### [114] [Analytic rank one propagation in Hida families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13145)
*Stefano Vigni*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了：在算术代数几何中两个一般猜想（p-adic Abel-Jacobi映射的单射性、Gillet-Soulé意义上实高度配对的定量正定性）的特定实例成立的假设下，对于所有但有限多个满足条件的素数p，如果非CM新形式f的解析秩为1，则其通过的p-adic Hida族中几乎所有偶数权与平凡Nebentypus的 specialization 的解析秩也为1。这为Greenberg关于模形式族中解析秩的"极小性猜想"提供了证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Greenberg提出了关于模形式族中解析秩的"极小性猜想"，即在一个p-adic Hida族中，如果某个特殊化的解析秩为1，那么族中几乎所有特殊化的解析秩都应为1。作者旨在为这一猜想提供理论证据，通过研究非CM新形式f的p-adic Hida族中特殊化的解析秩行为。

Method: 作者结合两个关键的工具：一是p-adic Abel-Jacobi映射的单射性猜想，二是Gillet-Soulé实高度配对的定量正定性猜想。在这些假设下，利用p-adic Hida族的理论框架，通过分析特殊化的解析秩与代数秩之间的关系来推导结论。具体地，将f的解析秩为1的条件传递到族中几乎所有偶数权、平凡Nebentypus的特殊化上。

Result: 证明了在特定假设下，若非CM新形式f的解析秩为1，则其所在的p-adic Hida族中几乎所有偶数权、平凡Nebentypus的特殊化也具有解析秩1（排除有限多个例外）。此结果印证了Greenberg的极小性猜想在解析秩为1情况下的预期行为。

Conclusion: 该结果为Greenberg关于模形式族中解析秩"极小性猜想"提供了支持性证据。但结论依赖于两个算术代数几何中尚未完全证明的一般猜想，因此是条件性结果。同时，对素数p的限制（排除有限多个）也表明该结果在"几乎所有"而非全部情形下成立。

Abstract: Let $f$ be a non-CM newform of weight $k\geq4$, level $N$ and trivial Nebentypus. Let $p\nmid N$ be an odd prime number that is ordinary for $f$ and denote by $\boldsymbol f^{(p)}$ the $p$-adic Hida family passing through $f$. Assuming very specific instances of two general conjectures in arithmetic algebraic geometry (injectivity of $p$-adic Abel-Jacobi maps, positive definiteness of archimedean height pairings à la Gillet-Soulé), we prove that, for all but finitely many $p$ as above, if the analytic rank of $f$ is $1$, then all but finitely many specializations of $\boldsymbol f^{(p)}$ of even weight and trivial Nebentypus have analytic rank $1$. This result provides evidence for Greenberg's "minimality conjecture" on analytic ranks in families of modular forms.

</details>


### [115] [On $\mathcal{B}^4$-almost periodicity for a class of arithmetical functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13172)
*Kritika Aggarwal,Debika Banerjee,Shubham Gupta*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Chandrasekharan和Narasimhan所引入的一类广泛算术函数的相关误差项在Besicovitch意义下具有B^4-几乎周期性，显著加强了此前文献中研究的B^2-几乎周期性结果，并由此推导出该误差项存在极限概率分布及其四次幂矩的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前文献中仅研究了相关误差项的B^2-几乎周期性，作者希望将其显著加强为B^4-几乎周期性，以获得更强的统计性质（如极限概率分布和四次幂矩），从而更深入地理解该类算术函数误差项的分布行为。

Method: 通过推导截断型的Voronoï公式，证明归一化后的误差项属于Besicovitch空间B^4。利用该空间性质，结合B^4-几乎周期性来证明误差项存在极限概率分布，并建立均值四次幂矩的显式表达式。

Result: 证明了归一化误差项在Besicovitch意义下具有B^4-几乎周期性，并由此推导出误差项存在极限概率分布，且获得了其均值四次幂矩的显式公式。

Conclusion: 该结果将已有的B^2-几乎周期性结果提升至B^4水平，为理解此类算术函数误差项的统计分布提供了更深层的理论刻画，并给出了更精细的矩信息。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the $\mathcal{B}^4$-almost periodicity in the sense of Besicovitch for a suitably normalized error term associated with a broad class of arithmetical functions introduced by Chandrasekharan and Narasimhan. This result significantly strengthens $\mathcal{B}^2$-almost periodicity previously investigated in the literature for related error terms. By deriving truncated Voronoï-type formulas, we demonstrate that the normalized error term lies in the Besicovitch space $B^4$. As a consequence, we deduce that the error term admits a limit probability distribution and establish an explicit formula for its mean fourth power moment.

</details>


### [116] [Generalizations of the Christoffel-Darboux formula and congruences involving Apéry-like numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13192)
*Zhi-Hong Sun*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文首先将正交多项式的Christoffel-Darboux公式推广到一般三递推序列，然后研究了由两个特定的三项递推关系定义的序列g_n(x)和v_n(x)的恒等式与同余性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Christoffel-Darboux公式是正交多项式理论中的核心工具，但传统的该公式仅适用于正交多项式。本文希望将其推广到更一般的三递推（三项递推）序列，并利用这一推广来研究两类特殊递推序列（g_n(x)和v_n(x)）的恒等式与同余性质，这些序列在组合学中可能与阶乘、调和数、Legendre多项式等相关。

Method: 首先将Christoffel-Darboux公式从正交多项式推广至一般三递推序列；然后利用该推广公式以及可能结合母函数、超几何方法等工具，对满足给定递推关系的g_n(x)和v_n(x)进行系统分析，推导其恒等式和同余式。递推关系分别为二阶递推（系数含(n+1)^2）和三阶递推（系数含(n+1)^3），对应不同的系数增长阶。

Result: 给出了Christoffel-Darboux公式对一般三递推序列的推广形式，并基于此建立了g_n(x)和v_n(x)所满足的若干代数恒等式及模同余性质。

Conclusion: 本文在理论层面推广了经典的Christoffel-Darboux公式，使其适用于更一般的三递推序列，并由此获得了g_n(x)与v_n(x)的有趣恒等式和同余结果，为研究此类递推序列的性质提供了新的工具和视角。

Abstract: In this paper, we first extend the Christoffel-Darboux formula for orthogonal polynomials to general three-term recurrence sequences, and then investigate the identities and congruences for $g_n(x)$ and $v_n(x)$ given by \begin{align*} &g_0(x)=1,\ g_1(x)=\frac{x+1}2,\ (n+1)^2g_{n+1}(x)=\Big(2n(n+1)+\frac{x+1}2\Big)g_n(x)-n^2g_{n-1}(x)\ (n\ge 1), \\&v_0(x)=1,\ v_1(x)=x,\ (n+1)^3v_{n+1}(x)=(2n+1)(n(n+1)+x)v_n(x)-n^3v_{n-1}(x)\ (n\ge 1).\end{align*}

</details>


### [117] [Infinitely many elliptic curves over $\mathbb{Q}(i)$ of exact ranks 4 and 6 with $j$-invariant 1728](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13202)
*Ben Savoie*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文为每个r∈{4,6}构造了定义在$\mathbb{Q}(i)$上的显式单参数椭圆曲线族，具有j-不变量1728且秩恰好为r，包含无穷多条彼此非同构的曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前作者已经完成了秩为2的类似构造，本文旨在将该策略推广到更高的秩（4和6），以证明在固定j-不变量的情况下，高斯有理数域$\mathbb{Q}(i)$上可以存在任意指定秩的椭圆曲线族。

Method: 通过构造显式的$\mathbb{Q}(i)$-有理点来给出秩的下界；利用Kai关于数域上线性模式素数值的定理对特化进行局部行为控制；采用$[1+i]$-下降（descent）方法获得秩的上界。在秩6情形中，三个构造点与$(0,0)$的支撑向量张成自对偶Reed-Muller码$\mathrm{RM}(1,3)$，该码同时作为控制Selmer群的二次剩余Laplacian的核出现。

Result: 成功构造了秩恰好为4和6的显式椭圆曲线族，这些曲线定义在$\mathbb{Q}(i)$上且j-不变量为1728，下界与上界通过下降方法匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作将作者之前的秩2构造推广至秩4和6，展示了通过将对称高斯素数配置替换为满足多个互补平方恒等式的二元线性形式系统，可以在高斯数域上构造具有任意指定秩的椭圆曲线族。

Abstract: For each $r\in\{4,6\}$, we construct an explicit one-parameter family of elliptic curves over $\mathbb{Q}(i)$ containing infinitely many pairwise nonisomorphic curves genuinely defined over $\mathbb{Q}(i)$ with $j$-invariant $1728$ and rank exactly $r$. We construct explicit $\mathbb{Q}(i)$-rational points to bound the ranks from below. Kai's theorem on prime values of linear patterns over number fields provides specializations with controlled local behavior, allowing us to obtain matching upper bounds via $[1+i]$-descent. The construction extends the strategy of the author's earlier rank-$2$ paper by replacing a symmetric Gaussian-prime configuration with systems of binary linear forms satisfying several complementary square identities. In the rank-$6$ case, the support vectors attached to the three constructed points and $(0,0)$ span the self-dual Reed-Muller code $\mathrm{RM}(1,3)$, which also occurs as the kernel of the quadratic-residue Laplacian governing the Selmer group.

</details>


### [118] [Asymptotic Analysis and Phase Transition of the Bessel-Kuznetsov Transform with an Oscillatory Phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13232)
*Yuhang Shi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对GL(2)的Kuznetsov迹公式中Bessel-Kuznetsov积分变换在高度振荡线性相位条件下的半经典极限进行了严格的显式渐近分析，发现了一个以α=1/2π为临界点的尖锐相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典界保证光滑非振荡测试函数的Bessel-Kuznetsov变换快速衰减，但现代解析数论应用（特别是扭曲移位卷积和）常涉及高度振荡的线性相位e(αx)，需要理解该变换在此条件下的渐近行为。

Method: 将WKB近似应用于虚阶Bessel核，对Bessel-Kuznetsov积分变换在t→∞的半经典极限下进行严格的显式渐近分析。

Result: 识别出依赖扭曲参数α的尖锐相变：在次临界区域（α≤1/2π）变换快速衰减；在超临界区域（α>1/2π），几何振荡与谱核共振，产生阶为O(t^{-1})的局部主项，并伴随简化的算术相位。

Conclusion: 该结果为振荡测试函数下Kuznetsov迹公式的谱侧行为提供了完整的描述，填补了经典快速衰减界在振荡情形下不适用的空白，对扭曲移位卷积和相关解析数论问题具有重要意义。

Abstract: The spectral side of the Kuznetsov trace formula for $GL(2)$ is governed by the Bessel-Kuznetsov integral transform $\checkφ(t)$. While classical bounds guarantee rapid decay of this transform for smooth, non-oscillatory test functions, modern applications in analytic number theory---particularly those involving twisted shifted convolution sums---frequently encounter test functions exhibiting a highly oscillatory linear phase $e(αx)$. In this paper, we provide a rigorous and explicit asymptotic analysis of $\checkφ(t)$ in the semiclassical limit $t \to \infty$ under such oscillatory conditions. By applying the WKB approximation to the imaginary-order Bessel kernel, we identify a sharp phase transition dependent on the twist parameter $α$. We prove that in the sub-critical regime ($α\le 1/2π$), the transform decays rapidly. Conversely, in the super-critical regime ($α> 1/2π$), the geometric oscillations resonate with the spectral kernel, yielding a localized main term of order $O(t^{-1})$ with a remarkably simplified arithmetic phase.

</details>


### [119] [On a conjecture of Corradi and Katai](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13266)
*Krishnarjun Krishnamoorthy*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了Liouville函数对应的Goldbach型求和，证明了其中存在足够的抵消，并将其应用于Liouville函数的符号模式分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goldbach猜想是数论中的经典问题，而Liouville函数的求和性质与素数分布和解析数论中的齐性分布问题密切相关。作者希望通过Goldbach型和来理解Liouville函数的抵消行为，进而揭示其符号模式的深层结构。

Method: 论文采用解析数论的方法，考虑Liouville函数对应的Goldbach型求和（即对两个数之和形式求和并乘以Liouville函数），通过估计这些和的大小来证明存在足够的抵消。随后利用这些抵消结果来研究Liouville函数的符号模式。

Result: 成功证明了Liouville函数的Goldbach型和中存在充分的抵消，并将该结果应用到Liouville函数的符号模式研究上，得到了相应的推论。

Conclusion: 该研究加深了对Liouville函数乘性结构和符号模式的理解，为后续研究Liouville函数与Goldbach类型问题的关联提供了新的分析工具和视角。

Abstract: We consider Goldbach type sums corresponding to the Liouville function and prove the existence of sufficient cancellations. We also consider applications to sign patterns in the Liouville function.

</details>


### [120] [Convolution-type Bombieri-Vinogradov theorem with well-factorable Weights, and its applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13299)
*Zhiyuan Yang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用Pascadi的三重良因子卷积估计和不完全Kloosterman和估计，将Bombieri-Friedlander-Iwaniec经典素数等分布结果推广到卷积形式，改进了Wang的结果，并应用于两个数论问题：证明了n≤x中P^+(n)<P^+(n+1)的密度大于0.296，以及P^+(p-1)≥p^c的素数密度上界S(c)，分别改进了作者此前0.280的结果和Ding-Wang的7/2·log(1/c)结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bombieri-Friedlander-Iwaniec于1986年利用良因子权证明了素数在模数达到x^{4/7-ε}的算术级数中等分布。本文旨在将这一经典结果推广到卷积形式，并以此为工具改进两个相关数论问题（连续整数最大素因子比较、素数平移的最大素因子下界）的已知结果。

Method: 本文采用Pascadi建立的三重良因子卷积估计与不完全Kloosterman和估计，将BFI经典定理推广至卷积形式，从而在更广泛的条件下改进了Wang的卷积形式结果。随后将该卷积形式的等分布结果应用于两个具体数论问题：通过解析数论中的筛法与密度估计，推导出P^+(n)<P^+(n+1)的渐近密度下界0.296，以及P^+(p-1)≥p^c对应素数比例的上界函数S(c)。

Result: 得到两个主要结果：(1) 对于x→∞，满足P^+(n)<P^+(n+1)的n≤x的数量大于0.296x，改进了作者此前0.280的结论；(2) 对于0.7404<c<1，limsup_{x→∞}(1/π(x))·#{p≤x:P^+(p-1)≥p^c}≤S(c)，其中S(c)在c∈[184/189,1)时定义为积分形式，在c∈(0.7404,184/189]时定义为分段函数，且S(c)<7/2·log(1/c)，改进了Ding-Wang(2025)的7/2·log(1/c)上界。

Conclusion: 本文通过将BFI定理推广到卷积形式，成功改进了连续整数最大素因子比较问题和王的结果，以及在素数平移最大素因子下限问题上的Ding-Wang结果。这表明Pascadi的三重良因子卷积估计在素数等分布问题上具有更强的适用性和更强的数值结果。

Abstract: In 1986, Bombieri, Friedlander and Iwaniec famously obtained that primes are equidistributed in arithmetic progressions to moduli up to $x^{4/7-\varepsilon}$, using well-factorable weights. In this paper, we apply Pascadi's triply-well-factorable convolution estimate and his estimation of incomplete Kloosterman sums to generalize this result to a convolutionform, which improves Wang's result under certain conditions. As for application, we consider the asymptotic density of $\#\{n\leq x:P^+(n)<P^+(n+1)\}$ and $\#\{p\leq x:P^+(p-1)\geq p^c\}$, where $P^+(n)$ denote the largest prime factor of $n$. We show that for $x\rightarrow\infty$, one has\begin{align*}
  \#\{n\leq x:P^+(n)<P^+(n+1)\}>0.296x
  \end{align*}
  and
  \begin{align*}
  &\mathop{\lim\sup}_{x\rightarrow\infty} \frac{1}{π(x)}\#\{p\leq x:P^+(p-1)\geq p^c\}\\&\leq S(c)=\left\{ \begin{aligned}
  & \int_0^{1-c}\frac{2}{(5/8-189u/200)(1-u)}\mathrm{d}u, \quad&& \frac{184}{189}\leq c<1,\\& S\left(\frac{184}{189}\right)+\frac{10}{3}\log\frac{184}{189c},\quad&& 0.7404<c\leq \frac{184}{189},
  \end{aligned}\right.
  \end{align*}
  where the function $S(c)$ satisfties $S(c)<\frac{7}{2}\log\frac{1}{c} $ for $0.7404<c<1$. The first result improves a previous result $0.280$ by the author (2026). The second result constitutes an improvement upon that of Ding and Wang (2025), who obatined $\mathop{\lim\sup}_{x\rightarrow\infty} \frac{1}{π(x)}\#\{p\leq x:P^+(p-1)\geq p^c\}\leq \frac{7}{2}\log\frac{1}{c}$.

</details>


### [121] [Local Minkowski units in non-abelian extensions with cyclic Sylow $p$-subgroups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13312)
*Wan Lee,Donghyeok Lim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个判定局部Minkowski单位在p处存在性的准则，适用于Galois扩张且其Galois群同构于非p-群与循环p-群的直积的情形。作为应用，构造了在各种分歧条件下允许p处局部Minkowski单位的非交换扩张，并分析了数域Zp-扩张中单位的Iwasawa模结构，同时将研究推广到p次单位根群非平凡的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部Minkowski单位是代数数论中与单位群结构、分歧理论和Iwasawa理论密切相关的重要对象。此前对其存在性的判定缺乏对Galois群为更一般形式（非p-群与循环p-群的直积）的扩张的完整刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过建立局部Minkowski单位存在性的判定准则，利用Galois群直积结构进行分解分析；结合Iwasawa模理论研究Zp-扩张中单位的结构；并将方法推广到p次单位根群非平凡的情况。

Result: 得到了对所有Galois群同构于非p-群与循环p-群直积的扩张均适用的局部Minkowski单位存在性准则，构造了满足该性质的多种非交换扩张，并揭示了Zp-扩张中单位Iwasawa模的相应结构性质。

Conclusion: 本文系统解决了特定类Galois扩张中局部Minkowski单位的存在性问题，其准则和构造方法在分歧条件和单位模结构分析方面具有广泛适用性，并为后续研究p次单位根群非平凡情形提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We establish a criterion for the existence of a local Minkowski unit at $p$ that applies to all Galois extensions with Galois group isomorphic to the direct product of a non-$p$-group and a cyclic $p$-group. As applications, we construct non-abelian extensions admitting a local Minkowski unit at $p$ under various ramification conditions and analyze the Iwasawa module structure of units in $\mathbb{Z}_p$-extensions of number fields. We also extend our study to the case where the group of $p$-power roots of unity is nontrivial.

</details>


### [122] [Some convolution identities for mock modular forms arising from the theory of holomorphic projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13324)
*Jonathan G. Bradley-Thrush,Frank Garvan,Jayashree Kalita,Larry Rolen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过推广全纯投影的理论，将Imamoğlu-Raum-Richter关于调和Maass形式与全纯模形式乘积的结果从Pell方程D为平方数的情形推广到一般D的情形，并由此得到18个mock theta函数的卷积恒等式（可归结为5个基本恒等式），同时解决了一个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积恒等式和递推公式在模形式理论及组合学中有重要应用。近年来，非全纯模形式（如调和Maass形式）中的卷积公式引起了新的关注。Imamoğlu、Raum和Richter在2014年建立了全纯投影理论来处理此类问题，但他们的方法仅覆盖Pell方程m²-Dn²=N中D为平方数（即N给定时仅有有限个解）的情形。本文旨在处理一般D的情形，特别是有无穷多解的Pell方程情形，并由此解决相关猜想、发现新的卷积恒等式。

Method: 本文发展了一种新的全纯投影方法，推广了Imamoğlu-Raum-Richter的框架，使其适用于一般D的情形（包括Pell方程有无限多解的情况）。通过这一方法，作者推导出18个mock theta函数的卷积恒等式，并将其还原为5个可以直接相互推导的基本恒等式。此外，附录中还用更直接的q级数方法给出了这些恒等式的替代证明。

Result: 主要结果是：(1) 建立了适用于一般D的情形的全纯投影公式，覆盖了Pell方程有无穷多解的情形；(2) 解决了第二作者的近期猜想；(3) 得到了18个mock theta函数的卷积恒等式，并归结为5个基本恒等式；(4) 在附录中通过q级数方法给出了这些恒等式的直接证明，同时展示了全纯投影公式作为自动发现和验证此类公式的工具的实用性。

Conclusion: 本文通过推广全纯投影理论，显著拓展了卷积恒等式的适用范围，将此前仅适用于Pell方程D为平方数的理论推广到一般情形。所得恒等式不仅解决了具体猜想，还揭示了全纯投影公式作为自动发现和验证卷积恒等式的强大工具的价值，在mock theta函数、模形式论和相关组合学领域具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Convolution identities and recursive formulas have long played a role in the theory of holomorphic modular forms and their applications. These have served both as striking formulas and as fundamentally useful tools for applications to combinatorics. Recently, there has been renewed interest in examples arising from non-holomorphic modular forms. These include the famous Hurwitz-Kronecker class number relations dating to 1885, and groundbreaking work of Imamoğlu, Raum, and Richter from 2014. Imamoğlu, Raum, and Richter developed a theory of holomorphic projection for products of (vector-valued) harmonic Maass forms and holomorphic modular forms to produce many such formulas. This was related shortly thereafter by Duncan, Griffin, and Ono to replicable-type functions in the sense of Conway and Norton, and such recursions played a key role in their proof of the Umbral Moonshine Conjecture.
  Here, we develop new results on holomorphic projections of such functions which is more convenient for many natural cases. Imamoğlu, Raum, and Richter's choice corresponds to the case in which the generalized Pell equation $m^2 - Dn^2 = N$ has a square value for $D$, which has only finitely many solutions for a given value of $N$. Our main results cover the cases of general $D$, in particular those for which the Pell equation has infinitely many solutions.
  As an application, we resolve a recent conjecture of the second author. More generally, our approach yields 18 convolution identities for mock theta functions, which we boil down to 5 identities that can directly be used to prove the others. In the appendices, we also show how these identities can be proven by more direct $q$-series methods; however, the key utility of the holomorphic projection formulas is that they give a tool to automatically discover and verify such formulas.

</details>


### [123] [Gross vectors modulo 2 and elliptic curves of prime conductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13371)
*Matija Kazalicki,Siniša Slijepčević*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个奇偶性定理：对素数p>3，范德Hoeven的Gross向量系数模2张成F_2^{S_p}，由此证明Kazalicki-Kohen猜想（正秩椭圆曲线的Brandt特征向量所有系数为偶数），并推出正秩、素数导子、根数+1的椭圆曲线模次数被4整除，从而这些秩2曲线满足Watkins猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超奇异椭圆曲线与模形式、Brandt矩阵和Gross向量的奇偶性之间存在深层联系。作者旨在建立Gross向量奇偶性的满秩性质，并利用这一代数结构证明关于椭圆曲线秩、Brandt特征向量系数以及模次数的若干猜想（Kazalicki-Kohen猜想和Watkins猜想）。

Method: 关键步骤是将Gross三元格型的表示数奇偶性归结为垂直于Frobenius的秩二子格的表示。利用Ibukiyama的显式极大阶，将所得本原二元二次型与Xiao-Zhou-Deng-Qu对F_p上超奇异椭圆曲线的参数化中出现的二元型进行对照。再借助类域论和Chebotarev定理孤立出各个超奇异坐标。最后结合Mestre和Gross-Kudla的公式处理模次数部分。

Result: 证明了(m_i(D) mod 2)向量张成整个F_2^{S_p}；作为推论，任何具有素数导子p和正Mordell-Weil秩的椭圆曲线E/Q，其按S_p指标排列的Brandt特征向量所有系数均为偶数，证实了Kazalicki-Kohen猜想；进一步证明这类根数+1曲线的模次数被4整除，从而秩2情形满足Watkins猜想。

Conclusion: 该奇偶性定理不仅统一并证明了若干重要猜想，还提供了新的代数判据：有理超奇异类上出现奇系数可作为秩为0的代数证明。这项工作在算术统计、模形式与超奇异几何之间建立了深刻的联系。

Abstract: Let p > 3 be a prime, and let S_p denote the geometric isomorphism classes of supersingular elliptic curves in characteristic p whose j-invariants lie in F_p. For each negative fundamental discriminant -D for which p is inert in Q(sqrt(-D)), let m_i(D), i in S_p, be the integral coefficients of the corresponding Gross vector. We prove that the vectors (m_i(D) mod 2)_{i in S_p} span F_2^{S_p}. The key step reduces the parity of the representation numbers of Gross's ternary lattices to representation by rank-two sublattices perpendicular to Frobenius. Using Ibukiyama's explicit maximal orders, the resulting primitive binary forms are identified with those occurring in the Xiao--Zhou--Deng--Qu parametrization of supersingular elliptic curves over F_p. Class field theory and Chebotarev's theorem then allow the individual supersingular coordinates to be isolated.
  As a consequence, if E/Q has prime conductor p and positive Mordell--Weil rank, then every coefficient of its Brandt eigenvector indexed by S_p is even, proving a conjecture of Kazalicki and Kohen. Thus an odd coefficient at a rational supersingular class is an algebraic certificate of rank 0. Combining this parity theorem with formulas of Mestre and Gross--Kudla, we also prove that the modular degree of every positive-rank elliptic curve of prime conductor and root number +1 is divisible by 4. Consequently, Watkins' conjecture holds for all such curves of rank 2.

</details>


### [124] [Intersective Polynomials and Universal Separation of Divosor Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13388)
*Zihan Zhang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了互素多项式对以及低次多项式对的通用除子分布分离问题，给出了分离发生的充要条件，并通过代数数论与自适应局部路由论证提供了完整刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 除子函数沿多项式取值时的分布分离（divisor-profile separation）是解析数论中的重要问题。作者希望精确刻画在何种条件下两个多项式沿整数取值的除子计数函数可以普遍地分离，特别是区分可分离与不可分离的边界条件，并推广到含公共不可约因子的情形。

Method: 方法包括：利用root modulo progression上的一致几乎素数（uniform almost-prime）结果；结合自适应的局部路由论证（adaptive local-routing argument）处理p-adic边界情形；借助伽罗瓦作用是否具有derangement来判断UV是否在几乎所有素数模下都有根；对不同次数（特别是不超过二次）的多项式对进行分类讨论。

Result: 对于互素多项式对：普遍无界分离当且仅当其中一个多项式是intersective（即模每个正整数都有根）。对于含公共不可约因子的一般情形，分离强制UV在几乎所有素数模下有根（等价于伽罗瓦作用无derangement）。对于所有次数至多2的配对，分离的充要条件是UV及F、G中至少一个为intersective，且此判别与内容（content）和因子重数无关。此外，任意次数下三个线性支撑因子带任意正整数重数的相同判别同样成立。

Conclusion: 作者完整解决了互素多项式对任意次数的通用分离分类，以及所有次数至多2的多项式对的分离判别问题，并推广至多个线性因子情形。核心结论是分离性由intersective性质决定，且通过模素数根的存在性（伽罗瓦derangement条件）刻画。

Abstract: We classify universal divisor-profile separation for coprime polynomial pairs of arbitrary degree and for all pairs of degree at most two. For \(A\subset\N\) and \(m\in\Z\), let \(d_A(m)\) count the members of \(A\) dividing \(m\). For coprime nonzero \(F,G\in\Z[x]\), universally unbounded separation between \(d_A(F(n))\) and \(d_A(G(n))\) occurs if and only if one of \(F,G\) is intersective, that is, has a root modulo every positive integer. More generally, an intersective factor separated from finitely many polynomial opponents yields simultaneous one-sided dominance against all of them.
  For pairs with common irreducible factors, let \(U,V\) be the products of the factors occurring only on the two respective sides. Universal separation forces \(UV\) to have a root modulo almost every prime; equivalently, its Galois action has no derangement. We resolve the remaining finite \(p\)-adic boundary for all pairs of degree at most two: separation holds exactly when \(UV\) and at least one of \(F,G\) are intersective, and the criterion is unchanged by contents or factor multiplicities. The same criterion holds, in arbitrary degree, for three linear support factors with arbitrary positive multiplicities. The proofs combine uniform almost-prime values on root progressions with an adaptive local-routing argument.

</details>


### [125] [The distribution of $k$-free ideals in ray class groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13509)
*Adrian Barquero-Sanchez,Jack Heimrath,Bernd Sing,Nicolás Sirolli,Caylee Spivey,Michael Wijaya*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文将k-free整数在算术级数中分布这一经典问题推广到任意数域的背景下，利用射线类群的语言建立了有界范数的k-free理想在给定射线类中数量的渐近公式（含误差项），证明k-free理想在射线类间均匀分布，并通过对理想类群和窄类群使用Landau更精确的理想计数渐近获得了改进的误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典数论中长期研究k-free整数在算术级数中的分布问题（可追溯至Gegenbauer和Cohen-Robinson的结果），但此前在数域上的推广（如Benkowski、Nymann和Sittinger的工作）仅限于理想类群或窄类群的框架。本文的动机是将该问题进一步推广到更一般的射线类群设置中，从而能够处理模（模理想）的细化信息，同时探索是否能利用更精确的Landau理想计数渐近来改进误差项。

Method: 采用射线类群的语言将算术级数的概念推广到数域中；通过建立k-free理想与Möbius型函数之间的关联，结合Dirichlet级数和Perron公式等解析数论工具，推导出k-free理想在射线类中计数的渐近公式；在理想类群和窄类群的特殊情形下，利用Landau更精细的理想计数渐近结果来缩减误差项。

Result: 建立了任意数域中k-free理想在射线类中的渐近计数公式并给出了明确的误差项，证明了k-free理想在射线类间是均匀分布的；在理想类群和窄类群情形下获得了改进的误差估计；所获结果统一恢复并推广了经典（Q上）和前人（数域上理想类群/窄类群框架）的结论，并辅以具体的数值算例验证。

Conclusion: 本文完满地解决了k-free理想在任意数域射线类中分布的均匀性问题，不仅推广了经典Gegenbauer与Cohen-Robinson公式以及Benkowski、Nymann、Sittinger的结果，还通过Landau渐近改进了特殊情形下的误差估计，为类群框架下的平方自由/无幂因子理想计数提供了统一的解析处理方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend the classical problem of studying the distribution of $k$-free integers in arithmetic progressions to the setting of arbitrary number fields. Using the language of ray class groups, we establish asymptotic formulas, together with error terms, for the number of $k$-free ideals of bounded norm lying in a given ray class. In particular, our results show that $k$-free ideals are equidistributed among ray classes. We also obtain improved error estimates in the cases of ideal class groups and narrow class groups by using sharper ideal counting asymptotics due to Landau. Our results recover the classical formulas of Gegenbauer and Cohen--Robinson over $\mathbb{Q}$ and extend previous work of Benkowski, Nymann, and Sittinger to the setting of ray class groups. We also present explicit computational examples that illustrate the asymptotic formulas and the equidistribution of $k$-free ideals among ray classes.

</details>

### [126] [Amplitude analysis of $B^+ \!\to K^+ π^+ π^-$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12612)
*LHCb collaboration,R. Aaij,M. Abdelfatah,A. S. W. Abdelmotteleb,C. Abellan Beteta,F. Abudinén,T. Ackernley,A. A. Adefisoye,B. Adeva,M. Adinolfi,P. Adlarson,C. Agapopoulou,C. A. Aidala,Z. Ajaltouni,S. Akar,K. Akiba,P. Albicocco,J. Albrecht,R. Aleksiejunas,F. Alessio,P. Alvarez Cartelle,R. Amalric,S. Amato,J. L. Amey,Y. Amhis,L. An,L. Anderlini,M. Andersson,P. Andreola,M. Andreotti,S. Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文通过LHCb探测器在√s=7和8 TeV的质子-质子对撞数据（积分亮度3 fb⁻¹）中，对无粲三体衰变B⁺→K⁺π⁺π⁻进行振幅分析，报告了中间共振态的分支比和准两体CP破坏不对称度。采用三种互补方法（isobar近似、K-matrix形式、准模型无关程序）精确描述主导的K⁺π⁻和π⁺π⁻ S波贡献，并给出ω(782)直接衰变与ρ(770)⁰-ω(782)混合两种替代结果，为大多数中间态提供了最高精度的分支比和CP不对称测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三体无粲B介子衰变是研究强相互作用动力学和寻找超越标准模型新物理的重要渠道，而B⁺→K⁺π⁺π⁻衰变中复杂的S波贡献（尤其是K⁺π⁻和π⁺π⁻通道）是振幅建模中最具挑战性的部分，需要精确建模以提取可靠的物理参数。

Method: 基于LHCb探测器采集的3 fb⁻¹数据，对B⁺→K⁺π⁺π⁻衰变进行振幅分析。采用三种互补的建模方法：①基于isobar近似的物理动机解析模型；②K-matrix形式主义；③准模型无关方法，同时研究重叠交叉分波。此外还考虑了π⁺π⁻末态通过ω(782)直接衰变或ρ(770)⁰-ω(782)混合表现的替代方案。

Result: 获得了绝大多数中间态的分支比和CP不对称度的最精确测量值，建立了更坚实的参考基准。三种建模方法的结果相互一致，为探测模型无关的超越标准模型新物理提供了干净检验平台，并为强动力学和B⁺→K⁺π⁺π⁻中CP破坏效应的来源提供了新见解。

Conclusion: 三种互补的振幅建模方法得到一致的结果，验证了物理分析的可靠性；所提供的高精度分支比与CP不对称性测量可作为未来模型无关新物理搜索的基准参考，同时深化了对无粲B衰变中强动力学与CP破坏机制的理解。

Abstract: The branching fractions and quasi-two-body $C\!P$-violating asymmetries of intermediate states obtained through an amplitude analysis of the charmless three-body decay $B^+ \!\to K^+ π^+ π^-$ are reported. The analysis is based on $pp$ collision data at centre-of-mass energies $\sqrt{s}=7$ and $8\,\text{TeV}$ recorded with the LHCb detector, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $3\,\text{fb}^{-1}$. The most challenging aspect of the amplitude modelling lies in the description of the dominant $K^+ π^-$ and $π^+ π^-$ S-wave contributions. This is achieved by three complementary approaches based on a physically motivated analytic model built on the isobar approximation, the K-matrix formalism, and a quasi-model-independent procedure in which overlapping crossing partial waves are simultaneously studied. In addition, alternative sets of results are presented, considering the $π^+ π^-$ final state to manifest either through direct $ω(782)$ decays or $ρ(770)^0\textrm{-}ω(782)$ mixing. The most precise measurements of branching fractions and $C\!P$ asymmetries are obtained for the vast majority of intermediate states, establishing firmer reference points against which to cleanly probe model-independent physics beyond the Standard Model. The results from all three approaches agree and provide new insight into strong dynamics and the origin of $C\!P$-violation effects in $B^+ \!\to K^+ π^+ π^-$ decays.

</details>


### [127] [Resolution of outstanding puzzles in $B^+ \!\to K^+ π^+ π^-$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12613)

Zhuo,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于LHCb探测器在2011-2012年收集的3 fb⁻¹ pp碰撞数据，对B⁺→K⁺π⁺π⁻衰变进行了振幅分析。通过放宽K*₀(1430)⁰共振与K⁺π⁻中缓慢变化标量部分之间基于幺正性的假设，显著改善了模型与数据的一致性。该分析推翻了B→K*₀(1430)π衰变主导B→Kππ相空间的实验共识，转而支持QCD因子化而非微扰QCD的预测；同时无需引入此前分析中ad-hoc的"f_X(1300)"成分即可建模标量π⁺π⁻振幅，最终发现了十个中间衰变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前对B→Kππ扇区的研究在S波贡献的建模上遗留了关键未解问题，需要更灵活的模型来与实验数据达成更好的一致性。

Method: 利用LHCb探测器在2011-2012年收集的pp碰撞数据（积分亮度3 fb⁻¹），对B⁺→K⁺π⁺π⁻衰变进行全振幅分析，关键改进在于放宽K*₀(1430)⁰共振与缓慢变化标量K⁺π⁻部分之间基于幺正性假设的关系，并对标量π⁺π⁻振幅使用已知态进行建模。

Result: 放宽假设后模型与数据吻合度显著提升；B⁺→K*₀(1430)⁰π⁺分支比与QCD因子化预测一致而非微扰QCD，逆转了此前测量的结论；无需ad-hoc的f_X(1300)成分即可建模标量π⁺π⁻振幅；发现十个中间衰变。

Conclusion: 该分析解决了B→Kππ扇区中S波建模的历史遗留问题，修改了对K*₀(1430)⁰衰变贡献的认识，为理解该衰变道的物理提供了更清晰的图景。

Abstract: An amplitude analysis of $B^+ \!\to K^+ π^+ π^-$ decays is presented, based on $pp$ collision data recorded with the LHCb detector in 2011--2012, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $3\,\text{fb}^{-1}$. Previous studies of the $B \!\to K ππ$ sector have left key unresolved questions concerning the model of the S-wave contributions. A pivotal finding is that relaxing unitarity-based assumptions about the relation between the $K^*_0(1430)^0$ resonance and the slowly varying scalar part in $K^+π^-$ leads to considerably better agreement between the model and data. The $B^+ \!\to K^*_0(1430)^0 π^+$ branching fraction now challenges the experimental consensus that $B \!\to K^*_0(1430) π$ decays dominate the $B \!\to K ππ$ phase space, aligning with the predictions of QCD factorisation rather than perturbative QCD, thus reversing the agreement found in previous measurements. With this increased flexibility, it also becomes possible to model the scalar $π^+ π^-$ amplitude using established states, eliminating the need for the ad-hoc ``$f_X(1300)$'' component included in previous analyses of the $B \!\to Kππ$ sector. These advances facilitate the discovery of ten intermediate decays.

</details>


### [128] [Observation of several sources of $C\!P$ violation in $B^+ \!\to K^+ π^+ π^-$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12614)

Zhuo,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于LHCb在2011-2012年采集的pp碰撞数据（积分亮度3 fb⁻¹），对B⁺ → K⁺π⁺π⁻衰变进行了幅度分析，首次确认了六种CP破坏现象，包括发现B⁺→ρ(770)⁰K⁺衰变中的准两体CP破坏、确立B⁺→f₂(1270)K⁺衰变中幅度级别的CP破坏，以及在全弹性S波ππ-ππ重散射区域和涉及自旋-3共振的衰变中首次报告CP破坏的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CP破坏是理解宇宙物质-反物质不对称性的关键，而多体B介子衰变因其丰富的动力学结构为研究CP破坏的不同机制（准两体、幅度级别、干涉效应等）提供了独特窗口。此前尚未在B⁺→K⁺π⁺π⁻衰变的多个通道中系统性地揭示这些CP破坏现象。

Method: 利用LHCb探测器采集的pp碰撞数据进行全动力学幅度分析（amplitude analysis），对B⁺→K⁺π⁺π⁻衰变中的不同贡献成分（包括ρ(770)⁰、f₂(1270)等中间态以及不同ππ分波之间的干涉）进行完整建模，通过拟合数据检验各成分中的CP不对称性。

Result: 首次确认六种CP破坏现象：1) 发现B⁺→ρ(770)⁰K⁺中的准两体CP破坏；2) 确立B⁺→f₂(1270)K⁺中的幅度级CP破坏；3) 在全弹性S波ππ-ππ重散射区域首次报告CP破坏证据；4) 在涉及自旋-3共振的衰变中首次报告CP破坏证据；5) 在S-P波干涉中观测到显著的CP破坏效应；6) 在S-D波干涉中找到CP破坏证据。

Conclusion: 这些结果首次在多体B衰变中系统性揭示了多种不同来源的CP破坏效应，其中S-P和S-D波干涉中的CP破坏必须由长程相互作用驱动，这对于理解强相互作用非微扰效应在CP破坏中的角色具有重要意义，也为后续更精确的CP破坏研究和标准模型检验提供了新方向。

Abstract: An amplitude analysis of $B^+ \!\to K^+ π^+ π^-$ decays is presented in which six $C\!P$-violating phenomena are judged to be of significance for the first time. This analysis is based on $pp$ collision data recorded with the LHCb detector in 2011-2012, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $3\,\text{fb}^{-1}$. Quasi-two-body $C\!P$ violation in $B^+ \!\to ρ(770)^0 K^+$ decays is discovered, while $C\!P$ violation at amplitude level is established in $B^+ \!\to f_2(1270) K^+$ decays. First evidence for $C\!P$ violation is reported in both the fully elastic S-wave $ππ$-$ππ$ rescattering region and also for any decay involving a spin-3 resonance. Additionally, significant $C\!P$-violation effects are identified in the interference between different $ππ$ partial waves, with observation in S-P wave interference and evidence in S-D wave interference, both of which must be driven by long-distance interactions.

</details>


### [129] [Axion-Like Particle Search with a Hybrid Cherenkov-Scintillation Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12616)
*A. A. Aguilar-Arevalo,S. Biedron,J. Boissevain,M. Borrego,L. Bugel,M. Chavez-Estrada,J. M. Conrad,R. L. Cooper,J. R. Distel,J. C. D'Olivo,E. Dunton,B. Dutta,D. E. Fields,M. Gold,E. Guardincerri,E. C. Huang,N. Kamp,D. Kim,K. Knickerbocker,W. C. Louis,C. F. Macias-Acevedo,R. Mahapatra,J. Mezzetti,J. Mirabal,M. J. Mocko,D. A. Newmark,P. deNiverville,C. O'Connor,V. Pandey,D. Poulson,H. Ray,E. Renner,T. J. Schaub,A. Schneider,M. H. Shaevitz,D. Smith,W. Sondheim,A. M. Szelc,C. Taylor,A. Thompson,W. H. Thompson,R. T. Thornton,M. Tripathi,R. Van Berg,R. G. Van de Water,J. Zettlemoyer*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文展示了在束流倾倒设施上利用混合切伦科夫-闪烁体探测器进行轴子类粒子（ALPs）搜索的首次概念验证研究。基于CCM200探测器，结合纳米级时间分辨率和波长位移涂层实现的光学鉴别，构建了四个观测变量并组合成似然比分类器以抑制稳态背景。在0.001~10 MeV质量范围内未观察到显著超出，但相比先前CCM120搜索，在更少曝光量下实现了更优的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索ALPs作为超出标准模型的新物理候选粒子，并首次验证混合切伦科夫-闪烁体探测技术在束流倾倒实验中的物理潜力，克服传统探测器在背景抑制方面的局限。

Method: 利用CCM200探测器（10吨液态氩，200个PMT，80%涂覆波长位移材料），通过纳米级时间分辨区分瞬发切伦科夫辐射和闪烁光；构建四个基于切伦科夫发射和事件拓扑的观测量，并组合为似然比分类器进行背景压制。

Result: 在0.001至10 MeV的ALP质量范围内未观察到超过预期背景的显著统计超出；但得益于混合探测带来的背景抑制能力，在曝光量更少的情况下仍超越了此前CCM120搜索的灵敏度。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了混合切伦科夫-闪烁体探测器在ALP搜索中的可行性及其物理潜力，为此类探测器未来在更广泛的新物理搜索中的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: This analysis presents the first proof-of-concept search for axion-like particles (ALPs) using a hybrid Cherenkov-scintillation detector at a beam-dump facility. The work is based on the Coherent CAPTAIN-Mills (CCM) experiment, a 10-ton liquid argon light collection detector located at Los Alamos National Laboratory. The CCM200 detector is instrumented with 200 photomultiplier tubes (PMTs), providing approximately 50% photocathode coverage. To enable optical discrimination, 80% of the PMTs are coated with a wavelength-shifting material while the remaining 20% are left uncoated. This configuration, combined with nanosecond-scale timing resolution, provides sensitivity to prompt Cherenkov radiation while maintaining efficient detection of liquid argon scintillation light [1, 2]. This analysis constructs four observables that exploit the Cherenkov emission and event topology expected from ALP-induced electromagnetic interactions. These observables are combined into a likelihood-ratio classifier that provides powerful rejection of steady-state backgrounds. No statistically significant excess above the expected background prediction is observed for $10^{-3}~\mathrm{MeV} < m_a < 10~\mathrm{MeV}$. Nevertheless, the improved background rejection enabled by hybrid Cherenkov-scintillation detection allows this analysis to surpass the sensitivity of the previous CCM120 search [3] despite less exposure and demonstrate the physics potential for hybrid Cherenkov-scintillation detectors.

</details>

Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该论文基于BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的20.3 fb⁻¹数据，在质心能量3.773 GeV下，利用单标签技术对双光子融合过程e⁺e⁻→e⁺e⁻η′的微分Born截面进行测量，从而高精度地提取了η′介子的单虚空间类跃迁形状因子Q²|F(Q²)|，覆盖Q²∈[0.1, 6.0] GeV²范围，在Q²<1.5 GeV²区域精度优于3%，并首次在Q²<0.3 GeV²区域给出了直接测定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量η′介子的单虚空间类跃迁形状因子对于检验量子色动力学（QCD）的低能行为、理解介子内部结构以及验证理论预言（如手征微扰论和光锥求和规则）具有重要意义。此前在低Q²区域缺乏高精度实验数据，特别是在Q²<0.3 GeV²区域尚无直接测定，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: 采用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上采集的20.3 fb⁻¹数据样本，在质心能量3.773 GeV下，通过单标签（single-tag）技术测量双光子融合过程e⁺e⁻→e⁺e⁻γγ*→e⁺e⁻η′的微分Born截面，其中仅探测一个散射轻子（标记光子为虚光子），从而提取η′介子的单虚空间类跃迁形状因子Q²|F(Q²)|。

Result: 测量覆盖Q²∈[0.1, 6.0] GeV²的动量转移范围，在Q²<1.5 GeV²区域内达到了优于3.0%的测量精度，具有前所未有的精确度，并首次在Q²<0.3 GeV²的低动量转移区域提供了直接测量结果。

Conclusion: 该工作为η′介子单虚空间类跃迁形状因子提供了高精度的实验测定，特别是在此前缺乏数据的低Q²区域填补了空白，为检验理论模型和理解强相互作用在低能标下的行为提供了重要实验约束。

Abstract: Using a data sample corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $20.3\ \text{fb}^{-1}$, collected with the BESIII detector at a center-of-mass energy of $3.773\ \text{GeV}$ at the BEPCII collider, we report a precision measurement of the product $Q^2|F(Q^2)|$, where $F(Q^2)$ is the single-virtual space-like transition form factor of the $η'$ meson and $Q^2$ is the squared momentum transfer of the tagged virtual photon. The transition form factor is extracted from the differential Born cross section of the two-photon fusion processes $e^+e^- \to e^+e^-γγ^* \to e^+e^-η^\prime$ using a single-tag technique, where only one scattered lepton is detected. The measurement covers $Q^2 \in [0.1, 6.0]$ GeV$^2$, achieving unprecedented precision, better than $3.0\%$ for $Q^2 < 1.5$ GeV$^2$, and providing the first direct determination at $Q^2 < 0.3$ GeV$^2$.

</details>


### [131] [MANGO: An Autodiff Neutrino Oscillation Engine for Differentiable Analysis Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13429)
*Pierre Granger*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: MANGO是一个可组合的中微子振荡计算引擎，其中所有计算量对包括传播几何、探测器深度、各地球壳层密度在内的所有输入都是可微的。反向模式求导仅需2.5-3倍前向计算开销，使得对369个地球层析参数计算敏感度的成本等同于对6个标准振荡参数。该引擎支持多种传播模式，并能将梯度连续流动穿透探测器响应、事件加权、分箱及似然函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子振荡概率的计算已是一个已解决的问题，但其导数的计算尚未解决。传统解析概率公式无法涵盖传播几何、探测器深度和地球壳层密度等输入变量，这限制了对中微子物理模型中各参数灵敏度的分析，特别是地球层析成像和实验设计等应用场景。

Method: MANGO采用可组合的振荡引擎设计，所有输出对全部输入可微。支持真空、恒定密度、分层PREM、任意剖面和绝热太阳传播模式，并提供非标准相互作用、3+N无菌态、退相干和非幺正混合的前端。利用反向模式自动微分，其计算成本取决于输出而非输入维度。所有前向模型、BSM限制和微分路径均通过精确解析解和有限差分进行验证。

Result: MANGO计算369个地球密度、电子分数和壳层半径参数的敏感度的开销与计算6个标准振荡参数持平。三味和分层地球概率与外部基准（OscProb、NuFast-Earth）在10^-9到10^-5精度内一致。在风格化的地球层析分析中，单次通过即计算六区径向密度模型边缘化不确定性，并通过逆Fisher矩阵的微分评估探测器角度分辨率的敏感性。

Conclusion: MANGO通过使中微子振荡全链路可微，提供了传统解析概率公式无法达到的实验设计度量，能够支持从概率阶段连续流动到探测器响应、事件加权和似然的梯度信号，为中微子物理灵敏度分析和实验优化开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Computing neutrino oscillation probabilities is a solved problem; computing their derivatives is not. We present MANGO (MANGO: A Neutrino Gradient Oscillator), a composable oscillation engine in which every computed quantity is differentiable with respect to all inputs, including propagation geometry, detector depth, and individual Earth-shell densities. None of these quantities appear in traditional analytic probability formulas. The engine supports vacuum, constant-density, layered-PREM, arbitrary-profile, and adiabatic solar propagation, alongside front-ends for non-standard interactions, 3+N sterile states, decoherence, and non-unitary mixing. Because reverse-mode cost depends on output rather than input dimension, evaluating sensitivities incurs a constant 2.5-3x forward-pass overhead. As a result, calculating sensitivities for all 369 density, electron-fraction, and shell-radius parameters of a layered Earth costs no more than the 6 standard oscillation parameters. By maintaining exact sensitivity signals, MANGO allows gradients to flow continuously past the probability stage and through detector response, event weighting, binning, and likelihoods. We demonstrate this on a stylized Earth-tomography analysis. In a single pass, MANGO computes the marginalized uncertainty on a six-zone radial density model and, by differentiating through the inverse Fisher matrix, evaluates its sensitivity with respect to detector angular resolution. This provides an experimental-design metric unreachable using traditional analytic probability formulas. Three-flavor and layered-Earth probabilities match external benchmarks (OscProb, NuFast-Earth) to within 10^-9 to 10^-5, while all forward models, BSM limits, and differentiation paths are verified against exact analytic solutions and finite differences.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Distance Matrices of Ordered Point Clouds and Their Persistent Homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12620)
*David Hien*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文建立了时间序列的距离矩阵过滤与其状态空间嵌入的Čech（或Vietoris-Rips）过滤之间的度一链映射关系，研究了由此诱导的同调映射，并将其应用于简化图像持续性（image persistence）计算，以分析骑行签名（cycling signatures）中的不同运动类型转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 距离矩阵可视为热图，其子水平集即为时间序列分析中广泛使用的递归图。作者希望从持续的拓扑学视角，建立时间序列距离矩阵过滤与状态空间嵌入过滤之间的理论联系，并将此用于简化图像持续性计算中对骑行签名的分析。

Method: 论文在时间序列的距离矩阵过滤与状态空间嵌入的Čech/Vietoris-Rips过滤之间构造了一个度一链映射，并研究该映射诱导的同调映射；具体证明了从H_0到H_1的映射本质上是满射，并给出从H_1到H_2映射非平凡的示例。

Result: 建立了度一链映射并分析了其同调层面的性质：映射从H_0到H_1本质上为满射，从H_1到H_2可为非平凡。这些链映射被证明可简化时间序列拓扑分析工具（骑行签名）中图像持久性的计算，并能提供更精细的信息以区分不同类型的骑行运动转换。

Conclusion: 距离矩阵过滤与状态空间嵌入过滤之间的链映射为时间序列的拓扑分析提供了理论桥梁，不仅简化了图像持久性计算，还允许对骑行运动中不同类型运动之间的转换进行更细致的分析。

Abstract: The distance matrix of a finite point cloud can be visualized as a heatmap. When the data arise from a time series, the sublevel sets of this image are known as recurrence plots and are widely used in time series analysis. Motivated by this perspective, we establish a relationship between the distance-matrix filtration of the time series and the Čech (or Vietoris--Rips) filtration of its state-space embedding in the form of a degree-one chain map. We study the induced maps in homology, showing that the map from $H_0$ into $H_1$ is essentially surjective and providing an example where the map from $H_1$ into $H_2$ is nontrivial. These chain maps can be applied to simplify image persistence computations arising in the computation of cycling signatures, a topological tool for time series analysis. Moreover, these computations yield finer information that allows the analysis of transitions between different types of cycling motion.

</details>

### [133] [Stochastic Spatial Metapopulation Modelling of HPAI Control and Poultry Restocking on Jolly Island](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12956)
*Hammed O. Fatoyinbo,Indranil Ghosh,Parul Tiwari,Peter O. Olanipekun,Afeez Abidemi,Ryan H. L. Ip*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于随机空间SEIR的元种群模型，用于模拟虚构Jolly岛上高致病性禽流感（HPAI）疫情暴发，同时集成了疫情控制策略（预防性扑杀、早期关养）和恢复策略（容量限制的分阶段补栏），证明整合建模能有效降低疫情负担和复燃风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPAI暴发期间需要快速控制措施，同时需要对扑杀后的农场安全补栏做出基于证据的决策。现有研究通常将疫情控制和疫情后恢复分开处理，缺乏统一框架来评估两者间的相互作用和权衡。

Method: 构建了一个随机空间SEIR元种群模型，应用于虚构的Jolly岛。将农场分为"Broiler-2"、"有机鸭"和"其他"三类生产系统。模型整合了局部传播、环境传播、移动介导传播和距离依赖性传播，以及响应性和预防性扑杀、生产特异性关养和容量限制补栏策略。

Result: 预防性扑杀将平均累计负担从16,362.7降至13,631.9个感染农场日（降低16.7%）；早期关养显著减少疫情规模，更强的环境传播则提高疫情峰值。模型假设下，2026年5月24日为首个满足0.20复燃概率阈值的安全补栏日期；与基线种群补栏相比，容量限制补栏将累计负担降低8.45%，复燃概率从0.780降至0.533。

Conclusion: 将疫情控制和疫后恢复整合到单一建模框架中具有重要价值。及时关养、针对性预防性扑杀和分阶段容量限制补栏可同时降低疫情负担和复燃风险，但实际运营决策还需结合监测、生物安全、经济因素和法规要求。

Abstract: Highly pathogenic avian influenza (HPAI) outbreaks require rapid control during active transmission and evidence-based decisions on the safe restocking of depopulated farms. We developed a stochastic spatial SEIR-based metapopulation model for a synthetic HPAI outbreak on the fictional Jolly Island. Farms were classified as `Broiler-2', `organic duck', or `Other' production systems. The model incorporated local, environmental, movement-mediated, and distance-dependent transmission, together with reactive and preventive culling, production-specific confinement, and capacity-based restocking. The simulated epidemic was geographically concentrated and differed substantially among production classes. Preventive culling reduced mean cumulative burden from 16,362.7 to 13,631.9 infectious-farm-days, with an overall reduction of 16.7\%. Earlier confinement substantially reduced epidemic magnitude, while stronger environmental transmission increased the epidemic peak. Restocking risk declined as the epidemic approached resolution. Under the model assumptions, 24 May 2026 was the first candidate date satisfying the predefined rebound-probability threshold of 0.20. For restocking on 15 March 2026, none of the tested restocking fractions met this criterion. Capacity-based restocking reduced cumulative burden by 8.45\% and rebound probability from 0.780 to 0.533, compared with restocking relative to the baseline population. These findings demonstrate the value of integrating epidemic control and post-outbreak recovery within a single modelling framework. Timely confinement, targeted preventive culling, and phased capacity-based restocking may reduce both epidemic burden and resurgence risk, although operational decisions should also incorporate surveillance, biosecurity, economic considerations, and regulatory requirements.

</details>


### [134] [Shared environmental risk selects asymmetric inheritance of a protective reserve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13370)
*Quentin Thommen*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文通过最小细胞分裂模型研究环境共享如何改变细胞谱系间保守保护储备的遗传策略，发现充分共享的创新环境会选择不对称分配的离散分支，相变以有限分支跨越而非连续偏离发生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究环境共享如何改变多样化（diversification）的价值，即使谱系所经历的边缘统计量保持不变，环境共享仍可能驱动对称性破缺，从而影响长期细胞的群体增长策略。

Method: 构建母细胞将保护性储备分给两个子细胞的最小模型，每个固定分配策略产生随机人口算子，以最大Lyapunov指数判定长期增长率；引入保护律非线性和储备周转，并用主导模态二阶近似将不对称优势分解为平均算子增长代价和集体波动敏感性降低两部分，与完整随机算子乘积数值对比。

Result: 弱环境共享偏好对称遗传，而充分共享的创新在α≈0.2附近选择不对称分离分支；相变通过有限分支跨越实现。不对称相位在改变保护律与储备周转时仍然存在，但其边界依赖保护非线性与储备记忆。二阶近似可预测有限不对称分支，而完整算子乘积决定数值穿越点。

Conclusion: 环境共享可在保守保护资源遗传中驱动对称性破缺；当谱系间多样化损失提高了分裂时产生的多样化价值时，不对称分配策略成为长期增长的最优解，且该转变具有离散分支跨越的不连续特性。

Abstract: Environmental sharing changes the value of diversification even when the marginal statistics experienced by each lineage remain unchanged. A minimal model of cell division couples this effect to the inheritance of a conserved protective reserve. Each mother partitions its reserve between two daughters, and each fixed partition policy generates a random demographic operator whose top Lyapunov exponent determines long-term growth. Weak environmental sharing favors symmetric inheritance, whereas sufficiently shared innovation selects a separated asymmetric branch near {α\simeq 0.2}. The transition therefore occurs by a finite branch crossing rather than by a continuous departure from equal partition. The asymmetric phase persists when the protection law or reserve turnover is changed, although its boundary depends on protection nonlinearity and reserve memory. Because shared and private environmental innovations have identical marginal statistics, the mean reproductive operator is independent of the shared-innovation fraction. A dominant-mode second-order approximation then separates the asymmetric advantage into a mean-operator growth cost of specialization and a reduction of sensitivity to collective fluctuations. This approximation predicts the finite asymmetric branch selected by the full random-operator dynamics, while the full operator product determines the numerical crossing. Environmental sharing can therefore drive symmetry breaking in the inheritance of a conserved protective resource when the loss of diversification between lineages increases the value of diversification generated at division.

</details>


<div id='stat.AP'></div>

# stat.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Hierarchical Spline-Based Bayesian Beta-Binomial Regression for Estimating Time-Varying Risk in Power Outages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12560)
*Justin Jacobs,Jesse Piburn,Aaron Myers*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层贝叶斯模型，用于从县级停电数据中估计随时间变化的停电风险。模型结合三次B样条基函数与Beta-Binomial似然，捕捉平滑非线性的恢复轨迹并处理观测客户数的过度离散。通过共享的超先验实现跨地理分组的分层收缩，使稀疏或短期事件能从更大群体中借用统计强度。后验推断使用PyMC中的NUTS采样器，可输出潜在停电概率的完整后验分布及衍生韧性指标（如风险曲线下面积AUC）。模型在威斯康星州南部的异构停电事件（取自EAGLE-I平台）上进行了验证，并与朴素梯形AUC估计对比，证明后验均值能恢复确定性积分的点估计结果，同时提供确定性方法无法提供的校准不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力停电风险评估是公用事业和应急管理的关键需求，但现有方法（如确定性梯形积分）只能提供点估计，无法量化不确定性，也难以处理稀疏事件的统计强度不足问题。作者旨在开发一种既能捕捉非线性恢复轨迹、又能处理过度离散且提供校准不确定性量化的统计建模框架。

Method: 采用分层贝叶斯模型：以三次B样条基函数拟合时间变化的停电风险对数几率，配以Beta-Binomial似然处理客户计数数据的过度离散；在Beta-Binomial浓度参数上设置共享超先验，实现地理分组间的分层收缩（借用统计强度）；使用PyMC实现NUTS采样进行后验推断；通过后验预测覆盖率、RMSE和留一交叉验证评估预测性能。

Result: 在威斯康星州南部取自EAGLE-I平台的异构停电事件上进行验证。与朴素梯形AUC估计的直接对比表明：后验均值能够恢复与确定性积分相同的点估计，同时提供确定性方法结构上无法实现的校准不确定性量化。

Conclusion: 该框架为公用事业和应急规划者提供了在不确定性下基准测试恢复动态、比较停电事件的原则性工具，优于确定性方法因其具备完整的不确定性传播能力。

Abstract: We propose a hierarchical Bayesian model for estimating time-varying outage risk from county-level power outage data. The model combines cubic B-spline basis functions with a Beta-Binomial likelihood to capture smooth, nonlinear recovery trajectories while accommodating overdispersion in observed customer counts. A shared hyperprior on the Beta-Binomial concentration parameter enables hierarchical shrinkage across geographically indexed groups, allowing sparse or short-lived events to borrow statistical strength from the broader population. Posterior inference is conducted via the No-U-Turn Sampler (NUTS) in PyMC, yielding full posterior distributions over latent outage probabilities and derived resilience metrics including the area under the risk curve (AUC). We assess predictive performance using posterior predictive coverage, RMSE, and leave-one-out cross-validation, and demonstrate the model across a heterogeneous set of outage events in southern Wisconsin drawn from the EAGLE-I power outage monitoring platform. A direct comparison against naive trapezoidal AUC estimation confirms that the posterior mean recovers the same point estimates as deterministic integration while providing calibrated uncertainty quantification that deterministic approaches structurally cannot. The framework offers utilities and emergency planners a principled tool for benchmarking recovery dynamics and comparing outage events under uncertainty.

</details>


### [136] [Evaluating AlphaEarth Foundations Embeddings for Wildfire Susceptibility Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12663)
*Yuan Zhuang,Sanaa Hobeichi,Peng Shi,Fei Huang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文评估了AlphaEarth Foundations (AEF)地理空间嵌入在野火敏感性制图中的应用价值。以澳大利亚维多利亚州(2017-2025)为案例，发现AEF嵌入能高精度重建常用物理变量，其驱动的敏感性模型ROC-AUC超过0.92，且在气候相似区域的近邻迁移性显著优于传统物理变量模型（维多利亚训练模型迁移至堪培拉时AUC提升约4%，至悉尼蓝山仅下降约2%，而物理变量模型平均下降约25%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统野火敏感性制图依赖从多种遥感、气候和地理空间产品中组装物理变量，需要大量数据协调和任务特定的特征工程，成本高昂。AEF提供的分析就绪型地理空间嵌入有望减少这种依赖性，但其在野火敏感性制图中的价值尚未被系统评估。

Method: 以澳大利亚维多利亚州(2017-2025)为案例，首先检验AEF嵌入对野火敏感性分析常用变量的重建精度；然后使用卫星获取的火灾发生数据训练基于嵌入的敏感性模型，评估分类性能(ROC-AUC)并识别高敏感性区域；最后通过将维多利亚训练的模型迁移至堪培拉和悉尼蓝山地区，评估近邻区域的可迁移性。

Result: AEF嵌入能高精度重建常用野火敏感性变量；嵌入驱动的敏感性模型ROC-AUC超过0.92，能一致识别维多利亚东部（特别是吉普斯兰和东北部高地）的高敏感性区域，并在中部和西北部发现局部热点。在近邻迁移中，维多利亚训练的嵌入模型迁移至堪培拉时ROC-AUC提升约4%，迁移至悉尼蓝山时仅下降约2%，而物理变量模型平均下降约25%。

Conclusion: AEF嵌入能有效支持野火敏感性制图，显著降低对繁重的数据协调和特征工程的需求，并在气候相似区域展现强迁移性。该研究为政府机构和（再）保险公司等下游用户构建可扩展的野火敏感性制图工作流提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Wildfire susceptibility mapping typically relies on physical variables assembled from multiple remote-sensing, climate, and geospatial products. AlphaEarth Foundations (AEF) provides analysis-ready geospatial embeddings that may reduce this dependence on heavy harmonisation and task-specific feature engineering, but their value for wildfire susceptibility mapping has not been systematically evaluated. Using Victoria, Australia (2017-2025), as a case study, we show that AEF embeddings can reconstruct commonly used variables in wildfire susceptibility analysis with high accuracy. In downstream susceptibility models trained on satellite-derived fire occurrence data, embedding-based susceptibility models achieve ROC-AUC values above 0.92 and consistently identify high wildfire susceptibility across eastern Victoria, particularly Gippsland and the north-eastern uplands, with additional localized hotspots in central and northwestern Victoria. A key feature of AEF embeddings is their strong near-region transferability within climatically similar regions. When embedding-based models trained in Victoria are applied to Canberra and Western Sydney-Blue Mountains, ROC-AUC improves by around 4% at Canberra and declines by around 2% at Western Sydney-Blue Mountains, compared with a mean decrease of approximately 25% for physical-variable models. These findings provide practical guidance for using AEF embeddings and lay a foundation for scalable wildfire susceptibility mapping workflows for downstream users such as government agencies and (re)insurers.

</details>


### [137] [When Method Choice Changes Statistical Inference: A Comparison of a Baseline Two-Stage Approach and Bayesian Joint Modeling for Longitudinal and Survival Data in an HIV Clinical Trial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12682)
*Alberta A. Johnson*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本研究基于一项包含467名患者的HIV临床试验数据，比较了两阶段法与贝叶斯联合模型在纵向生物标志物（CD4）与生存结局（全因死亡）分析中的表现。两种方法在ddI与死亡风险的关联估计上方向一致、幅度相近（两阶段法HR=1.342，联合模型HR=1.383），但联合模型估计的CD4保护效应更强（HR=0.776 vs 0.826），表明纵向生物标志信息的处理方式会实质影响统计推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向生物标志物数据与生存结局的联合分析在临床研究中具有重要意义，而两阶段法与贝叶斯联合模型是两种常用方法。然而，以往比较两者差异时往往将其归因于测量误差或信息性失访，缺乏基于实际临床试验数据的系统性对比。本文旨在通过真实HIV临床试验数据，检验两种方法在实际应用中的差异及其潜在来源。

Method: 本文利用一项包含467名HIV患者的临床试验数据（重复测量CD4指标，以全因死亡为生存结局），对比了两种分析方法：（1）两阶段法：先拟合线性混合效应模型，将患者预测的基线CD4值作为固定协变量纳入Cox比例风险模型；（2）贝叶斯联合模型：同时建模纵向CD4过程和生存过程，将死亡风险与当前潜在CD4值相关联。

Result: 两种方法估计的ddI与死亡风险关联方向一致、幅度相近：两阶段法HR=1.342（95% CI: 1.006-1.789），联合模型HR=1.383（95% CrI: 0.952-2.010）。CD4的保护效应在联合模型下更强（HR=0.776），而两阶段法估计较弱（HR=0.826）。由于两方法使用了纵向CD4过程的不同汇总方式，观察到的差异不能仅归因于测量误差或信息性失访。

Conclusion: 研究发现纵向生物标志物信息的处理方式对统计推断有实质性影响。两阶段法与贝叶斯联合模型在关联方向和幅度上基本一致，但在效应大小估计上存在差异，且这种差异源于方法本身对纵向数据的利用方式不同，而非简单的测量误差或失访机制所致。这一发现强调在纵向-生存联合分析中应谨慎选择分析方法。

Abstract: The two-stage approach and Bayesian joint modeling are commonly used to analyze longitudinal biomarker measurements together with time-to-event outcomes. Using data from an HIV clinical trial of 467 patients with repeated CD4 measurements and all-cause mortality as the survival outcome, we compared a baseline two-stage approach with a Bayesian joint model. The two-stage analysis fitted a linear mixed-effects model and included each patient's predicted baseline CD4 value as a fixed covariate in a Cox proportional hazards model. The joint model simultaneously modeled the longitudinal CD4 process and survival while linking mortality risk to the current underlying CD4 value. The estimated association between ddI and mortality was similar in direction and magnitude across the two approaches. The two-stage estimate was HR = 1.342 (95% CI: 1.006-1.789), whereas the joint-model estimate was HR = 1.383 (95% CrI: 0.952-2.010). The estimated protective association of CD4 was stronger under the joint model (HR = 0.776) than under the two-stage approach (HR = 0.826). Because the approaches used different summaries of the longitudinal CD4 process, the observed differences cannot be attributed solely to measurement error or informative dropout. The findings demonstrate that the treatment of longitudinal biomarker information can materially affect statistical inference.

</details>


### [138] [Spatial similarity in socioeconomic data: a wavelet approach for England](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13196)
*Duncan Cook,John AD Aston*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于离散小波变换的多分辨率表示方法，用于比较区域内部空间结构的相似性，并应用于英格兰2025年多重剥夺指数（IMD）数据，发现具有相似内部结构的区域往往跨越地区、城市化和平均剥夺水平等传统分类，挑战了这些类别作为地方地理代理指标的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英格兰的社会经济指标呈现复杂的空间格局，而基于平均值或宽泛地理分类的标准方法无法充分捕捉这种复杂性。因此需要一种能够基于区域内部空间结构进行比较的新方法。

Method: 该方法利用离散小波变换（DWT）生成多分辨率表示：将区域数据嵌入规则网格，提取局部窗口，将每个窗口表示为尺度特异性和方向特异性的细节系数集合；在这些系数上定义相异性度量，并在旋转和反射变换下进行最小化，以识别具有相似空间结构的连续统计单元集合。

Result: 将该方法应用于英格兰2025年IMD的LSOA层级数据，发现具有相似内部结构的区域往往跨越不同地区、不同城市化水平以及不同平均剥夺水平，表明传统分类无法有效代理局部地理特征。

Conclusion: 该方法为基于区域内剥夺分布方式识别可比地点提供了框架，对政策评估和政策可迁移性具有重要启示。

Abstract: Socioeconomic indicators in England exhibit complex spatial patterns that are not well captured by standard approaches based on averages or broad geographic classifications. We propose a method for comparing areas based on their internal spatial structure, using a multiresolution representation derived from the discrete wavelet transform. The method embeds areal data into a regular grid, extracts local windows, and represents each as a set of scale- and direction-specific detail coefficients. A dissimilarity measure, defined over these coefficients and minimised over rotations and reflections, is used to identify contiguous sets of statistical units with similar spatial structure. We apply the approach to England's 2025 Index of Multiple Deprivation at the lower layer super output area level. We show that areas with similar internal structure are often found across regions, levels of urbanisation, and average deprivation, challenging the use of these categories as proxies for local geography. The results provide a framework for identifying comparable places based on how deprivation is distributed within them, with implications for policy evaluation and transferability.

</details>


### [139] [Physics-informed distribution of relaxation times estimation and latent-space condition monitoring of solid oxide fuel and electrolysis cells from electrochemical impedance spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13305)
*Žan Gorenc,Žiga Gradišar,Felix Mütter,Vanja Subotić,Pavle Boškoski*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于物理信息的卷积自编码器，直接从电化学阻抗谱（EIS）中估计弛豫时间分布（DRT），无需针对光谱进行调参。通过将离散化阻抗-DRT关系嵌入训练过程，网络能生成阻抗一致的分布，在合成两ZARC光谱和三种独立固体氧化物电池数据集中均表现出高精度（归一化误差<1.1%），且潜空间具备可解释性，可捕获运行状态变化、缺氢事件和长期退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从EIS估计DRT是一个不适定（ill-posed）逆问题，对正则化选择高度敏感。传统方法需要针对不同光谱进行参数调优，缺乏通用性和鲁棒性，限制了DRT在电池状态监测中的实际应用。

Method: 提出物理信息卷积自编码器（physics-informed convolutional autoencoder）：将阻抗与DRT之间的离散化关系嵌入训练过程作为物理约束，使网络输出满足阻抗一致性；采用轻量级统一架构，无需针对数据集修改；通过解码器探测（decoder-probe）分析潜空间组织方式。

Result: 在合成两ZARC光谱中成功解析重叠弛豫过程；在三种独立固体氧化物燃料电池/电解池数据集中重构误差低于1.1%；潜空间组织与弛豫时间尺度相关，潜空间距离能捕捉运行变化、缺氢事件及长期退化。

Conclusion: 该轻量级统一架构可在无需修改的情况下应用于多种数据集，提供一致的DRT估计，并可作为可解释的条件监测基础，兼具准确性与物理一致性。

Abstract: Estimating the distribution of relaxation times (DRT) fromelectrochemical impedance spectroscopy (EIS) is an ill-posed inverse problem that is highly sensitive to regularisation choices. We propose a physics-informed convolutional autoencoder that estimates DRT directly from EIS data without spectrum-specific tuning. A discretised relation between impedance and the DRT is embedded in the training process, constraining the network to produce impedance-consistent distributions. The model resolves overlapping relaxation processes in synthetic two-ZARC spectra and accurately reconstructs measurements from three independent solid oxide fuel and electrolysis cell datasets, with range-normalised errors below 1.1%. Decoder-probe analysis shows that the learned latent representation is organised according to relaxation timescale. Distances in this latent space capture operating changes, hydrogen-shortage events, and long-term degradation. The same lightweight architecture is applied across all datasets without modification, providing consistent DRT estimation and an interpretable basis for condition monitoring.

</details>


### [140] [A Metric Space of Spatial Graphs: Two-Sample Testing, Data Depth, and Application to Cardiac Fibrosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13406)
*Anna Calissano,Arstanbek Okenov,Katja Zeppenfeld,Alexander Panfilov*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于空间图（spatial graphs）和旋转不变的Fused Gromov Wasserstein度量的框架，用于分析心脏纤维化斑块的几何与拓扑结构，并通过深度度量、统计检验及DepthPlot可视化工具，从组织病理图像中提取具有临床意义的纤维化纹理特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏纤维化会降低电传导能力并导致心律失常，而心律失常波的旋转通常围绕非导电的纤维化斑块进行，因此斑块的几何与拓扑结构对心律失常动力学至关重要。然而，尽管具有重要临床相关性，这些结构仍缺乏系统性的量化理解。本文旨在解决这一开放问题。

Method: 该方法首先利用骨架化（skeletonization）将每个纤维化斑块表示为空间图（节点嵌入欧氏空间、边编码组织几何属性）。核心贡献是引入空间图空间（spatial graph space），这是一个配备旋转不变Fused Gromov Wasserstein度量的度量空间，支持比较节点和边数不同的空间图。在此基础上，进行分布级统计检验和空间图的深度度量，并提出DepthPlot可视化工具用于度量空间中的深度度量展示。方法应用于患者间比较及心室内的斑块空间位置比较。

Result: 研究发现纤维化纹理表现出强烈的患者特异性特征，但部分心脏显示出显著的几何相似性，可能反映共享的病理突变或其他未知因素。通过定量深度度量，研究以中心和外周空间图刻画检验结果，证明该框架能为纤维化纹理表征提供统计学和临床上均有意义的见解。

Conclusion: 该研究为心脏纤维化斑块的结构分析提供了一个全新的数学框架，结合空间图度量、统计检验和可视化工具，能够有效揭示不同患者间纤维化纹理的异同，为理解心律失常的病理机制和潜在治疗靶点提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: Cardiac fibrosis reduces electrical conductivity and is a leading cause of arrhythmia. Arrhythmic waves typically rotate around non-conducting fibrotic patches, so the geometry and topology of these patches (spatially isolated regions of fibrotic tissue within the heart muscle) play an important role in arrhythmia dynamics. Despite their clinical relevance, these structures remain poorly understood. We address this open problem using histopathological images of human hearts affected by cardiac fibrosis. Each patch is represented as a spatial graph via skeletonization, where nodes are embedded as points in Euclidean space and edges encode geometric properties of the underlying tissue. The core methodological contribution of this work is the introduction of spatial graph space, a metric space equipped with a rotation-invariant Fused Gromov Wasserstein metric that enables comparison of spatial graphs with differing numbers of nodes and edges. Building on this, we perform a distribution-level statistical testing and depth measures for spatial graphs. To enable the interpretation of the spatial graph sample distribution, we introduce DepthPlot, a novel visualization tool for depth measures in metric spaces. Applying our methodology to compare patients and the spatial position of patches within the ventricles, we find that fibrotic textures exhibit strong patient-specific features, while some hearts display notable geometric similarities, potentially reflecting shared pathological mutations or other unknown factors. Through quantitative depth measures, we characterize test outcomes via central and peripheral spatial graphs, demonstrating that the proposed framework yields statistically and clinically meaningful insights into fibrotic texture characterization.

</details>
